Additionally, FreeAnimate introduces Inversion-Boosted Attention and Reference-Anchored Self-Attention modules to guarantee temporal consistency and identity preservation. Experimental results demonstrate that FreeAnimate outperforms existing training-free competitors and training-based baseline methods, achieving generation quality comparable to state-of-the-art methods and offering robust generalization across diverse datasets. Our project page is at https://freeani.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 人类图像动画领域取得了显著进展，主要归功于扩散模型的发展。然而，现有方法通常需要大量的训练数据和计算资源才能获得高质量结果，这限制了其泛化能力和可访问性。在这项工作中，我们提出了FreeAnimate，一个无需训练的框架，利用图像扩散模型的内在能力来实现时间一致性、身份保留和背景稳定性。我们的方法引入了一种新颖的预览生成策略，从生成的预览帧中提供时间和结构先验，有效引导姿态对齐和背景一致性，而无需训练。此外，FreeAnimate引入了反转增强注意力模块和参考锚定自注意力模块，以保证时间一致性和身份保留。实验结果表明，FreeAnimate优于现有的无训练竞争方法和基于训练的基线方法，生成的图像质量与最先进方法相当，并在多个不同数据集上展现出强大的泛化能力。我们的项目页面位于https://freeani.github.io/。

</details>


### [31] [ARAPDiffusion: ARAP Regularization for Diffusion-Based Deformable Shape Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06887)
*Haibo Liu,Jinghan Ke,Haitao Yang,Xiangru Huang,Georgios Pavlakos,Qixing Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ARAPDiffusion，一种将ARAP变形模型作为正则化损失注入潜在扩散模型的形状生成方法，无需大量3D训练数据即可学习变形形状集合的连续形状空间。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型需要大量3D训练数据，而真实3D形状数据获取成本高、样本有限，难以训练有效的生成模型。作者希望利用ARAP变形模型的几何先验来弥补数据不足的问题。

Method: 将ARAP变形模型作为正则化损失注入潜在扩散模型中，通过交替训练策略：利用LD模型合成的分布增强形状编码器/解码器，再利用形状解码器产生的正则化损失改进LD模型。采用无表示LD过程与隐式形状解码器，可适用于无组织点云。

Result: 在无条件和条件形状生成任务上的实验结果表明，ARAPDiffusion优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ARAPDiffusion通过注入ARAP变形先验，有效降低了对大量3D训练数据的依赖，同时提升了形状生成质量，验证了将物理变形模型与潜在扩散模型结合的可行性和优势。

Original Abstract: This paper introduces ARAPDiffusion, a latent diffusion model to learn the underlying continuous shape space of a deformation shape collection. The key innovation is in injecting the as-rigid-as-possible (ARAP) deformation model as regularization losses into latent diffusion (LD), releasing the requirement of having abundant 3D training data for learning generative models. In contrast to the standard LD, we show how the ARAP model can be used to improve both the encoder/decoder and the LD model. The training procedure alternates between using the synthetic distribution defined by the LD model to develop a regularization loss that enhances the shape encoder/decoder and using the shape decoder to develop a regularization loss to improve the LD model. We also show the benefit of the LD paradigm in combining a representation-free LD process and an implicit shape decoder that is applicable to unorganized point clouds. The experimental results of unconditional and conditional shape generation demonstrate the advantages of ARAPDiffusion over baseline approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出ARAPDiffusion，一种潜在扩散模型，用于学习变形形状集合的底层连续形状空间。其关键创新在于将尽可能刚体（ARAP）变形模型作为正则化损失注入潜在扩散（LD）中，从而释放了学习生成模型所需的大规模3D训练数据的要求。与标准LD相比，我们展示了如何利用ARAP模型同时改进编码器/解码器和LD模型。训练过程交替进行：利用LD模型定义的合成分布来开发正则化损失以增强形状编码器/解码器，以及利用形状解码器来开发正则化损失以改进LD模型。我们还展示了LD范式在结合无表示LD过程和适用于无组织点云的隐式形状解码器方面的优势。无条件和条件形状生成的实验结果证明了ARAPDiffusion相较于基线方法的优越性。

</details>


### [32] [Diagnosing Visual Ignorance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06890)
*Runyu Zhou,Qi Zhang,Qixun Wang,Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从机制和行为两个层面系统研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）中的语言先验依赖问题。内部机制上，通过反事实层替换和监督式逐层MLP探针，揭示了视觉语义与语言先验语义在语言解码器中的多阶段竞争瓶颈；外部行为上，提出基于多步高斯模糊的渐进视觉退化指标，发现大量样本在视觉内容严重破坏后仍可被正确回答，表明现有基准无意中奖励了对视觉信息的忽视。研究指出语言先验依赖是系统性的路由失败，并提出需基于结构隔离或反事实数据来设计训练和评估协议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）经常依赖语言先验产生自信但视觉证据薄弱的答案，然而这种行为的内在机制及其对基准评估的影响尚未被充分理解。

Method: 1) 内部机制：结合反事实层替换与监督式逐层MLP探针，追踪真实视觉语义和语言先验语义在语言解码器各层的竞争过程。2) 外部行为：提出基于多步高斯模糊的渐进视觉退化指标，识别在视觉内容逐渐被破坏时答案仍保持不变的样本。

Result: 1) 发现多阶段瓶颈：中间层未能有效检索视觉信息，后层进一步抑制残留视觉信号而偏向文本空间偏差。2) 在12个VQA基准和3个代表性VLM上，大量样本在严重甚至完全视觉模糊下仍可被回答，表明现有基准可能无意中奖励了视觉无知。

Conclusion: 语言先验依赖是系统性的路由失败，既影响模型内部机制也损害基准有效性。未来研究需基于结构隔离或反事实数据设计训练分布和评估协议，以强制实现真正的跨模态对齐。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) frequently rely on language priors, producing confident answers that are weakly grounded in visual evidence. While this behavior is widely observed, its internal mechanisms and its impact on benchmark evaluation remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we study language-prior reliance from both mechanistic and behavioral perspectives. Internally, we combine counterfactual layer replacement with supervised layer-wise MLP probing to trace how ground-truth visual semantics and language-prior semantics compete across the language decoder. Our analysis reveals a multi-stage bottleneck: intermediate layers often fail to effectively retrieve visual information, while later layers can further suppress surviving visual signals in favor of text-space biases. Externally, we introduce a progressive visual decay metric based on multi-step Gaussian blurring, which identifies instances whose answers remain invariant even as visual content is increasingly destroyed. Across twelve visual question-answering benchmarks and three representative VLMs, we find that a substantial fraction of examples remain answerable under severe or total visual obfuscation, indicating that current benchmarks can inadvertently reward visual ignorance. These findings demonstrate that language-prior reliance is a systematic routing failure affecting both model internals and benchmark validity. Finally, we outline critical pathways for future research, highlighting the necessity of designing training distributions and evaluation protocols built on structurally isolated or counterfactual data to enforce genuine cross-modal grounding.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）经常依赖语言先验，生成自信但视觉证据薄弱的答案。尽管这种行为已被广泛观察，但其内在机制及对基准评估的影响仍未得到充分理解。在本工作中，我们从机制和行为两个角度研究语言先验依赖。在内部机制上，我们结合反事实层替换与监督式逐层MLP探针，追踪真实视觉语义和语言先验语义在语言解码器中的竞争过程。分析揭示了多阶段瓶颈：中间层通常无法有效检索视觉信息，而后层则进一步抑制残存的视觉信号，偏向文本空间偏差。在外部行为上，我们提出基于多步高斯模糊的渐进视觉退化指标，用于识别那些在视觉内容不断被破坏时答案仍保持不变的样本。在12个视觉问答基准和三个代表性VLM上，我们发现大量样本在严重甚至完全视觉模糊下仍可被回答，表明当前基准可能无意中奖励了对视觉信息的忽视。这些发现表明，语言先验依赖是一种系统性的路由失败，既影响模型内部机制，也损害基准的有效性。最后，我们概述了未来研究的关键方向，强调需要基于结构隔离或反事实数据来设计训练分布和评估协议，以强制实现真正的跨模态对齐。

</details>


### [33] [Stream3D-VLM: Online 3D Spatial Understanding with Incremental Geometry Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06891)
*Hanxun Yu,Xuan Qu,Lei Ke,Boqiang Zhang,Yuxin Wang,Jianke Zhu,Dong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Stream3D-VLM，一个在线3D视觉语言模型，能够从流式视频中实时理解3D空间，采用自回归流式控制、轻量级视觉-空间特征融合模块和几何自适应体素压缩模块，并通过大规模数据生成流水线构建了超过100万在线时空3D问答对，在29项任务上显著优于现有模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D大语言多模态模型仅在离线场景下工作，需要完整的场景观测或预定义的视频片段，缺乏从流式视频中进行实时3D空间理解的能力。

Method: (1) 基于LLM的next-token预测目标，采用自回归流式控制建模来自主决定何时响应；(2) 设计轻量级视觉-空间特征融合模块，逐步注入时间对齐的几何先验；(3) 提出即插即用的几何自适应体素压缩模块，高效压缩视觉token以缓解长上下文解码开销；(4) 开发可扩展的数据生成流水线，构建超过100万在线时空3D问答对和29项任务的基准。

Result: 在在线和离线3D空间理解、推理和定位任务上，该方法显著优于闭源和开源模型，并在29项任务的全面基准上取得了最优结果。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过流式控制建模、渐进式几何特征注入和高效token压缩，可以实现从流式视频中实时理解3D空间，为在线3D视觉语言模型的研究提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Despite advances in 3D scene understanding, existing 3D Large Multimodal Models operate in offline settings, requiring complete scene observations or predefined video clips. In this paper, we present an online 3D vision-language model that enables real-time spatial understanding from streaming video. Our approach adopts an autoregressive streaming control modeling based on the LLM's next-token prediction objective to learn when to respond, and employs a lightweight Visual-Spatial Feature Integration (VSFI) module to incrementally inject temporally aligned geometry priors into the visual stream. To alleviate long-context decoding overhead, we propose a plug-and-play Geometry-Adaptive Voxel Compression (GAVC) module for efficient visual token compression. To address the scarcity of streaming 3D-language data, we further develop a scalable data generation pipeline that curates over 1M online spatio-temporal 3D QA pairs and establishes a comprehensive benchmark spanning 29 tasks. Extensive experiments show that our approach significantly outperforms both proprietary and open-source models across online and offline 3D spatial understanding, reasoning, and grounding tasks. The project page is available at https://stream3d-vlm.github.io/

中文摘要翻译: 尽管3D场景理解取得了进展，现有的3D大语言多模态模型仅在离线场景下运行，需要完整的场景观测或预定义的视频片段。本文提出了一个在线3D视觉语言模型，能够从流式视频中实现实时的空间理解。我们的方法采用基于大语言模型下一个词元预测目标的自回归流式控制建模，学习何时做出响应，并使用轻量级视觉-空间特征融合模块逐步向视觉流中注入时间对齐的几何先验。为缓解长上下文解码开销，我们提出了即插即用的几何自适应体素压缩模块，用于高效的视觉词元压缩。为解决流式3D语言数据稀缺的问题，我们进一步开发了一个可扩展的数据生成流水线，整理出超过100万个在线时空3D问答对，并建立了一个涵盖29项任务的综合基准。大量实验表明，在在线和离线的3D空间理解、推理和定位任务中，我们的方法显著优于闭源和开源模型。项目页面：https://stream3d-vlm.github.io/

</details>


### [34] [Lighting-Aware Representation Learning under Controllable Lighting Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06899)
*Lizhen Zhu,Charantej Reddy Pochimireddy,James Z Wang,Brad Wyble*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个光照感知表示学习框架，通过将光照变化作为显式训练信号（而非需要抑制的干扰因素）引入对比学习，联合学习语义一致性表示和光照依赖的视觉结构，在图像分类和检测任务上优于标准对比学习基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法通常通过数据增强使模型对光照变化保持不变性，但未显式建模光照信息；受人类视觉理论启发，提出将光照变化作为显式训练信号而非干扰因素来利用。

Method: 扩展对比学习框架，引入辅助目标函数捕获渲染场景中光照依赖的变化，使模型在保持语义一致性的同时，对光照依赖的视觉结构保持敏感。

Result: 在ImageNet、ExDark、PASCAL VOC基准的图像分类和目标检测任务上，光照感知训练在相同架构和训练预算下一致优于标准对比学习基线；在监督学习框架和简单光照变化场景下也表现良好。

Conclusion: 该方法能提升模型在复杂视觉环境中的鲁棒性和适应性，且适用于更常规的图像处理任务，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Variations in illumination remain a major challenge for visual representation learning, as they induce substantial appearance changes both across and within environments. While existing approaches typically address this issue through data augmentations that encourage models to become invariant to lighting changes, such strategies do not explicitly model lighting information during learning. Inspired by theories of human vision, we propose a lighting-aware representation learning framework that incorporates illumination variation as an explicit training signal rather than a nuisance factor to be suppressed. Our method extends contrastive learning by introducing an auxiliary objective that captures illumination-dependent variation in rendered scenes, enabling the model to jointly learn representations that preserve semantic consistency while remaining sensitive to lighting-dependent visual structure. We evaluate the proposed model on image classification and object detection tasks across the ImageNet, ExDark, and PASCAL VOC benchmarks. Results demonstrate that the proposed lighting-aware training consistently improves downstream performance over standard contrastive learning baselines, while maintaining the same architecture and training budget. Furthermore, our approach shows promising performance in supervised learning frameworks and under settings involving simpler lighting variation, suggesting broad applicability beyond complex illumination scenarios. These results indicate its potential to enhance model robustness and adaptability in complex visual environments as well as in more conventional image processing tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 光照变化仍是视觉表示学习面临的主要挑战，因为它会在不同环境及同一环境内部引发显著的外观变化。现有方法通常通过数据增强使模型对光照变化保持不变性来解决此问题，但这类策略在学习过程中并未显式建模光照信息。受人类视觉理论的启发，我们提出了一种光照感知表示学习框架，该框架将光照变化作为显式训练信号，而非需要抑制的干扰因素。我们的方法扩展了对比学习，引入了一个辅助目标函数来捕获渲染场景中光照依赖的变化，使模型能够在保持语义一致性的同时，对光照依赖的视觉结构保持敏感。我们在ImageNet、ExDark和PASCAL VOC基准上对该模型进行了图像分类和目标检测任务的评估。结果表明，所提出的光照感知训练在相同架构和训练预算下，一致优于标准对比学习基线的下游性能。此外，我们的方法在监督学习框架以及涉及更简单光照变化的场景中也展现了良好的性能，表明其具有超越复杂光照场景的广泛适用性。这些结果表明，该方法在复杂视觉环境以及更常规的图像处理任务中，都具有提升模型鲁棒性和适应性的潜力。

</details>


### [35] [LUCID: Learning Unified Control for Image Deflaring and Exposure Mastery in Nighttime Photography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06901)
*Tingyu Yang,Yuan Cheng,Xiaoyun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUCID统一了夜间图像复原中眩光与噪声的纠缠退化，通过眩光解耦模块和扩散驱动模块实现连续可控的夜间修复，支持选择性光源控制、伪影去除及HDR重建，在真实场景中优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法孤立处理夜间场景中的眩光与噪声退化，忽视了二者在物理上的纠缠特性，缺乏统一且可控的复原框架。

Method: 提出LUCID统一框架：1）眩光解耦模块去除光学伪影以提供结构引导；2）扩散驱动模块利用生成先验重建清晰图像；3）四模式训练策略引入显式可控性，通过无分类器引导实现光源及眩光/鬼影的选择性控制，并支持连续曝光控制的HDR重建。

Result: 在多种真实夜间场景上，LUCID一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将夜间复原重构为连续可控过程而非固定矫正，通过解耦与生成先验的协作融合，实现了对退化因素的有效控制与高质量重建。

Original Abstract: Photography is the art of painting with light, yet nighttime scenes are shaped by competing degradations: intense flares obscure scene structure, while photon-limited regions collapse into noise. Conventional approaches address these factors in isolation, overlooking the fact that these degradations are fundamentally entangled. To bridge this gap, we introduce LUCID, a unified framework that reframes nighttime restoration as a continuous and controllable process rather than a fixed correction. We decompose nighttime restoration into two cooperative components: a flare disentanglement module that lifts the 'curtain' of optical artifacts to provide reliable structural guidance, and a diffusion-driven module that leverages generative priors to reconstruct clean and well-exposed imagery. Crucially, LUCID introduces explicit controllability through a novel four-mode training strategy, enabling users to steer the restoration process via classifier-free guidance (CFG) and allowing selective control over light sources and their associated flare and ghosting artifacts, while also supporting high dynamic range (HDR) reconstruction through continuous exposure control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LUCID consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse real-world nighttime scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 摄影是用光作画的艺术，然而夜间场景受到相互竞争的退化因素影响：强烈的眩光遮挡了场景结构，而光子受限区域则坍缩为噪声。传统方法孤立地处理这些因素，忽视了这些退化在本质上是相互纠缠的。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了LUCID——一个统一框架，将夜间复原重新定义为连续且可控的过程，而非固定的矫正。我们将夜间分解为两个协同组件：一个眩光解耦模块，用于掀开光学伪影的"帷幕"以提供可靠的结构引导；以及一个扩散驱动模块，利用生成先验重建清晰且曝光良好的图像。关键在于，LUCID通过一种新颖的四模式训练策略引入了显式可控性，使用户能够通过无分类器引导（CFG）来调控复原过程，实现对光源及其相关眩光和鬼影伪影的选择性控制，同时通过连续曝光控制支持高动态范围（HDR）重建。大量实验表明，LUCID在多种真实世界夜间场景中持续优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [36] [Beyond Skeletons: Learning Animation Directly from Driving Videos with Same2X Training Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06903)
*Yuan Zeng,Yujia Shi,Yuhao Yang,Dongxia Liu,Zongqing Lu,Wenming Yang,Qingmin Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DirectAnimator是一个无需姿态提取即可直接学习驱动视频的人像动画框架，通过引入驱动线索三元组（姿态、面部、位置）和CueFusion DiT模块实现鲁棒控制，并采用Same2X训练策略处理跨身份对齐问题，在更少计算资源下达到SOTA视觉质量和身份保持效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖姿态估计器提取中间表示，但这类信号在遮挡或复杂姿态下容易出错，因此作者希望提出一种绕过姿态提取、直接从原始驱动视频学习的框架，以提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出DirectAnimator框架，核心包括：(1) 驱动线索三元组（Driving Cue Triplet），包含姿态线索、面部线索和位置线索，以语义丰富且稳定的形式捕获运动、表达和对齐信息；(2) CueFusion DiT模块，用于融合这些线索以实现可靠的去噪控制；(3) Same2X训练策略，在驱动与参考身份不同时，通过将跨ID特征与同ID数据学习到的特征对齐来正则化优化并加速收敛。

Result: 大量实验表明DirectAnimator在视觉质量和身份保持方面达到SOTA，对遮挡和复杂关节运动具有鲁棒性，且计算资源需求更少。

Conclusion: DirectAnimator通过去除姿态提取依赖，提出高效的线索融合机制和跨ID训练策略，在降低计算开销的同时显著提升了人像动画的鲁棒性和视觉效果。

Original Abstract: Human image animation aims to generate a video from a static reference image, guided by pose information extracted from a driving video. Existing approaches often rely on pose estimators to extract intermediate representations, but such signals are prone to errors under occlusion or complex poses. Building on these observations, we present DirectAnimator, a framework that bypasses pose extraction and directly learns from raw driving videos. We introduce a Driving Cue Triplet consisting of pose, face, and location cues that captures motion, expression, and alignment in a semantically rich yet stable form, and we fuse them through a CueFusion DiT block for reliable control during denoising. To make learning dependable when the driving and reference identities differ, we devise a Same2X training strategy that aligns cross-ID features with those learned from same-ID data, regularizing optimization and accelerating convergence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DirectAnimator attains state-of-the-art visual quality and identity preservation while remaining robust to occlusions and complex articulation, and it does so with fewer computational resources. Our project page is at https://directanimator.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 人像动画旨在根据驱动视频中提取的姿态信息，从静态参考图像生成视频。现有方法通常依赖姿态估计器来提取中间表示，但这种信号在遮挡或复杂姿态下容易出错。基于这些观察，我们提出了DirectAnimator——一个绕过姿态提取、直接从原始驱动视频学习的框架。我们引入了一个驱动线索三元组，包含姿态、面部和位置线索，以语义丰富且稳定的形式捕获运动、表情和对齐信息，并通过CueFusion DiT模块将它们融合，以实现可靠的去噪控制。为使学习在驱动与参考身份不同时仍然可靠，我们设计了Same2X训练策略，将跨ID特征与同ID数据中学习到的特征对齐，从而正则化优化并加速收敛。大量实验表明，DirectAnimator在视觉质量和身份保持方面达到了最先进水平，同时对遮挡和复杂关节运动具有鲁棒性，并且计算资源需求更少。项目页面：https://directanimator.github.io/。

</details>


### [37] [polyDAG: Polynomial Acyclicity Constraints for Efficient Continuous Causal Discovery in Visual Semantic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06908)
*Wenhao Zhang,Ramin Ramezani,Tao Han,Kai Hwang,Minyi Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: polyDAG提出了一种基于多项式迹约束的连续有向无环图学习框架，用有限多项式替代矩阵指数约束来保证无环性，大幅提升了视觉语义图因果发现的效率与结构恢复性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有连续有向无环图学习在视觉语义图构建中受限于矩阵指数无环约束的高计算成本，亟需更高效的约束形式来加速因果发现过程。

Method: 提出polyDAG框架，用有限多项式迹约束替代矩阵指数无环约束，证明多项式约束在无环图上严格为零；进一步推导出几何级数实现方法，避免显式求和循环，同时保持相同的无环性条件。

Result: 在合成Erdos-Renyi图和CelebA面部视觉属性数据集上，polyDAG平均结构汉明距离从318.4降至285.4，平均F1分数从0.725提升至0.756；在100节点上几何变体运行时间3.44秒，相比指数基线的5.16秒实现33.4%加速。

Conclusion: polyDAG通过多项式约束有效降低了连续因果发现的计算开销，在视觉语义图学习中兼顾效率与准确性，为大规模结构化语义变量建模提供了可扩展方案。

Original Abstract: Modern image-analysis pipelines often convert images into structured semantic variables, such as facial attributes, object concepts, and scene descriptors. Learning directed dependencies among these variables can produce interpretable visual semantic graphs, but continuous directed acyclic graph learning is limited by the cost of enforcing acyclicity. We present polyDAG, a polynomial acyclicity framework for efficient continuous causal discovery in visual semantic graphs. polyDAG replaces the matrix-exponential acyclicity constraint with a finite polynomial trace constraint and proves that the new constraint is zero exactly for acyclic graphs. We further derive a geometric-series implementation that avoids the explicit summation loop while preserving the same acyclicity condition. Experiments on synthetic Erdos-Renyi graphs and CelebA facial visual attributes show that polyDAG improves efficiency and structure recovery. Averaged over the revised synthetic protocol with d in {100, 200, 500}, polyDAG reduces mean structural Hamming distance from 318.4 to 285.4 and improves mean F1 score from 0.725 to 0.756. At 100 nodes, the geometric variant runs in 3.44 seconds compared with 5.16 seconds for the exponential baseline, corresponding to a 33.4 percent speedup. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/wenhaoz-fengcai/polyDAG.

中文摘要翻译: 现代图像分析流程通常将图像转换为结构化的语义变量，如面部属性、物体概念和场景描述符。学习这些变量之间的有向依赖关系可以生成可解释的视觉语义图，但连续有向无环图学习受限于施加无环性的计算成本。本文提出polyDAG，一个用于视觉语义图中高效连续因果发现的多项式无环性框架。polyDAG将矩阵指数无环约束替换为有限多项式迹约束，并证明新约束在无环图上恰好为零。我们进一步推导了一种几何级数实现，避免了显式求和循环，同时保持相同的无环性条件。在合成Erdos-Renyi图和CelebA面部视觉属性上的实验表明，polyDAG提升了效率和结构恢复能力。在d∈{100, 200, 500}的修正合成协议上，polyDAG平均结构汉明距离从318.4降至285.4，平均F1分数从0.725提升至0.756。在100个节点上，几何变体运行时间为3.44秒，而指数基线为5.16秒，对应33.4%的加速。代码与数据公开于https://github.com/wenhaoz-fengcai/polyDAG。

</details>


### [38] [DRIFT: From Robustness Gaps to Invariance Manifolds for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06918)
*Abhishek Ameta,Sayan Banerjee,Shreyas Pandith,Harshit,Ankita Chatterjee,Akshay Janardan Bankar,Amit Satish Unde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于冻结视觉基础模型(VFM)的AI生成图像检测方法，通过轻量投影头将表示空间分解为鲁棒子空间和脆弱子空间，学习真实图像的结构化不变性流形，实现开放世界下的跨生成器泛化检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无训练方法依赖VFM固定的不变性几何结构，缺乏对检测任务的自适应学习，导致开放世界下对未见生成器的泛化能力不足。

Method: 在冻结VFM基础上引入轻量投影头，将表示空间分解为互补的鲁棒子空间(抑制物理成像变换扰动，逼近真实图像流形切向方向)和脆弱子空间(保持对编辑类扰动的敏感性)；通过结构化排序间隔强制物理不变性与编辑诱导变异性之间的层次分离；检测阶段以多尺度分块漂移量作为双通道不变性签名，通过间隔违反测试判定真伪。

Result: 在未见生成器和分辨率上展现出强大的开放世界泛化能力，一致超越基于无训练鲁棒性的基线方法，并能提供可解释的不变性违规定位图。

Conclusion: 将AI生成图像检测转化为学习真实图像的流形结构，通过有监督的子空间分解和层次化间隔约束，能够有效提升检测器在开放场景下的泛化性能和可解释性。

Original Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative image models challenges existing AI-generated image detectors, particularly in open-world settings with unseen generators. Recent training-free approaches measure robustness gaps in frozen vision foundation models (VFMs), detecting fakes via perturbation-induced embedding drift. However, these methods rely on fixed invariance geometry inherited from pretraining and lack principled adaptation to the detection task. We instead formulate AI-generated image detection as learning a structured invariance manifold of real images under one-class supervision. Building upon a frozen VFM, we introduce lightweight projection heads that decompose representation space into complementary robust and fragile subspaces. The robust subspace is explicitly trained to suppress variations induced by physically plausible imaging transformations, approximating tangent directions of a real-image manifold, while the fragile subspace retains sensitivity to edit-like perturbations. A structured ordering margin enforces hierarchical separation between physical invariance and edit-induced variability, enabling detection as a margin-violation test relative to the learned manifold. At inference, multi-scale patch-wise drift under both transformation families yields a dual-channel invariance signature and interpretable localization. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong open-world generalization across unseen generators and resolutions, consistently outperforming training-free robustness-based baselines while providing interpretable invariance-violation maps.

中文摘要翻译: 生成图像模型的快速发展对现有AI生成图像检测器提出了挑战，特别是在包含未见生成器的开放世界场景下。近期无训练方法通过测量冻结视觉基础模型(VFM)中的鲁棒性差距，利用扰动引起的嵌入漂移来检测伪造图像。然而，这些方法依赖于预训练所继承的固定不变性几何结构，缺乏对检测任务的原理性自适应。本文则将AI生成图像检测表述为在单类监督下学习真实图像的结构化不变性流形。基于冻结VFM，我们引入轻量级投影头，将表示空间分解为互补的鲁棒子空间和脆弱子空间。鲁棒子空间经过显式训练以抑制由物理上合理的成像变换引起的变化，近似真实图像流形的切向方向；脆弱子空间则保持对编辑类扰动的敏感性。结构化排序间隔强制物理不变性与编辑诱导变异性之间的层次分离，从而能够将对所学流形的间隔违反测试作为检测依据。在推理阶段，两种变换族下的多尺度分块漂移产生双通道不变性签名，并提供可解释的定位。大量实验表明，该方法在未见生成器和分辨率上展现出强大的开放世界泛化能力，一致优于基于无训练鲁棒性的基线方法，同时提供可解释的不变性违规图。

</details>


### [39] [SVHighlights: Towards Extremely Long Sport Video Highlight Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06926)
*Donggyu Lee,Youngbin Ki,Jeonghun Kang,Taehwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出首个超长视频高光检测基准SVHighlights（视频平均时长2小时，总计640小时），并提出了无需训练的基于片段的方法TF-SELECTOR，通过语义分割和多模态大语言模型预测高光度，在多个指标上优于VTG基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高光检测方法主要针对短视频，缺乏面向超长视频（时长超过1小时）的合适基准，且现有模型在长视频上泛化能力差、缺乏全局上下文信息。

Method: 构建SVHighlights基准（320个超长体育视频，使用全长视频与官方高光视频配对自动生成标签）；提出TF-SELECTOR方法：无需训练，将视频按语义内容合并相邻镜头分割为上下文感知的片段，利用多模态大语言模型（融合视觉字幕、转录文本和音频音量）预测片段级高光度。

Result: SVHighlights包含320个视频，平均时长2小时，总计640.18小时；TF-SELECTOR在HIT@1、HIT@K和IoU指标上分别比VTG调优基线提升+3.12、+4.06和+2.95。

Conclusion: SVHighlights为超长视频高光检测提供了具有挑战性的测试基准，证明了基于片段的简单策略能有效扩展到小时级视频，且无需训练即可取得优异性能。

Original Abstract: While highlight detection for long-form videos is of great practical importance, most existing methods remain limited to short-form content, largely due to the absence of a suitable benchmark. To bridge this gap, we introduce SVHighlights, to the best of our knowledge, the first benchmark for highlight detection in extremely long sports videos, each exceeding one hour in duration, across multiple sports categories. SVHighlights is constructed from pairs of full-length sports videos and their corresponding official highlight videos using a dataset generation pipeline, enabling scalable label generation without conventional per-clip saliency annotation. The benchmark comprises 320 videos with an average duration of 2.00 hours and a total of 640.18 hours, substantially exceeding previous datasets. Existing methods also face fundamental challenges on long videos: models trained on short clips fail to generalize to hour-long content, and their clip-level scoring lacks the broader context needed to identify highlights. To address this and provide a strong baseline, we present TF-SELECTOR, a training-free segment-based approach that divides each video into context-aware segments by merging adjacent shots sharing the same semantic content, and predicts segment-level saliency scores using a large language model with multimodal inputs including visual captions, transcripts, and audio volume. Experiments demonstrate that TF-SELECTOR achieves superior performance across most metrics compared to Video Temporal Grounding (VTG)-tuned baselines, with improvements of +3.12 in HIT@1, +4.06 in HIT@K, and +2.95 in IoU. These results establish SVHighlights as a challenging testbed for long-form highlight detection and demonstrate that a simple segment-based strategy can effectively scale to hour-long videos.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然长视频的高光检测具有重要的实际意义，但现有大多数方法仍局限于短视频内容，这主要是由于缺乏合适的基准。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了SVHighlights——据我们所知，这是首个面向超长体育视频（每个视频时长超过一小时，涵盖多个体育类别）的高光检测基准。SVHighlights通过一个数据集生成流水线，从全长体育视频与其对应的官方高光视频配对中构建而成，无需传统的逐片段显著性标注即可实现可扩展的标签生成。该基准包含320个视频，平均时长为2.00小时，总计640.18小时，大幅超过此前数据集。现有方法在长视频上也面临根本性挑战：在短视频片段上训练的模型无法泛化到小时级内容，且其片段级评分缺乏识别高光所需的更广泛上下文。为解决这一问题并提供强基线，我们提出了TF-SELECTOR，一种无需训练的基于片段的方法。该方法通过合并共享相同语义内容的相邻镜头，将每个视频划分为上下文感知的片段，并使用多模态输入（包括视觉描述、转录文本和音频音量）的大语言模型预测片段级显著性分数。实验表明，与视频时间定位（VTG）调优的基线相比，TF-SELECTOR在大多数指标上取得了更优性能，在HIT@1、HIT@K和IoU上分别提升了+3.12、+4.06和+2.95。这些结果确立了SVHighlights作为长视频高光检测具有挑战性的测试平台的地位，并证明了简单的基于片段的策略可以有效地扩展到小时级视频。

</details>


### [40] [When CLIP Sees More, It Fights Back Harder: Multi-View Guided Adaptive Counterattacks for Test-Time Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06938)
*Sunoh Kim,Daeho Um*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多视图引导的自适应对抗攻击（MAC），通过构造增强视图、基于污染程度自适应调整攻击强度并聚合视图，显著提升了CLIP模型在对抗扰动下的鲁棒性，同时保持高效推理和内存效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有测试时对抗攻击（TTC）方法在强攻击下脆弱，因其依赖直接污染的原始视图，且采用噪声驱动的硬门控机制无法适应变化的污染严重程度。

Method: MAC首先构造输入图像的多个增强视图以获得多样化嵌入；然后对各视图的污染嵌入执行对抗攻击；接着基于估计的污染程度自适应缩放每个视图的对抗攻击强度；最后聚合自适应对抗攻击后的视图以得到鲁棒的最终预测。

Result: 在20个数据集和多种攻击场景下的广泛实验表明，MAC显著提升了鲁棒性，同时凭借其免调参设计保持了高推理速度和内存效率。

Conclusion: MAC通过多视图增强和污染感知的软加权自适应对抗攻击，有效克服了TTC的局限性，提升了CLIP的鲁棒性，且无需额外调参，兼顾性能与效率。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP have achieved remarkable zero-shot recognition capabilities, yet their robustness against adversarial perturbations remains limited. Test-time counterattack (TTC) was recently proposed to improve CLIP's robustness by perturbing an input image to steer it away from a corrupted state during inference. However, TTC remains fragile under strong attacks because its counterattack relies on a directly corrupted original view and employs a noise-driven hard-gating scheme that cannot adapt to varying corruption severity. To address these limitations, we introduce Multi-view guided Adaptive Counterattack (MAC), which performs counterattacks for multi-view with corruption-aware soft weighting. Specifically, MAC first constructs augmented views of an input image to obtain diverse embeddings. It then performs counterattacks to refine corrupted embeddings of views. Next, MAC adaptively scales the counterattack intensity for each view based on its estimated corruption degree. Finally, the adaptively counterattacked views are aggregated to yield a robust final prediction. Extensive experiments across 20 datasets and diverse attack scenarios demonstrate that MAC substantially improves robustness while preserving high inference speed and memory efficiency with its tuning-free design. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunoh-kim/MAC.

中文摘要翻译: 像CLIP这样的视觉-语言模型已经取得了显著的零样本识别能力，但它们在对抗扰动下的鲁棒性仍然有限。测试时对抗攻击（TTC）最近被提出通过扰动输入图像使其在推理过程中偏离被污染状态，从而提升CLIP的鲁棒性。然而，TTC在强攻击下仍然脆弱，因为其对抗攻击依赖于直接污染的原视图，并采用了无法适应变化污染严重程度的噪声驱动硬门控机制。为了解决这些局限性，我们引入了多视图引导的自适应对抗攻击（MAC），该方法对多视图执行具有污染感知软加权的对抗攻击。具体而言，MAC首先构建输入图像的增强视图以获得多样化嵌入；然后执行对抗攻击以细化各视图的被污染嵌入；接着，MAC根据每个视图估计的污染程度自适应地缩放其对抗攻击强度；最后，聚合自适应对抗攻击后的视图以得到鲁棒的最终预测。在20个数据集和多种攻击场景下的广泛实验表明，MAC凭借其免调参设计显著提升了鲁棒性，同时保持了高推理速度和内存效率。我们的代码可在https://github.com/sunoh-kim/MAC获取。

</details>


### [41] [SS-TPT: Stability and Suitability-Guided Test-Time Prompt Tuning for Adversarially Robust Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06943)
*Sunoh Kim,Daeho Um*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SS-TPT方法，通过稳定性与适宜性评分引导测试时提示微调，在不大量增加计算开销的前提下提升视觉-语言模型对抗鲁棒性，实现鲁棒性与吞吐量的最佳权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）在对抗扰动下脆弱，而测试时自适应防御方法虽能提升鲁棒性，但依赖大量增强视图导致推理速度显著下降，存在鲁棒性与吞吐量之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出稳定性与适宜性引导的测试时提示微调（SS-TPT），通过两个互补评分评估每个增强视图的质量：(1) 稳定性——预测对弱增强的不变性；(2) 适宜性——视图在特征空间中的密度。SS评分同时指导自适应过程（SS引导的一致性损失）和推理过程（SS加权的预测），增强可信视图、抑制被破坏视图。

Result: 在多种数据集和不同视图数量下，SS-TPT显著优于先前最先进方法，实现了更优的鲁棒性-吞吐量权衡，证明了方法的实用性和泛化性。

Conclusion: SS-TPT通过质量感知的视图选择机制，有效解决了测试时自适应中鲁棒性与效率的矛盾，是一种实用且通用的对抗鲁棒增强方法。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP achieve strong zero-shot recognition but remain highly fragile under adversarial perturbations. Recent test-time adaptation defenses improve robustness by leveraging many augmented views, but this leads to impractical slowdown and a clear robustness-throughput trade-off. To address this challenge, we present Stability and Suitability-guided Test-time Prompt Tuning (SS-TPT), evaluating the quality of each augmented view via two complementary scores: (1) stability, measuring prediction invariance to weak augmentations, and (2) suitability, measuring feature-space density among views. These stability and suitability (SS) scores guide both adaptation and inference through an SS-guided consistency loss and an SS-weighted prediction, amplifying trustworthy views while suppressing corrupted ones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SS-TPT significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods, achieving superior robustness-throughput trade-offs across diverse datasets and varying numbers of views, thereby demonstrating both strong practicality and generality. Our code is available at https://github.com/sunoh-kim/SS-TPT.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）在零样本识别上表现强劲，但在对抗扰动下仍然非常脆弱。最近的测试时自适应防御方法通过利用大量增强视图来提升鲁棒性，但这导致了不切实际的推理速度下降，并带来了明显的鲁棒性-吞吐量权衡问题。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了稳定性与适宜性引导的测试时提示微调（SS-TPT），通过两个互补评分来评估每个增强视图的质量：(1) 稳定性——衡量预测对弱增强的不变性；(2) 适宜性——衡量视图在特征空间中的密度。这些稳定性与适宜性（SS）评分通过SS引导的一致性损失和SS加权的预测来同时指导自适应过程和推理过程，从而放大可信视图并抑制被破坏的视图。大量实验表明，SS-TPT显著优于先前最先进的方法，在多种数据集和不同视图数量下实现了更优的鲁棒性-吞吐量权衡，展示了其强大的实用性和泛化能力。我们的代码已开源在https://github.com/sunoh-kim/SS-TPT。

</details>


### [42] [When is 3D Worth It? A Resource-Performance Frontier for CNNs and Transformers in Lung CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06950)
*Md Enamul Hoq,Sharafat Hossain,Imraul Emmaka,Linda Larson-Prior,Lawrence Tarbox,Jonathan Bona,Donald Johann Jr. and Fred Prior*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了2D、2.5D和3D三种输入维度在CNN和ViT架构下对肺结节分类任务的影响，发现2.5D CNN在判别力与稳定性之间取得了最佳权衡，而3D CNN存在阈值不稳定、Transformer存在预测退化等问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 3D医学影像虽被普遍认为更优，但实际价值取决于性能提升是否足以抵消计算成本与复杂度的增加。本文旨在严格受控条件下比较不同输入维度（2D、2.5D、3D）对模型行为的影响，而非提出新架构。

Method: 在固定训练协议下，分别在CNN和Vision Transformer（ViT）上评估2D、2.5D、3D三种输入维度的表现。使用无泄漏的NLST队列（n=1,977）和LIDC-IDRI辅助数据，采用ROC-AUC等指标进行系统比较。

Result: 2.5D CNN取得最优的判别-稳定性权衡（ROC-AUC 0.682，95% CI [0.546, 0.799]）且操作点稳定；3D CNN存在阈值不稳定性；Transformer出现全正例预测等退化行为。置信区间宽且有重叠，表明结果需审慎解读。

Conclusion: 在类别不平衡的肺癌筛查分类任务中，2D和2.5D输入在性能、稳定性和计算效率之间提供了比全3D表示更可靠的权衡。本文将结果定位为受控的资源-性能前沿和失败模式分类学，而非绝对优越性声明。

Original Abstract: Three-dimensional models are widely assumed preferable for volumetric medical imaging, yet their practical value depends on whether performance gains justify added computational cost and complexity. Rather than proposing a new architecture, we study how input dimensionality (2D, 2.5D, 3D) affects model behavior across convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs) under a fixed training protocol. Using a leakage-free NLST cohort (n = 1,977) with supporting LIDC-IDRI data, we find that the 2.5D CNN offers the most favorable discrimination-stability trade-off in our comparison (ROC-AUC 0.682, 95% CI [0.546, 0.799]) with a stable operating point. In contrast, 3D CNNs show threshold instability, and transformers exhibit degenerate predictions, such as all-positive predictions. Confidence intervals are wide and overlapping, so we present these results as a controlled resource-performance frontier and a failure-mode taxonomy rather than as definitive superiority claims. For class-imbalanced lung cancer screening classification, 2D and 2.5D inputs provide a more reliable trade-off between performance, stability, and computational efficiency than full 3D representations.

中文摘要翻译: 三维模型通常被认为更适合容积医学影像，然而其实用价值取决于性能提升是否足以抵消增加的计算成本与复杂度。本文并非提出新架构，而是在固定训练协议下研究输入维度（2D、2.5D、3D）如何影响卷积神经网络（CNN）和视觉Transformer（ViT）的模型行为。使用无泄漏的NLST队列（n=1,977）及辅助LIDC-IDRI数据，我们发现2.5D CNN在我们的比较中提供了最有利的判别力-稳定性权衡（ROC-AUC 0.682，95% CI [0.546, 0.799]），且具有稳定的操作点。相比之下，3D CNN存在阈值不稳定性，而Transformer则表现出退化预测，例如全正例预测。置信区间宽且相互重叠，因此我们将这些结果呈现为受控的资源-性能前沿和失败模式分类学，而非绝对优越性声明。对于类别不平衡的肺癌筛查分类任务，2D和2.5D输入在性能、稳定性和计算效率之间提供了比全3D表示更可靠的权衡。

</details>


### [43] [MVSegNet: A Lightweight Boundary-Aware Network for Fetal Lateral Ventricle Segmentation and Atrial Width Estimation in Prenatal Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06958)
*Arafat Hossain Sayem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVSegNet轻量级编解码网络，结合多尺度特征提取和边界感知细化，在胎儿侧脑室超声图像分割中达到最优性能，兼具高精度和低计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 胎儿脑室扩张通过超声测量侧脑室心房宽度来评估，准确分割至关重要，但超声图像中声影、散斑噪声和低对比度使分割困难，因此需要一种高效且精确的自动化分割方法。

Method: 提出MVSegNet轻量级编码器-解码器网络，结合多尺度特征提取和边界感知细化模块，在584张专家标注的经脑室超声图像上以70/15/15比例划分训练、验证和测试集。

Result: MVSegNet达到Dice 80.79%、IoU 68.47%、Hausdorff距离4.07 mm、心房宽度平均绝对误差3.40 mm；模型仅2.31M参数，在NVIDIA T4 GPU上达到165.6 FPS，在边界和测量指标上优于所有六种基线方法。

Conclusion: MVSegNet在保持低计算成本的同时，在边界和测量指标上优于所有评估的基线方法，支持其在自动化胎儿超声分析中的应用。

Original Abstract: Fetal ventriculomegaly is assessed by measuring the atrial width of the lateral ventricle in prenatal ultrasound. Accurate segmentation is essential for this measurement, but acoustic shadowing, speckle noise, and poor contrast make it difficult. We developed MVSegNet, a lightweight encoder-decoder network combining multi-scale feature extraction and boundary-aware refinement. The model was trained and evaluated on 584 expert-annotated transventricular ultrasound frames using a 70/15/15 split. Performance was compared against six segmentation baselines using overlap, boundary, and measurement metrics. MVSegNet achieved a Dice score of 80.79%, IoU of 68.47%, Hausdorff distance of 4.07 mm, and atrial width mean absolute error of 3.40 mm. The model contains 2.31 million parameters and runs at 165.6 frames per second on an NVIDIA T4 GPU. MVSegNet outperformed all evaluated baselines on boundary and measurement metrics while maintaining low computational cost, supporting its use in automated fetal ultrasound analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 胎儿脑室扩张通过产前超声测量侧脑室心房宽度来评估。准确分割对于这一测量至关重要，但声影、散斑噪声和低对比度使得分割困难。我们开发了MVSegNet，一种轻量级编码器-解码器网络，结合了多尺度特征提取和边界感知细化。该模型在584张专家标注的经脑室超声图像上以70/15/15的比例划分进行训练和评估。使用重叠、边界和测量指标与六种分割基线方法进行性能比较。MVSegNet的Dice得分为80.79%，IoU为68.47%，Hausdorff距离为4.07 mm，心房宽度平均绝对误差为3.40 mm。该模型包含231万个参数，在NVIDIA T4 GPU上运行速度为165.6帧/秒。MVSegNet在边界和测量指标上优于所有评估的基线方法，同时保持较低的计算成本，支持其在自动化胎儿超声分析中的应用。

</details>


### [44] [From Vision to Text: A Compact Multimodal Approach for Robust, Cross-Domain Presentation Attack Detection on ID Cards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06966)
*Qingwen Zeng,Juan E. Tapia,Sneha Das,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对身份证件演示攻击检测（PAD）中的跨域迁移问题，提出了一种基于生成式和判别式模块的紧凑多模态模型，融合视觉与文本数据。研究发现多模态模型在零样本设置下失效，且现有合成数据集无法反映真实挑战，因此呼吁重新评估合成数据基准，构建更真实多样的数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 身份证件PAD面临跨域迁移挑战，且由于隐私限制可用数据稀缺。现有方法泛化能力不足，尤其是零样本场景下表现不佳，而合成数据集也无法真实反映实际挑战，亟需更有效的方法和更真实的评估基准。

Method: 提出一种紧凑的多模态模型，包含新的生成式模块和判别式模块，融合视觉数据（图像）和文本数据（文本信息）对真实和合成身份证图像进行演示攻击检测。

Result: 多模态模型在有监督微调后表现出强泛化能力，但在零样本设置下失败。模型容量和真实世界数据对可靠的PAD至关重要，而现有合成数据集可能无法反映真实世界的挑战。

Conclusion: 应重新评估合成数据作为基准的有效性，强调需要更真实、更多样化的数据集来推动PAD研究的发展。

Original Abstract: Cross-domain shifts challenge Presentation Attack Detection (PAD) on ID Cards, given the restricted data available due to privacy concerns. This work proposes a compact multimodal model, based on new generative and discriminative blocks, which combines visual and textual data for PAD on genuine and synthetic ID images. While multimodal models exhibit strong generalisation after supervised fine-tuning, they fail in zero-shot settings. Our findings underscore that model capacity and real-world data are essential for reliable PAD, while existing synthetic datasets may not reflect real-world challenges. We argue for a re-evaluation of synthetic data as a benchmark and emphasise the need for more realistic, diverse datasets to advance PAD research.

中文摘要翻译: 跨域迁移对身份证件的演示攻击检测（PAD）提出了挑战，因为隐私问题导致可用数据受限。本文提出了一种紧凑的多模态模型，基于新的生成式和判别式模块，融合视觉和文本数据对真实与合成的身份证图像进行PAD。尽管多模态模型在监督微调后表现出强泛化能力，但在零样本设置下会失效。我们的研究结果强调，模型容量和真实世界数据对可靠的PAD至关重要，而现有的合成数据集可能无法反映真实世界的挑战。我们主张重新评估合成数据作为基准的有效性，并强调需要更真实、更多样化的数据集来推动PAD研究的发展。

</details>


### [45] [CL-CLIP: CLIP-Based Continual Learning Framework with Cost-Volume Category Decoupling for Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06978)
*Zihan Liu,Yuguang Yang,Shengjie Su,Jianing Pang,Linlin Yang,Chunyu Xie,Nikolai Yu. Zolotykh,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CL-CLIP框架，通过成本体积引导的类别解耦，为开放词汇检测器赋予持续学习能力，在持续微调下显著缓解灾难性遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 开放词汇检测器（如F-ViT）在零样本场景下对未见类别表现良好，但一旦对其进行持续更新（微调），会遭受严重的灾难性遗忘，失去先前校准的检测能力。因此需要将开放词汇检测器与持续学习能力相结合。

Method: 基于F-ViT和CAT-Seg，计算CLIP图像-文本相似性成本体积（密集的类别级响应图），将其作为零样本空间先验，将共享区域特征解耦为类别特定路径，然后由多专家RoI头（Multi-Expert RoI head）处理。

Result: 在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO数据集上，CL-CLIP显著提升了F-ViT基线在持续微调下的性能，与现有持续目标检测器相比具有竞争力，特别是在适应新类别的同时保持基类性能。

Conclusion: CL-CLIP通过成本体积引导的类别解耦策略，成功将开放词汇检测器的零样本泛化能力与持续学习能力统一起来，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。

Original Abstract: Continual Object Detection (COD) requires a detector to acquire new categories over time while preserving previously learned ones. This goal is closely related to open-vocabulary detection, since both settings require reasoning over categories that are not fully covered by the annotations available at the current training stage. Recent CLIP-based open-vocabulary detectors have shown strong zero-shot generalization, and frameworks such as F-ViT demonstrate that vision-language pretraining can provide powerful zero-shot detection ability for unseen categories. However, real-world deployments cannot remain purely zero-shot: once these detectors are continually updated on newly introduced categories, they suffer severe catastrophic forgetting and quickly lose their previously calibrated detection ability. We therefore propose CL-CLIP, a CLIP-based COD framework that equips open-vocabulary detectors with better continual learning ability through cost-volume-guided category decoupling. Specifically, following CAT-Seg, we compute a CLIP image-text similarity cost volume, defined as dense category-wise response maps between visual tokens and class text embeddings. This zero-shot spatial prior decomposes shared region features into class-specific pathways, which are then processed by a Multi-Expert RoI head. Extensive experiments on PASCAL VOC and MS-COCO show that CL-CLIP substantially improves the F-ViT baseline under continual fine-tuning and achieves competitive performance with existing continual object detectors, especially in adapting to newly introduced categories while preserving competitive base-class performance.

中文摘要翻译: 持续目标检测（COD）要求检测器在随时间获取新类别的同时保持先前学到的类别。该目标与开放词汇检测密切相关，因为两种设置都需要对当前训练阶段标注未完全覆盖的类别进行推理。近期基于CLIP的开放词汇检测器展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，像F-ViT这样的框架证明了视觉-语言预训练能够为未见类别提供强大的零样本检测能力。然而，实际部署无法停留在纯零样本状态：一旦这些检测器在新引入的类别上持续更新，它们会遭受严重的灾难性遗忘，并迅速失去先前校准的检测能力。因此，我们提出了CL-CLIP，一个基于CLIP的COD框架，通过成本体积引导的类别解耦为开放词汇检测器赋予更好的持续学习能力。具体而言，遵循CAT-Seg，我们计算CLIP图像-文本相似性成本体积，将其定义为视觉令牌与类别文本嵌入之间的密集类别级响应图。这种零样本空间先验将共享区域特征分解为类别特定路径，然后由多专家RoI头进行处理。在PASCAL VOC和MS-COCO上的大量实验表明，CL-CLIP在持续微调下显著提升了F-ViT基线，并与现有持续目标检测器取得了具有竞争力的性能，特别是在适应新引入类别的同时保持竞争性的基类性能。

</details>


### [46] [Don't Pause: Streaming Video-Language Synchrony for Online Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06991)
*Zhenyu Yang,Kairui Zhang,Shengsheng Qian,Weiming Dong,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LyraV提出了一种面向在线视频理解的流式视频-语言同步（SVLS）范式，通过无训练验证有限状态机（FDTC）和轻量级预测模块（SToP），实现逐帧增量令牌解码，让视频感知与语言生成实时交错，达到98.29%的同步率和3.89 FPS的实时处理速度，且展现出动态推理能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型在流式场景中，生成响应时会暂停视频感知，破坏实时视频-语言同步，导致卡顿。

Method: 提出层次化控制框架，包含两个核心创新：(1) FDTC——基于无训练验证的有限状态机，在高层语义上决定继续说话、开始新回复或保持静默；(2) SToP——即插即用的轻量级预测模块，动态调整语言生成速率以匹配视觉内容节奏。具体地，LyraV执行逐帧增量、子预算解码，每帧间隔内只发射符合实时预算的小块令牌，确保感知不被完整句子阻塞。

Result: 在5个在线和3个离线基准上，LyraV保持了骨干模型的一般理解能力，同时显著提升了流式同步性和叙事流畅性，实现了98.29%的视频播放同步率和3.89 FPS的实时处理速度，并展现出流式令牌动态推理能力。

Conclusion: LyraV通过层次化控制框架有效解决了流式视频理解中感知与生成不同步的问题，实现了细粒度同步，并观察到其在流式令牌上具备动态推理能力。

Original Abstract: Online Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) have advanced toward seamless human-AI interaction through frame-by-frame processing and proactive responding. However, a critical challenge remains in streaming scenarios: existing models typically pause video perception while generating responses, breaking real-time video-language synchrony and causing stutters. To address this, we introduce a novel paradigm for online video understanding: Streaming Video-Language Synchrony (SVLS), and present LyraV, a live streaming assistant built upon a hierarchical control framework with two core innovations. First, the Frame-Driven Transition Controller (FDTC), a training-free verification-based finite-state machine, makes high-level semantic decisions on when to continue speaking, start a new response, or stay silent. Second, the Streaming Token Pacer (SToP), a plug-and-play lightweight predictive module, dynamically adapts the language generation rate to match the pace of the visual content. Concretely, LyraV performs \emph{per-frame incremental, sub-budget decoding}: within each frame interval it emits only a small chunk of tokens that fits the real-time budget, so perception is never blocked for a full sentence. Together, these components enable LyraV to seamlessly interleave incoming video frames with generated word tokens, achieving a fine-grained synchrony. Extensive experiments conducted on five online and three offline benchmarks demonstrate that LyraV preserves the backbone's general understanding ability while substantially improving streaming synchrony and narrative fluency, delivering a 98.29\% synchrony with video playback and a real-time processing speed of 3.89 FPS. Interestingly, we observe an empirical capability in LyraV: dynamic reasoning over streaming tokens, enabling continuous interpretation and "thinking" alongside visual input.

中文摘要翻译: 在线视频大语言模型（Video-LLMs）通过逐帧处理和主动响应，在人机无缝交互方面取得了进展。然而，在流式场景中仍存在一个关键挑战：现有模型在生成响应时通常会暂停视频感知，破坏了实时视频-语言同步并导致卡顿。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种面向在线视频理解的新范式——流式视频-语言同步（Streaming Video-Language Synchrony, SVLS），并构建了LyraV，一个基于层次化控制框架的直播助手，包含两项核心创新。首先，帧驱动转移控制器（Frame-Driven Transition Controller, FDTC），一个无需训练、基于验证的有限状态机，在高层语义上决定何时继续说话、开始新回复或保持静默。其次，流式令牌节奏器（Streaming Token Pacer, SToP），一个即插即用的轻量级预测模块，动态调整语言生成速率以匹配视觉内容的节奏。具体地，LyraV执行逐帧增量、子预算解码：在每个帧间隔内仅发射符合实时预算的小块令牌，从而感知永远不会被完整句子阻塞。这些组件共同使LyraV能够将传入的视频帧与生成的词令牌无缝交错，实现细粒度同步。在5个在线和3个离线基准上进行的广泛实验表明，LyraV在保持骨干模型一般理解能力的同时，显著提升了流式同步性和叙事流畅性，实现了98.29%的视频播放同步率和3.89 FPS的实时处理速度。有趣的是，我们观察到LyraV的一项经验性能力：对流式令牌进行动态推理，使其能够在对视觉输入进行持续解释和"思考"。

</details>


### [47] [GuideCAD: A Lightweight Multimodal Framework for 3D CAD Model Generation via Prefix Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07024)
*Minseong Kim,Jinyeong Park,Sungho Park,Jibum Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GuideCAD提出一种轻量级多模态3D CAD生成方法，通过映射网络将图像嵌入转换为前缀嵌入，利用预训练大语言模型整合视觉与文本信息，仅用少量可训练参数即可生成高质量3D CAD模型，参数减少约4倍，训练效率提升2倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的多模态3D CAD生成方法需要大量计算资源，训练效率低下，亟需一种更高效的训练方式。

Method: 提出GuideCAD方法，使用映射网络将图像嵌入转换为前缀嵌入，使预训练的大语言模型能够整合视觉和文本信息；随后基于Transformer的解码器利用视觉-文本嵌入预测构建序列，从而生成3D CAD模型。

Result: 在自构建的GuideCAD数据集（文本-图像对）上，GuideCAD在生成高质量3D CAD模型的同时，参数量减少约4倍，训练效率提升2倍。

Conclusion: GuideCAD通过利用语义丰富的视觉-文本表示和极少可训练参数，实现了高效的3D CAD生成，显著优于微调方法。

Original Abstract: Multi-modal approaches used for 3D CAD generation require substantial computational resources, necessitating efficient training. To address this, we propose GuideCAD, which leverages semantically rich visual-textual representations having only a small number of trainable parameters to generate 3D CAD models. Specifically, GuideCAD uses a mapping network that converts image embeddings into prefix embeddings, enabling a pretrained large language model to integrate visual and textual information. As a result, a transformer-based decoder predicts the construction sequence using the visual-textual embeddings in order to generate the 3D CAD model. For experimental evaluation, we construct a new dataset, referred to as GuideCAD, which consists of text-image pairs. Each pair includes a text prompt that represents a 3D CAD construction sequence and its corresponding 3D CAD image. Our experimental results show that GuideCAD generates comparably high-quality 3D CAD models while using approximately four times fewer parameters and achieving twice the training efficiency compared to fine-tuning approaches. We have released the source code and dataset for our method at: https://github.com/mskimS2/GuideCAD

中文摘要翻译: 用于3D CAD生成的多模态方法需要大量的计算资源，因此亟需高效的训练方式。为解决这一问题，我们提出GuideCAD，该方法利用语义丰富的视觉-文本表示，仅使用少量可训练参数即可生成3D CAD模型。具体而言，GuideCAD使用一个映射网络将图像嵌入转换为前缀嵌入，使预训练的大语言模型能够整合视觉和文本信息。随后，基于Transformer的解码器利用视觉-文本嵌入预测构建序列，从而生成3D CAD模型。为进行实验评估，我们构建了一个新的数据集（也称为GuideCAD），该数据集由文本-图像对组成，每对包含表示3D CAD构建序列的文本提示及其对应的3D CAD图像。实验结果表明，GuideCAD在生成质量相当的3D CAD模型的同时，参数量比微调方法减少约四倍，训练效率提升两倍。我们已在https://github.com/mskimS2/GuideCAD上公开了源代码和数据集。

</details>


### [48] [Never Seen Before: Benchmarking Genuine Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval with Consistent Video-Sourced Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07032)
*Zhenyu Yang,Zemin Du,Shengsheng Qian,Changsheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZeroSight 是一个针对零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）的新基准，通过从视频中提取一致性的参考-目标图像对、使用2022年3月后数据确保真正零样本场景，并提出了一种无需训练的MLLM驱动方法SC4CIR，实验表明现有数据集和评估指标夸大了CIR方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ZS-CIR数据集存在两个主要问题：(1) 参考图像与目标图像之间因噪声来源导致完全不相关；(2) 使用CLIP已训练过的公开图像数据集，未能实现真正的零样本场景。因此需要构建一个更可靠、真正零样本的基准来准确评估CIR方法的能力。

Method: (1) 构建ZeroSight基准：从视频中提取连续帧作为参考-目标对，确保视觉和语义一致性；使用2022年3月31日后发布的视频数据，确保CLIP未预训练过；利用LLM辅助生成相对描述。(2) 提出SC4CIR方法：一种无需训练的基于MLLM的方法，通过3个对称一致性检查来识别困难负样本，即插即用，可集成到各种CIR方法中。

Result: 对27种方法的实验结果表明，现有的ZS-CIR数据集和评估指标会导致检索性能虚高，夸大了CIR方法的实际能力。

Conclusion: ZeroSight为ZS-CIR提供了更可靠的评估基准，而SC4CIR作为一种即插即用的训练-free方法，能有效提升各种CIR方法的性能。当前CIR方法的真实能力可能被现有基准高估。

Original Abstract: Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval (ZS-CIR) aims to retrieve a target image based on a query composed of a reference image and a relative caption without training samples. Existing ZS-CIR datasets often suffer from complete irrelevance between reference and target images due to noisy image sources, and do not achieve a true zero-shot scenario as they use public image datasets that models like CLIP have been trained on. To tackle these challenges, we introduce ZeroSight, a novel benchmark for ZS-CIR. It includes a dataset with consistent reference-target pairs sourced from videos, a data construction pipeline, and evaluation methods that consider the ranking of multiple positive and negative target images. We ensure visually and semantically consistent reference-target pairs by extracting frames from a single video and generating relative captions using LLM-assisted methods. To ensure a true zero-shot scenario, we use video data published after March 31, 2022, ensuring it was not included in CLIP's pre-training data. Additionally, we propose a training-free MLLM-driven method, SC4CIR (Symmetric Consistency for CIR), which can effectively identify hard negative targets through 3 symmetric consistency checks. This method is plug-and-play, seamlessly integrating with various CIR methods and significantly improving performance. Our experimental results from 27 methods reveal that current ZS-CIR datasets and evaluation metrics result in inflated retrieval performance, exaggerating the capabilities of CIR methods. Our benchmark and models can be accessed at https://github.com/sotayang/ZeroSight.

中文摘要翻译: 零样本组合图像检索（ZS-CIR）旨在没有训练样本的情况下，基于由参考图像和相对描述组成的查询来检索目标图像。现有的ZS-CIR数据集常因图像来源噪声而导致参考图像与目标图像完全不相关，并且由于使用了CLIP等模型已训练过的公开图像数据集，未能实现真正的零样本场景。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了ZeroSight，一个全新的ZS-CIR基准。它包含一个从视频中提取的一致参考-目标对数据集、一个数据构建流水线，以及考虑多个正负目标图像排名的评估方法。我们通过从单个视频中提取帧并使用LLM辅助方法生成相对描述，确保了参考-目标对在视觉和语义上的一致性。为确保真正的零样本场景，我们使用了2022年3月31日之后发布的视频数据，确保其未包含在CLIP的预训练数据中。此外，我们提出了一种无需训练的MLLM驱动方法SC4CIR（对称一致性用于CIR），该方法通过3个对称一致性检查能够有效识别困难负样本目标。该方法即插即用，可无缝集成到各种CIR方法中并显著提升性能。我们对27种方法的实验结果表明，当前的ZS-CIR数据集和评估指标导致检索性能虚高，夸大了CIR方法的实际能力。我们的基准和模型可在https://github.com/sotayang/ZeroSight获取。

</details>


### [49] [ForensicConcept: Transferable Forensic Concepts for AIGI Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07034)
*Menyanshu Zhou,Ziyin Zhou,Ke Sun,Yunpeng Luo,Jiayi Ji,Xiaoshuai Sun,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ForensicConcept框架，通过提取检测器的显式取证概念并跨骨干网络迁移，解决了AI生成图像检测器在未知生成器上泛化能力差的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在分布内数据上表现良好，但在未见过的生成器上泛化失败，而检测器的黑盒特性使其难以揭示决策依据，阻碍了对失败原因的理解和改进。

Method: (1) 通过Transformer注意力定位决策关键补丁，聚类为紧凑概念码本；(2) 引入基于CleanDIFT扩散特征的生成痕迹参考，通过邻域结构一致性(CKNNA)量化骨干痕迹对齐；(3) 提出概念码本注入，将扩散派生的概念迁移到目标骨干网络。

Result: 在GenImage、GAN-family和Chameleon基准测试上，该方法一致优于先前方法；CKNNA对齐度可以预测迁移效果，解释了不同骨干网络产生可迁移取证证据能力差异的原因。

Conclusion: ForensicConcept通过显式可审计的取证概念提取与跨骨干迁移，有效提升了AI生成图像检测器在未知生成器上的泛化能力，且CKNNA对齐度为迁移效果提供了原则性解释。

Original Abstract: AI-generated image detectors achieve high accuracy on in-distribution data but often fail on unseen generators. A key obstacle to understanding this failure is the black-box nature of current detectors: they do not reveal which evidence drives their decisions. We propose ForensicConcept, a framework that extracts explicit forensic concepts from detectors and enables their transfer across backbones. Our method localizes decision-critical patches via Transformer attribution, clusters them into a compact concept codebook, and uses a concept-aligned projection to produce auditable evidence readouts. Motivated by prior studies showing that DINO representations can guide diffusion generation and exhibit concept-level correspondence with diffusion features, we introduce a generation-trace reference based on CleanDIFT diffusion features and quantify backbone-trace alignment via neighborhood-structure consistency (CKNNA). We further propose concept codebook injection to transfer diffusion-derived concepts into target backbones. Experiments on GenImage, GAN-family, and Chameleon benchmarks show consistent improvements over prior methods. We also find that CKNNA alignment predicts transfer effectiveness, providing a principled explanation for why some backbones yield more transferable forensic evidence than others.

中文摘要翻译: AI生成图像检测器在分布内数据上表现良好，但在未见过的生成器上常常失败。理解这一失败的关键障碍在于当前检测器的黑盒特性：它们不揭示驱动决策的证据。我们提出ForensicConcept，一个从检测器中提取显式取证概念并使其能够在不同骨干网络之间迁移的框架。我们的方法通过Transformer注意力定位决策关键补丁，将其聚类为紧凑的概念码本，并利用概念对齐投影生成可审计的证据输出。受先前研究中DINO表示能够引导扩散生成并与扩散特征呈现概念级对应关系的启发，我们引入基于CleanDIFT扩散特征的生成痕迹参考，并通过邻域结构一致性(CKNNA)量化骨干痕迹对齐。我们进一步提出概念码本注入，将扩散派生的概念迁移到目标骨干网络中。在GenImage、GAN-family和Chameleon基准上的实验表明，该方法一致优于先前方法。我们还发现CKNNA对齐度能够预测迁移效果，这为解释为何某些骨干网络能产生更具迁移性的取证证据提供了原则性说明。

</details>


### [50] [STREAM: Stochastic Riemannian Flow Matching with Anisotropic Decoder for Digital Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07036)
*Won June Cho,Daeky Jeong,Hyeongyeol Lim,Hongjun Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STREAM提出利用预训练的组织病理学视觉基础模型特征作为潜空间，并在单位超球面上应用黎曼流匹配进行病理图像生成，解决了现有方法中“条件塌陷”导致生成质量与多样性下降的问题，在乳腺癌和结直肠癌数据集上达到了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有组织病理学图像生成模型使用预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）作为条件信号，导致“条件塌陷”——条件信号主导潜空间，降低了生成样本的质量和多样性。因此，本文提出将预训练的组织病理学VFM直接作为潜空间，利用其编码丰富语义信息的patch-token特征。

Method: STREAM包含两个阶段：1) 在单位超球面S^{d-1}上采用桥式随机扰动建立每token的可整流性，用于训练潜空间中的扩散变换器（DiT）；2) 设计一种新颖的各向异性解码器，沿速度场雅可比的高能量方向保持保真度，同时沿低能量方向分配鲁棒性。

Result: 在乳腺癌和结直肠癌数据集上，STREAM在重建和生成性能上均达到了最优结果。

Conclusion: STREAM是首个在病理学领域应用黎曼流匹配的框架，通过将组织病理学VFM特征作为潜空间并采用黎曼几何建模，有效解决了条件塌陷问题，实现了高质量、多样化的病理图像生成。

Original Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation addresses critical challenges in computational pathology, including patient privacy and the growing need for large-scale training data for foundation models. Latent diffusion models have dominated the image generation domain, with recent works emphasizing that the choice of latent space is critical to the quality of generated images. Existing state-of-the-art generative models in histopathology use pretrained Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) as conditioning signals, and we observe that this leads to "conditioning collapse," where the conditioning signal dominates the latent space and lowers the quality and diversity of generated samples. Therefore, we instead use pretrained histopathology VFMs as the latent space itself, leveraging their patch-token features that encode rich semantic information. We empirically show that these features are $\ell_2$-normalized and lie on the unit hypersphere $\mathcal{S}^{d-1}$ with strong angular dominance and intrinsic curvature, making them naturally suited for a Riemannian formulation. We therefore present STREAM, the first framework to apply Riemannian flow matching in the pathology domain. STREAM consists of two stages: 1) a bridge-type stochastic perturbation that establishes per-token rectifiability on $\mathcal{S}^{d-1}$ for training a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) in latent space, and 2) a novel anisotropic decoder that allocates robustness to low-energy directions of the velocity-field Jacobian while preserving fidelity along its high-energy directions. Together, STREAM achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction and generation performance on breast and colorectal cancer datasets. The code will be publicly released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 合成组织病理学图像生成解决了计算病理学中的关键挑战，包括患者隐私以及基础模型对大规模训练数据日益增长的需求。潜在扩散模型主导了图像生成领域，近期工作强调潜空间的选择对生成图像质量至关重要。现有组织病理学中最先进的生成模型使用预训练的视觉基础模型（VFM）作为条件信号，我们观察到这会导致"条件塌陷"——条件信号主导潜空间，降低了生成样本的质量和多样性。因此，我们转而使用预训练的组织病理学VFM作为潜空间本身，利用其编码丰富语义信息的patch-token特征。我们通过实验证明这些特征经过ℓ₂归一化后位于单位超球面S^{d-1}上，具有强烈的角度主导性和内在曲率，使其天然适合黎曼几何建模。因此，我们提出了STREAM，这是首个在病理学领域应用黎曼流匹配的框架。STREAM包含两个阶段：1) 在S^{d-1}上设计桥式随机扰动，为训练潜空间中的扩散变换器（DiT）建立每token的可整流性；2) 一种新颖的各向异性解码器，在速度场雅可比矩阵的低能量方向分配鲁棒性，同时在其高能量方向保持保真度。综合而言，STREAM在乳腺癌和结直肠癌数据集上达到了最优的重建和生成性能。代码将在接收后公开。

</details>


### [51] [TrioPose: Native Triple-Stream Diffusion Transformers for Pose-Guided Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07053)
*Dian Gu,Zhengyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrioPose提出一种基于SD3.5M架构的原生姿态驱动框架，通过三流姿态感知DiT、可学习关系偏置掩码和姿态引导空间损失加权策略，有效解决了多人姿态引导文本到图像生成中的肢体扭曲和特征串扰问题，在Human-Art等基准上实现了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于UNet的姿态引导文本到图像生成方法在复杂多人场景中存在肢体扭曲和特征串扰问题，且UNet难以建模长程空间依赖。虽然新型多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）具备优越的全局建模能力，但简单的信号拼接会严重破坏预训练潜空间分布，因此需要一种既能利用MM-DiT优势又不破坏预训练分布的姿态驱动方法。

Method: 1) 三流姿态感知DiT（TSPA-DiT）：将姿态作为独立模态，采用逐层激活和零初始化双残差注入，在不破坏预训练潜空间稳定性的前提下施加几何约束。2) 可学习关系偏置掩码（LRBM）：将拓扑连接性细化为细粒度物理状态，映射为连续的注意力软约束，解耦实例间干扰。3) 姿态引导空间损失加权（PGSLW）：利用热图导出的误差图调制原生扩散目标，将解剖监督聚焦于易失真区域。

Result: 在Human-Art、CrowdPose和OCHuman等挑战性基准上达到SOTA性能。在Human-Art上AP达64.33，相比先前方法提升30%，在视觉保真度和文本-图像语义对齐方面树立了新标准。

Conclusion: TrioPose通过将姿态作为独立模态并引入三种创新设计，有效解决了复杂多人姿态引导生成中的关键挑战，证明了基于MM-DiT的原生姿态驱动框架在多人生成任务中的优越性。

Original Abstract: Pose-guided text-to-image generation often suffers from limb distortions and feature crosstalk in complex multi-person scenarios. While existing UNet-based adapters struggle with long-range spatial dependencies, emerging Multimodal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) offer superior global modeling. However, naive signal concatenation in MM-DiTs severely disrupts pre-trained latent distributions. To address this, we propose TrioPose, a native pose-driven framework built upon the SD3.5M architecture. Specifically, we introduce a Triple-Stream Pose-Aware DiT (TSPA-DiT) that treats pose as an independent modality. It employs layer-wise activation and zero-initialized dual-residual injection to smoothly enforce geometric constraints while preserving pre-trained latent stability. To resolve severe multi-instance occlusions, we design a Learnable Relational Bias Mask that categorizes topological connectivity into fine-grained physical states, mapping them into continuous attention soft constraints to effectively decouple inter-instance interference. Furthermore, a Pose-Guided Spatial Loss Weighting strategy modulates the native diffusion objective using heatmap-derived error maps, focusing anatomical supervision strictly on distortion-prone regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TrioPose achieves state-of-the-art performance across challenging benchmarks, including Human-Art, CrowdPose, and OCHuman. Notably, it attains an AP of $64.33$ on Human-Art, representing a $30\%$ improvement over prior arts, while setting new standards for visual fidelity and text-image semantic alignment in complex multi-human generation.

中文摘要翻译: 姿态引导的文本到图像生成在复杂多人场景中常遭受肢体扭曲和特征串扰问题。现有基于UNet的适配器难以处理长程空间依赖，而新兴的多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）具备优越的全局建模能力。然而，MM-DiT中简单的信号拼接会严重破坏预训练的潜空间分布。为解决这一问题，我们提出TrioPose，一个基于SD3.5M架构的原生姿态驱动框架。具体而言，我们引入三流姿态感知DiT（TSPA-DiT），将姿态视为独立模态。它采用逐层激活和零初始化的双残差注入，在保持预训练潜空间稳定性的同时平滑地施加几何约束。为解决严重的多实例遮挡，我们设计了可学习关系偏置掩码，将拓扑连接性细化为细粒度的物理状态，将其映射为连续的注意力软约束，从而有效解耦实例间干扰。此外，姿态引导的空间损失加权策略利用热图导出的误差图调制原生扩散目标，将解剖监督严格聚焦于易扭曲区域。大量实验表明，TrioPose在Human-Art、CrowdPose和OCHuman等挑战性基准上均达到最优性能。值得注意的是，它在Human-Art上取得了64.33的AP，相比先前方法提升了30%，同时在复杂多人生成中为视觉保真度和文本-图像语义对齐设立了新标准。

</details>


### [52] [AsyncPatch Diffusion: spatially-flexible image generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07079)
*Samuele Papa,Valentin De Bortoli,Guillaume Couairon,Daniel Sýkora,Romuald Elie,Klaus Greff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AsyncPatch Diffusion，一种联合扩散框架，允许对输入的不同空间区域施加不同噪声水平，实现空间自适应去噪轨迹，无需任务特定微调即可生成高质量图像并自然支持图像修复。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准扩散模型对整个样本施加统一的噪声水平，强制所有空间区域遵循相同的去噪轨迹，限制了模型在空间异质性场景（如图像修复、局部编辑）中的灵活性和适应性。

Method: 1) 提出AsyncPatch Diffusion，一种联合扩散框架，为图像像素或潜在令牌等不同输入维度分配不同的噪声水平；2) 证明了该异步腐蚀过程定义了有效的生成过程，并推导了首个有效的ELBO；3) 提出受控噪声水平采样器，平衡平均腐蚀水平和空间变异度，避免训练中极端异构配置过拟合；4) 引入输入引导机制，利用干净或部分腐蚀区域引导未知区域生成，提升局部一致性和纹理匹配。

Result: 在ImageNet 256和LSUN上达到与常规扩散相当的生质量；无需任务特定微调即可自然支持图像修复；提出的输入引导机制提升了局部一致性和纹理匹配；支持不确定性引导加速和自回归采样等自适应生成策略。

Conclusion: AsyncPatch Diffusion通过空间异质噪声水平分配，扩展了扩散模型的生成能力，在保持生成质量的同时实现了更高的灵活性和适应性，特别适用于图像修复等局部生成任务。

Original Abstract: Standard diffusion models corrupt an entire sample with a single shared noise level, forcing all spatial regions to follow the same denoising trajectory. We introduce AsyncPatch Diffusion, a joint-diffusion framework that assigns distinct noise levels to different input dimensions, such as image pixels, or latent tokens. We show how this asynchronous corruption defines a valid generative process while supporting a richer family of spatially heterogeneous denoising trajectories, and prove the first valid ELBO for this process. We show that a single pretrained model can perform spatially adaptive generation, where different regions are denoised on different schedules. A key challenge is training: naive independent noise-level sampling overemphasizes highly heterogeneous configurations and underrepresents homogeneous noise levels, that are crucial during sampling. We address this with a controlled noise-level sampler that regulates both the average corruption level and its spatial variability. AsyncPatch achieves generation quality comparable to conventional diffusion on ImageNet 256 and LSUN, while being natively suited for inpainting without task-specific fine-tuning. We further introduce input guidance, which uses clean or partially corrupted regions to guide the generation of unknown regions, improving local consistency and texture matching. Finally, we demonstrate adaptive generation strategies including uncertainty-guided acceleration and autoregressive sampling.

中文摘要翻译: 标准扩散模型对整个样本施加单一的共享噪声水平，强制所有空间区域遵循相同的去噪轨迹。本文提出AsyncPatch Diffusion——一种联合扩散框架，为不同的输入维度（如图像像素或潜在令牌）分配不同的噪声水平。我们展示了这种异步腐蚀如何定义一个有效的生成过程，同时支持更丰富的空间异质去噪轨迹族，并证明了该过程的首个有效ELBO。我们证明，单个预训练模型可以执行空间自适应生成，即不同区域按不同调度进行去噪。一个关键挑战是训练：朴素的独立噪声水平采样过度强调高度异构的配置，而低估了在采样过程中至关重要的均匀噪声水平。我们通过一个受控噪声水平采样器解决了这个问题，该采样器同时调节平均腐蚀水平及其空间变异性。AsyncPatch在ImageNet 256和LSUN上实现了与常规扩散相当的生成质量，同时天然适用于图像修复而无需任务特定的微调。我们进一步引入了输入引导，利用干净或部分腐蚀区域来引导未知区域的生成，改善了局部一致性和纹理匹配。最后，我们展示了包括不确定性引导加速和自回归采样在内的自适应生成策略。

</details>


### [53] [An Adaptive Data cleaning Framework for Noisy Label Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07086)
*Chen-Hsuan Fang,Wei-Hsinag Chen,Pin-Hsuan Yu,Jung-Hua Wang,Tsung-Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需人工阈值和噪声先验的自适应数据清洗框架，通过融合局部、全局和学习动态的多度量特征空间聚类，实现鲁棒的噪声标签检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据清洗和样本选择策略依赖人工设定阈值、噪声比例先验或单一度量指标（学习动态或几何结构），在复杂数据场景下不稳定，亟需一种阈值无关、鲁棒且低调参的噪声标签检测方法。

Method: 提出自适应的数据清洗框架，将样本映射到统一低维特征空间，通过模块化特征拼接整合局部（类自适应KNN局部不一致性）、全局（K-means全局质心距离）和学习动态（z归一化得分）三种线索；在该特征空间上执行多度量聚类，自适应地将样本划分为干净主导和噪声主导两部分，无需人工阈值或噪声先验。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、MNIST和ImageNet-100上5%~40%对称标签噪声实验中取得高召回率；在ImageNet-100的40%噪声下达到接近完美的召回率（≥98%）；后续训练在各项设定下均取得精度提升，尤其在ImageNet-100严重噪声下效果显著。

Conclusion: 多度量融合提供了一种阈值无关、实用且低调参的噪声标签检测策略，有效提升模型在噪声标签下的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) excel in computer vision tasks given large annotated datasets. In real-world applications, however, labels are often corrupted by ambiguity, human error, or dynamic environments. Over-parameterized DNNs easily memorize these noisy labels during training, degrading model accuracy and generalization. Existing data-cleaning and sample-selection strategies often rely on manually specified thresholds, prior knowledge of the noise ratio, or a single metric (either learning dynamics or geometric structure), making them unstable in complex data regimes. This paper proposes a self-adaptive data-cleaning framework that integrates local, global, and learning dynamics cues for robust noisy-label detection. Samples are mapped into a unified low-dimensional feature space through a modular feature concatenation paradigm. We provide two instantiations: a 2D metric integrating class-adaptive KNN-based local disagreement with k-means-based global centroid distance, and a 3D multi-metric that additionally incorporates a z-normalized score. Unlike conventional 1D Gaussian Mixture Models applied to a single scalar metric, our framework performs multi-metric clustering on the feature space to adaptively partition samples into clean-dominant and noise-dominant components without requiring manual thresholds or noise priors. Experiments on CIFAR-10, MNIST, and ImageNet-100 with 5% to 40% symmetric label noise show high recall across settings, including near-perfect recall (>=98%) on ImageNet-100 at 40% noise. Subsequent training yields accuracy gains across evaluated settings, especially under severe corruption on ImageNet-100. These findings suggest that multi-metric integration provides a threshold-free, practical, and low-tuning strategy for noisy label detection.

中文摘要翻译: 深度神经网络（DNNs）在大规模标注数据集上表现优异。然而在实际应用中，标签常因歧义、人为错误或动态环境而被污染。过参数化的DNNs在训练过程中容易记忆这些噪声标签，从而降低模型精度和泛化能力。现有数据清洗和样本选择策略通常依赖人工设定阈值、噪声比例的先验知识或单一度量指标（学习动态或几何结构），在复杂数据场景下不稳定。本文提出一种自适应的数据清洗框架，融合局部、全局和学习动态线索以实现鲁棒的噪声标签检测。通过模块化特征拼接范式，样本被映射到统一的低维特征空间。我们提供了两种实例化方法：一个二维度量整合了类自适应KNN局部不一致性和K-means全局质心距离；一个三维多度量额外引入了z归一化得分。与传统的对单一标量度量应用一维高斯混合模型不同，我们的框架在特征空间上执行多度量聚类，自适应地将样本划分为干净主导和噪声主导两部分，无需人工阈值或噪声先验。在CIFAR-10、MNIST和ImageNet-100上，5%到40%对称标签噪声的实验结果表明，该框架在各设定下均取得高召回率，其中在ImageNet-100的40%噪声下实现了接近完美的召回率（≥98%）。后续训练在各项评估设定下均取得精度提升，尤其在ImageNet-100严重噪声条件下效果显著。这些发现表明，多度量融合为噪声标签检测提供了一种阈值无关、实用且低调参的策略。

</details>


### [54] [Detecting Temporally Localized Manipulations in Authentic Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07090)
*Okan Umur,Ali Emre Güşlü,Ibrahim Delibasoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有深度伪造检测数据集缺乏对“真实视频中插入短暂伪造片段”这一场景的建模，为此构建了一个专门用于检测短时、高逼真局部视频操控的新数据集，并基于DINOv3特征评估了两种检测方法，建立了初步基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集主要针对整体伪造、对象移除或视频修复，未能有效覆盖“真实视频中插入一段短时高逼真操控片段、且原视频随后继续播放”的场景，因此需要专门的数据集和基准来推动该方向的研究。

Method: 使用DINOv3特征，评估两种互补方法：(1) 线性探针方法，结合三种不同阈值策略进行帧级分类；(2) 基于连续帧相似度的方法，利用DINOv3特征检测时域操控边界。

Result: 两种方法在自建测试集上建立了部分操控视频检测的初步基准，实验表明需要内容自适应的阈值化机制来提升检测效果。

Conclusion: 现有数据集存在盲区，本文构建的新数据集和基准实验揭示了局部短时操控检测的挑战，并指出内容自适应阈值化是未来改进的关键方向。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of video editing and generative artificial intelligence technologies has made realistic video manipulation increasingly accessible. Although existing datasets have significantly advanced research in deepfake detection, object removal, and video inpainting, they do not adequately model scenarios in which a short manipulated segment is inserted into an otherwise authentic video and the original video continues afterward. In this study, we review representative datasets from the literature, analyze their characteristics, and discuss their limitations with respect to temporally localized realistic manipulation detection. Based on this analysis, we motivate the need for a new dataset specifically designed for authentic videos containing short and highly realistic manipulated intervals. Finally, we evaluate two complementary approaches on our custom-curated test set to establish an initial benchmark for this challenging scenario. The first employs a linear probe on DINOv3 features, assessed under three thresholding strategies. The second leverages DINOv3 features with a consecutive frame similarity-based method to detect temporal manipulation boundaries. Together, these experiments provide an initial benchmark for partially manipulated video detection and highlight the need for content-adaptive thresholding mechanisms. The dataset, code, and supplementary materials are publicly available at https://github.com/OkanUmur/temporally-localized-video-manipulation-detection.

中文摘要翻译: 视频编辑和生成式人工智能技术的快速发展使得逼真的视频操控越来越容易实现。尽管现有数据集显著推动了深度伪造检测、对象移除和视频修复的研究，但它们未能充分建模以下场景：在原本真实的视频中插入一段短时操控片段，且原视频后续内容继续播放。在本研究中，我们回顾了文献中的代表性数据集，分析了它们的特征，并讨论了它们在时域局部化真实操控检测方面的局限性。基于这一分析，我们论证了构建一个新数据集的必要性，该数据集专门针对包含短时且高度逼真操控片段的真实视频。最后，我们在自建测试集上评估了两种互补方法，为这一具有挑战性的场景建立了初步基准。第一种方法采用DINOv3特征的线性探针，在三种阈值化策略下进行评估。第二种方法基于DINOv3特征与连续帧相似度来检测时域操控边界。这些实验共同为部分操控视频检测提供了初步基准，并凸显了内容自适应阈值化机制的必要性。数据集、代码和补充材料已公开发布于 https://github.com/OkanUmur/temporally-localized-video-manipulation-detection。

</details>


### [55] [LARA: Latent Action Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07100)
*Mengya Liu,Baoxiong Jia,Jiangyong Huang,Jingze Zhang,Siyuan Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LARA框架，通过表征对齐联合优化潜在动作模型（LAM）和视觉-语言动作模型（VLA），使得LAM获得动作轨迹监督、VLA获得前向动力学正则化，在仿真和真实机器人操控任务上分别提升约10%、5%和15%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖大规模高质量真实机器人数据，但真实数据稀缺；LAM虽能从无标签人类视频中学到潜在动作表征辅助VLA，但LAM与VLA分开训练，导致LAM在VLA训练时缺乏接地（ungrounded），VLA模型被冻结的LAM表征所限制，二者无法相互增益。

Method: 提出LARA（潜在动作表征对齐）框架，这是一种即插即用的方法，通过表征对齐联合优化LAM和VLA。具体地，LAM在动作轨迹的监督下学习以避免虚假视觉变化，VLA通过LAM学习的前向动力学进行正则化以减少功能无效轨迹的幻觉。

Result: 在3个仿真基准和一个精心设计的真实世界机器人操控基准上，LARA在预训练、预训练VLA模型的后训练增强以及LAM精调三个场景下，分别平均提升约10%、5%和15%。

Conclusion: LARA框架通过联合优化LAM和VLA实现了二者的互惠增益，有效缓解了VLA训练数据稀缺问题，并在多个机器人操控任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Visual-language action (VLA) models enable robots to predict actions directly from observations and language instructions, but their performance depends on large-scale, high-quality data and is limited by the scarcity of real-world robot action datasets. To facilitate VLA model learning with abundant unlabeled human videos, Latent Action Models (LAM) learn latent action representations from visual dynamics to provide additional supervision for VLA learning. However, LAM and VLA are typically trained separately, leaving LAM ungrounded during VLA training and VLA models constrained by frozen LAM representations. To address these issues, we propose Latent Action Representation Alignment (LARA), a plug-and-play framework that jointly optimizes LAM and VLA via representation alignment. This enables reciprocal benefits where LAMs learn with action trajectories to avoid spurious visual changes, while VLAs are regularized by forward dynamics learned within LAMs to reduce hallucinations of functionally ineffective trajectories. We demonstrate LARA versatility and effectiveness for pre-training, post-training enhancement of pre-trained VLA models, and LAM refinement, achieving an average of ~10%, ~5%, and ~15% improvement over 3 simulation and 1 meticulously designed real-world robotic manipulation benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言动作（VLA）模型使机器人能够直接从观测和语言指令预测动作，但其性能依赖于大规模高质量数据，并受到真实世界机器人动作数据集稀缺的限制。为利用丰富的无标签人类视频促进VLA模型学习，潜在动作模型（LAM）从视觉动态中学习潜在动作表征，为VLA学习提供额外监督。然而，LAM和VLA通常分开训练，导致LAM在VLA训练过程中缺乏接地（ungrounded），而VLA模型受限于冻结的LAM表征。为解决这些问题，我们提出潜在动作表征对齐（LARA），一种即插即用的框架，通过表征对齐联合优化LAM和VLA。这实现了互惠增益：LAM通过动作轨迹学习以避免虚假视觉变化，而VLA通过LAM学习的前向动力学进行正则化，减少功能无效轨迹的幻觉。我们展示了LARA在预训练、预训练VLA模型的后训练增强以及LAM精调方面的通用性和有效性，在3个仿真基准和1个精心设计的真实世界机器人操控基准上，分别平均提升约10%、5%和15%。

</details>


### [56] [GP-Adapter: Gaussian Process CLIP-Adapter for Few-Shot Out-of-Distribution Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07102)
*Taisei Saito,Koretaka Ogata,Takafumi Hiroi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GP-Adapter 是一种无需训练的框架，通过在冻结的 CLIP 嵌入上构建基于高斯过程的类别级单分类器，实现少样本分类和分布外检测，无需微调主干网络，内存开销为 O(CK²)。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CLIP 在零样本识别上表现优异，但其确定的相似度分数缺乏不确定性信息，在分布偏移和数据稀缺场景下可靠性不足。现有方法需要微调或依赖确定性特征，无法有效建模不确定性。

Method: GP-Adapter 在冻结的 CLIP 图像和文本嵌入上，分别为每个类别构建图像特征（RBF核）和文本提示（线性核）的单类高斯过程，融合两者的预测统计量（均值与方差）生成方差感知的置信度分数，用于分布外检测。无需训练 CLIP 主干，仅需少量 K-shot 缓存和轻量超参数选择。

Result: 在 ImageNet 和多个 OOD 基准上，GP-Adapter 在少样本分类上具有竞争力，并在与提示学习基线结合时持续提升 OOD 检测性能，验证了高斯过程不确定性与提示学习的互补性。

Conclusion: 将概率推理（高斯过程）与大规模预训练视觉-语言模型（CLIP）相结合，能够在低数据和分布偏移场景下提升模型的可靠性。

Original Abstract: We propose GP-Adapter, a training-free framework that augments CLIP (Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training) with Gaussian Process (GP) uncertainty modeling for few-shot classification and out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. While CLIP achieves strong zero-shot recognition, it yields deterministic similarity scores and offers limited uncertainty information, which is critical under distribution shift and data scarcity. GP-Adapter constructs modality-specific, class-wise one-class GPs on top of frozen CLIP embeddings using an RBF kernel for image features and a linear kernel for text prompts and fuses their predictive statistics to produce a variance-aware confidence score for OOD detection. The method requires no fine-tuning of the CLIP backbone and relies only on a small $K$-shot cache and lightweight hyperparameter selection, with memory cost scaling as $O(CK^2)$ for $C$ classes and $K$ shots. Experiments on ImageNet and multiple OOD benchmarks show that GP-Adapter provides competitive few-shot performance and consistently improves OOD detection when combined with prompt-learning baselines, highlighting the complementarity between GP-based uncertainty modeling and prompt learning. Overall, our results suggest that integrating probabilistic inference with large pre-trained vision-language models can improve reliability in low-data and distribution-shifted settings. Code is available at https://github.com/tms-byte/GP-Adapter

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出 GP-Adapter，一个无需训练的框架，通过高斯过程不确定性建模增强 CLIP（对比语言-图像预训练），用于少样本分类和分布外检测。尽管 CLIP 在零样本识别上表现强劲，但其输出确定的相似度分数，提供的有限不确定性信息在分布偏移和数据稀缺场景下至关重要。GP-Adapter 在冻结的 CLIP 嵌入之上，为图像特征使用 RBF 核、为文本提示使用线性核，构建模态特定、类别级的单类高斯过程，并融合它们的预测统计量以生成方差感知的置信度分数用于分布外检测。该方法无需微调 CLIP 主干网络，仅依赖少量 K-shot 缓存和轻量超参数选择，内存开销为 O(CK²)，其中 C 为类别数，K 为样本数。在 ImageNet 和多个 OOD 基准上的实验表明，GP-Adapter 提供了有竞争力的少样本性能，并在与提示学习基线结合时持续提升分布外检测能力，凸显了基于高斯过程的不确定性建模与提示学习之间的互补性。总体而言，我们的结果表明，将概率推理与大规模预训练视觉-语言模型相结合，可以提升在低数据和分布偏移场景下的可靠性。代码开源在 https://github.com/tms-byte/GP-Adapter。

</details>


### [57] [3DMorph: Single-Image-Guided Local 3D Shape Editing and Morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07115)
*Tobias Preintner,Yunfei Deng,Phillip Müller,Sebastian Illing,Adrian König,Thomas Bäck,Elena Raponi,Niki van Stein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3DMorph，一种无需训练的框架，利用单张编辑图像实现局部3D形状编辑与变形，同时引入Delta3D基准用于评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D形状编辑方法多为全局性、领域特定、需要复杂用户交互或仅关注外观而非几何形状，缺乏简单有效的局部几何编辑方法。

Method: 3DMorph是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，通过单张图像引导，自动定位相关3D区域并将2D修改迁移到3D，同时保持未修改区域不变，还支持生成原始与编辑对象之间的中间形状。

Result: 实验结果表明3DMorph能够将直观的2D编辑有效转换为3D编辑，在图像引导的局部3D编辑任务上优于现有的最先进生成和编辑方法。

Conclusion: 3DMorph为3D形状的局部几何编辑提供了一种简单、无需训练且有效的解决方案，并可通过Delta3D基准进行标准化评估。

Original Abstract: Despite recent progress in 3D generation, intuitive editing of existing shapes remains limited. Unlike images, which benefit from well-established inpainting tools, general 3D objects such as meshes still lack simple and effective methods for local shape editing. Existing approaches are often global, domain-specific, require complex user interaction, or focus on appearance (color and texture) rather than geometry. We introduce 3DMorph, a training-free framework for single-image-guided local 3D shape editing and morphing. Given an edited image showing a desired shape modification, our method automatically localizes the relevant 3D region and transfers 2D modifications to 3D while preserving unmodified areas. 3DMorph also enables intermediate shape generation between the original and edited objects, facilitating design exploration. To benchmark editing quality, we introduce Delta3D, an image-guided local 3D editing benchmark with paired ground-truth edits. Experimental results show that 3DMorph translates intuitive 2D edits into 3D, outperforming state-of-the-art generative and editing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管3D生成领域取得了最新进展，但对现有形状进行直观编辑的能力仍然有限。与受益于成熟修复工具的图像不同，网格等通用3D对象仍然缺乏简单有效的局部形状编辑方法。现有方法通常是全局性的、领域特定的、需要复杂的用户交互，或仅关注外观（颜色和纹理）而非几何形状。我们提出了3DMorph，一种无需训练的框架，用于单图像引导的局部3D形状编辑与变形。给定一张显示期望形状修改的编辑图像，我们的方法自动定位相关3D区域，并将2D修改迁移到3D，同时保持未修改区域不变。3DMorph还能在原始对象与编辑对象之间生成中间形状，便于设计探索。为了对编辑质量进行基准测试，我们引入了Delta3D，一个带有成对真实编辑标注的图像引导局部3D编辑基准。实验结果表明，3DMorph能够将直观的2D编辑转化为3D编辑，性能优于当前最先进的生成和编辑方法。

</details>


### [58] [Native3D: End-to-End 3D Scene Generation via Unified Mesh-Texture Modeling and Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07117)
*Yibo Liu,Ziwei Zhang,Haozhou Pang,Menghao Li,Lanshan He,Gan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Native3D是首个完全绕过2D中间表示的端到端3D场景生成框架，通过统一网格-纹理联合表示和3D表示对齐损失，在生成质量和编辑灵活性上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法需要将3D表示适配到2D域以利用预训练扩散模型，这不可避免地引入了几何结构畸变和纹理细节退化等域适应问题。为此，本文提出直接进行端到端3D场景生成，绕过2D中间表示。

Method: (1) 设计统一网格-纹理联合表示，通过基于Transformer的场景编码器同时建模几何结构和纹理特征，保持场景内物体的空间关系和视觉一致性；(2) 提出3D表示对齐损失（3D REPA Loss），采用改进的对比学习机制在潜在空间中对齐多层级语义表示，增强几何和纹理保真度。

Result: 实验结果表明，Native3D在生成质量和编辑灵活性两方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Native3D为3D场景编辑提供了一种新颖的解决方案，通过端到端生成框架有效解决了域适应问题。

Original Abstract: This paper presents Native3D, the first end-to-end 3D scene generation framework that completely bypasses 2D intermediate representations. Traditional approaches typically require adapting 3D representations to the 2D domain to leverage pre-trained diffusion models, which inevitably introduces domain adaptation issues including geometric structural distortion and texture detail degradation. To address these limitations, we design a unified mesh-texture joint representation that simultaneously models both geometric structures and texture features through a Transformer-based scene encoder, effectively maintaining spatial relationships and visual consistency among objects within scenes. We further propose the 3D Representation Alignment Loss (3D REPA Loss), which employs an improved contrastive learning mechanism to align multi-level semantic representations in the latent space, significantly enhancing geometric and textural fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate that Native3D outperforms existing methods in both generation quality and editing flexibility, providing a novel solution for 3D scene editing.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了Native3D，这是首个完全绕过2D中间表示的端到端3D场景生成框架。传统方法通常需要将3D表示适配到2D域以利用预训练扩散模型，这不可避免地引入了域适应问题，包括几何结构畸变和纹理细节退化。为了解决这些局限性，我们设计了一种统一的网格-纹理联合表示，通过基于Transformer的场景编码器同时建模几何结构和纹理特征，有效保持了场景内物体之间的空间关系和视觉一致性。我们进一步提出了3D表示对齐损失（3D REPA Loss），采用改进的对比学习机制在潜在空间中对齐多层级语义表示，显著增强了几何和纹理保真度。实验结果表明，Native3D在生成质量和编辑灵活性方面均优于现有方法，为3D场景编辑提供了一种新颖的解决方案。

</details>


### [59] [Consistent-Inversion: Reverse Consistency Guidance for Structure-Preserving Visual Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07145)
*Xiaocheng Lu,Jingcai Guo,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Consistent-Inversion是一种无需训练的逆向一致性引导框架，通过在目标轨迹与源反转轨迹之间施加逆向一致性约束，在保持背景结构的同时实现精准的图像编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有免训练编辑方法依赖反转耦合，导致源重构与目标编辑之间的轨迹不匹配，要么破坏背景/布局细节，要么过度约束目标编辑效果。

Method: 构建辅助目标侧噪声表示，执行源引导的逆向去噪，计算逆向一致性差异作为校正信号，对选定的早期目标去噪步骤进行修正，不更新模型参数，与反转类编辑器兼容。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上，Consistent-Inversion在统一的SD3.5协议下提升了背景和结构保真度，同时保持目标提示对齐；兼容性实验验证了该方法在经典Stable-Diffusion反转管道上的有效性。

Conclusion: 逆向一致性引导能够在无需训练和模型参数更新的前提下，有效解耦源重构与目标编辑，以极小推理开销实现结构保持的视觉编辑。

Original Abstract: Text-guided diffusion models have become effective tools for real-image visual editing, where the edited image must follow a target instruction while preserving editing-irrelevant structure. Most training-free editors rely on inversion: a source image is mapped to a noisy latent trajectory and the terminal latent is reused for target-prompt denoising. This reuse is useful for preservation, but it also couples source reconstruction and target editing. The resulting trajectory mismatch may either damage background/layout details or over-constrain the intended edit. This paper presents Consistent-Inversion, a training-free reverse consistency guidance framework for structure-preserving visual editing. Instead of treating the inverted source latent as a fixed initialization, Consistent-Inversion checks whether an intermediate target trajectory can be reversed toward the source inversion trajectory under the source prompt. To make this check well-defined, we construct an auxiliary target-side noise representation, perform source-guided reverse denoising, and use the resulting reverse consistency discrepancy as a correction signal for selected early target denoising steps. The method does not update model parameters, is compatible with inversion-based editors, and introduces only a small inference overhead when applied sparsely. Experiments on PIE-Bench show that Consistent-Inversion improves background and structural fidelity under a unified SD3.5 protocol while maintaining target-prompt alignment, and compatibility experiments further verify the same correction principle on classical Stable-Diffusion inversion pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 文本引导的扩散模型已成为真实图像视觉编辑的有效工具，编辑后的图像必须遵循目标指令，同时保留与编辑无关的结构。大多数免训练编辑器依赖于反转：源图像被映射到一条噪声潜变量轨迹，终端潜变量被复用于目标提示去噪。这种复用有助于保持结构，但也耦合了源重构与目标编辑。由此产生的轨迹不匹配要么损坏背景/布局细节，要么过度约束预期的编辑效果。本文提出Consistent-Inversion，一种无需训练的逆向一致性引导框架，用于保持结构的视觉编辑。Consistent-Inversion不将反转后的源潜变量视为固定的初始值，而是检查中间目标轨迹是否能在源提示下反向回到源反转轨迹。为了使这一检查具有良好定义，我们构建了一个辅助的目标侧噪声表示，执行源引导的逆向去噪，并将由此产生的逆向一致性差异作为选定的早期目标去噪步骤的校正信号。该方法不更新模型参数，与基于反转的编辑器兼容，且在稀疏应用时仅引入极小的推理开销。在PIE-Bench上的实验表明，Consistent-Inversion在统一的SD3.5协议下提升了背景和结构保真度，同时保持目标提示对齐；兼容性实验进一步验证了相同校正原则在经典Stable-Diffusion反转管道上的有效性。

</details>


### [60] [TraRA: Trajectory-level Recognition Aggregation for Video Text Spotting in Urban Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07161)
*Duc Tri Tran,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Vijay John,Phi Le Nguyen,Yasutomo Kawanishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraRA是一种即插即用的轨迹级文本识别聚合方法，通过时间聚类和视觉-语言聚合模块，有效提升视频文本检测与识别在监控场景下的鲁棒性，在多个基准上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频文本识别方法通常对每一帧独立进行识别，忽略了帧间时序信息，导致在动态监控场景（运动模糊、遮挡、尺度变化）下识别结果不一致且不准确，亟需一种能够利用时序和多模态一致性来提升识别鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出TraRA，包含两个核心模块：(1) 时间聚类模块——通过对时序和视觉上一致的文本实例进行聚类，精炼含噪的轨迹；(2) 视觉-语言聚合模块——采用LoRA增强的视觉-语言模型，跨帧融合视觉线索与语言上下文，在整条文本轨迹上聚合信息实现鲁棒识别。

Result: 在RoadText、BOVText、ArTVideo和ICDAR15四个公开基准上的大量实验表明，TraRA在跟踪和识别性能上一致地提升了现有最先进视频文本识别方法的表现。

Conclusion: TraRA通过轨迹级的信息聚合和跨模态融合，有效解决了监控场景下视频文本识别的挑战，是一种即插即用且性能优越的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Video Text Spotting (VTS) is essential for urban surveillance and intelligent transportation systems, enabling automated reading of street signs, vehicle markings, and scene text in video streams. However, reliable recognition remains challenging due to dynamic video factors common in surveillance scenarios, including motion blur, occlusion, and scale variation, which degrade frame-level recognition. Existing VTS methods typically perform recognition independently on each frame, leading to inconsistent and inaccurate results across sequences. To address these limitations, we propose TraRA (Trajectory-level Recognition Aggregation for VTS), a plug-and-play method that performs trajectory-level text recognition by leveraging temporal and multimodal consistency. TraRA integrates two key modules: (1) the Temporal Clustering and (2) the Vision-Language Aggregation.
