In this work, we formally investigate how structural modularity supports functional compositionality in inhibition-dominated threshold-linear networks (TLNs). We introduce a novel class of modular network assembly called low-rank gluings, where component subnetworks with arbitrary internal connectivity are connected via specific low-rank couplings. We prove that the global fixed points of these networks are constrained to be combinations of the local fixed points of their constituent modules. For a more structured subclass, called rank-1 gluings, we provide a complete characterization that determines which combinations of local fixed points yield global ones. We apply these results to graph-based networks, extending fixed point decomposition rules from combinatorial threshold-linear networks (CTLNs) to the more flexible family of generalized CTLNs (gCTLNs), thereby proving that these structural rules are more robust than initially posited. Finally, we demonstrate that these gluing rules provide a mathematically tractable recipe for engineering compositional dynamics, enabling the construction of networks with a combinatorially large repertoire of predictable attractors that can be understood from simpler component motifs, ranging from compositions of fixed points to compositional limit cycles.

中文摘要翻译: 大脑在相对稳定的结构和有限资源下，常规性地产生高度灵活和复杂的行为。支撑这一能力的一个关键机制是组合性（compositionality），它使大脑能够高效地将复杂任务分解为更简单、可重复使用的基元。尽管网络模块性常被与生物和人工网络中的组合性联系起来，但在非线性网络中这种关系的严格数学刻画仍属空白。本文正式研究了抑制主导的阈值线性网络（TLNs）中结构模块性如何支持功能组合性。我们引入了一类新颖的模块化网络组装方法，称为低秩粘合（low-rank gluings），其中具有任意内部连接性的组件子网络通过特定的低秩耦合相连。我们证明了这些网络的全局不动点被约束为其组成模块的局部不动点的组合。对于一个更具结构性的子类——秩-1粘合（rank-1 gluings），我们提供了完整的特征刻画，用以确定哪些局部不动点的组合能产生全局不动点。我们将这些结果应用于基于图的网络，将不动点分解规则从组合阈值线性网络（CTLNs）扩展到更灵活的广义CTLNs（gCTLNs），从而证明这些结构规则比最初假设的更为鲁棒。最后，我们展示了这些粘合规则提供了一种数学上可处理的工程化组合动力学配方，使得能够构建具有组合数量庞大且可预测吸引子的网络——这些吸引子可以从更简单的组件基序理解，范围从不动点的组合到组合极限环。

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Elmes*: Automated Construction of Fine-Grained Evaluation Rubrics for Large Language Models in Long-Tail Educational Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06546)
*Tao Liu,Ye Lu,Ruohua Zhang,Siyu Song,Wentao Liu,Aimin Zhou,Hao Hao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Elmes*框架，用于构建教育场景下大语言模型的细粒度评估标准，并构建了包含330个场景的Edu-330基准，揭示了LLM教育能力的多维性及评估中的偏见。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有教育领域LLM评估基准要么侧重通用正确性，要么依赖人工设计的评估标准，难以扩展到长尾教学场景，缺乏可规模化且具备教学法基础的评估框架。

Method: 提出Elmes*框架，包含声明式多智能体引擎（教师-学生-评委交互）和SceneGen自演化模块，后者从专家定义的教学维度协同优化评估标准和测试数据，构建了覆盖11个学科、3个年级段、10种任务类型的Edu-330基准。

Result: 教育能力是多维的：顶级LLM主要在创造力和价值观整合上有差异；知识强的模型可能在苏格拉底式引导上失败；教育专用模型InnoSpark获得最高人工评分；LLM评委能保持与人类可比的排名但评分方差更低，存在自我偏好等偏见；专家打分的少样本锚定能改善人-LLM对齐。

Conclusion: Elmes*为教学导向的LLM评估提供了可扩展的诊断基础设施。

Original Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) for education requires measuring how models teach, not only what they know. Existing benchmarks emphasize domain-general correctness or depend on manually designed rubrics that scale poorly to long-tail pedagogical scenarios. We introduce Elmes*, an end-to-end framework for constructing, refining, and applying fine-grained scenario-specific rubrics. Elmes* combines a declarative multi-agent engine for teacher--student--judge interactions with SceneGen, a self-evolving module that co-optimizes evaluation criteria and test data from expert-defined pedagogical dimensions. Using Elmes*, we build Edu-330, covering 330 scenarios across 11 subjects, 3 grade bands, and 10 task types, with over 1{,}000 second-level indicators. Experiments on Edu-330 and four expert-authored gold-standard scenarios show that educational capability is multidimensional: top-tier LLMs differ mainly in creativity and values integration, knowledge-strong models may fail at Socratic scaffolding, and the education-specialized InnoSpark achieves the best human-evaluated average score. LLM judges preserve human-comparable rankings with much lower scoring variance, but exhibit judge-specific biases such as self-preference. Ablations show that expert-scored few-shot anchoring improves human--LLM alignment, while reasoning enforcement and greedy decoding are model-dependent. Elmes* thus provides scalable diagnostic infrastructure for pedagogically grounded LLM evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 评估用于教育的大语言模型（LLM）需要测量模型的"教学能力"，而不仅仅是其知识水平。现有基准侧重于领域通用正确性，或依赖于人工设计的评估标准，这些方法难以扩展到长尾教学场景。我们提出Elmes*，一个用于构建、优化和应用细粒度场景特定评估标准的端到端框架。Elmes*结合了声明式多智能体引擎（驱动教师-学生-评委交互）与SceneGen自演化模块，后者从专家定义的教学维度协同优化评估标准和测试数据。利用Elmes*，我们构建了Edu-330基准，覆盖11个学科、3个年级段和10种任务类型的330个场景，包含超过1000个二级指标。在Edu-330和四个专家编写的黄金标准场景上的实验表明，教育能力是多维的：顶级LLM主要在创造力和价值观整合上存在差异；知识储备强的模型可能在苏格拉底式引导上失败；教育专用模型InnoSpark取得了最佳人工评估平均得分。LLM评委能保持与人类可比的排名，同时具有更低的评分方差，但表现出评委特定的偏见（如自我偏好）。消融实验表明，专家打分的少样本锚定可改善人-LLM对齐，而推理强制和贪心解码的效果则依赖于具体模型。Elmes*为教学导向的LLM评估提供了可扩展的诊断基础设施。

</details>


### [93] [FAIR-Calib: Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration for Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06547)
*Haoyu Huang,Linlin Yang,Sheng Xu,Boyu Liu,Guodong Guo,Zhongqian Fu,Hang Zhou,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FAIR-Calib，一种面向扩散大语言模型(dLLM)的后训练量化(PTQ)框架，通过两阶段校准优先保护易翻转的边界决策状态，显著降低量化误差导致的决策翻转与提交后不匹配问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLM)在迭代精炼token时采用不可逆提交机制，导致早期脆弱决策存在"稳定性滞后"问题。后训练量化(PTQ)误差容易在写入边界翻转这些临界决策，且一旦被锁定就会永久放大，严重影响模型性能。

Method: 两阶段PTQ框架：阶段I，利用全精度教师模型估计位置先验，结合边界命中(frontier hits)与掩码阶段可靠性；阶段II，执行离策略(off-policy)、逐层校准，通过最小化重加权的隐藏状态MSE，优先保护脆弱边界状态，无需昂贵的端到端扩散 rollout。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型上(W4A4量化)一致优于现有基线方法，显著减少了边界决策翻转，并在多个基准上抑制了提交后不匹配问题。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了所提出的加权目标是输出KL散度的一个有效代理；实验表明FAIR-Calib能够有效保护dLLM中脆弱的边界状态，提升量化后模型的稳定性与性能。

Original Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) refine tokens iteratively but commit them irreversibly, leading to a "stability lag" where early decisions remain fragile even after being written. We reveal that Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) error easily flips these borderline decisions at the write frontier, which are then permanently locked in and amplified. To address this, we propose Frontier-Aware Instability-Reweighted Calibration (FAIR-Calib), a two-stage PTQ framework for dLLMs. Stage I probes a full-precision teacher to estimate a position prior that combines frontier hits and masked-stage reliability. Stage II performs off-policy, layer-wise calibration by minimizing a reweighted hidden-state MSE, effectively prioritizing the protection of fragile frontier states without requiring expensive end-to-end diffusion rollouts. We further theoretically justify our weighted objective as a surrogate for output KL divergence. Empirically, FAIR-Calib consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on LLaDA and Dream (W4A4), significantly reducing frontier decision flips and suppressing post-commit mismatches across diverse benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 扩散大语言模型(dLLM)通过迭代方式精炼token，但其提交(commit)机制是不可逆的，这导致了"稳定性滞后"问题——即使早期决策已被写入，它们仍然十分脆弱。我们发现，后训练量化(PTQ)误差很容易在写入边界处翻转这些临界决策，而这些被翻转的决策随后会被永久锁定并放大。为解决这一问题，我们提出了FAIR-Calib（边界感知的不稳定性重加权校准），一种面向dLLM的两阶段PTQ框架。阶段I通过探测一个全精度教师模型来估计一个位置先验，该先验结合了边界命中(frontier hits)和掩码阶段可靠性。阶段II执行离策略(off-policy)的逐层校准，通过最小化重加权的隐藏状态MSE，优先保护脆弱的边界状态，且无需昂贵的端到端扩散rollout。我们进一步在理论上证明，所提出的加权目标是输出KL散度的一个有效代理。实验结果表明，FAIR-Calib在LLaDA和Dream模型上（W4A4量化）一致优于现有的最先进基线方法，显著减少了边界决策翻转，并在多个基准上抑制了提交后的不匹配问题。

</details>


### [94] [The Identity Trap in EEG Foundation Models: A Diagnostic Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06647)
*Jun-You Lin,Ying Choon Wu,Tzyy-Ping Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了EEG基础模型中的一个普遍问题——“身份陷阱”，即模型在subject-disjoint交叉验证中的高准确率可能源自个体身份特征而非真正的临床生物标志。作者提出FMScope诊断框架，通过五种表征级诊断方法（方差分解、主体轴擦除、无周期1/f消融、逐层标签探测、组内方向一致性）识别该问题，并在四个数据集和三个预训练模型上验证了其普遍性和可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型在subject-disjoint交叉验证下报告的高准确率具有歧义性：它可能反映真实的临床生物标志，也可能反映与标签相关的个体身份特征。作者希望在被微调前的表征层面诊断这一问题，区分模型学到的究竟是生物标志还是个体身份。

Method: 提出FMScope协议，包含五种诊断手段：(1) 方差分解，量化主体间方差占比；(2) 主体轴擦除，去除表征中的主体身份线性子空间；(3) 无周期1/f消融，移除EEG中的无周期成分；(4) 逐层标签探测，分析各层特征与标签/身份的关系；(5) 组内方向一致性，衡量同类样本表征方向的一致性。采用2×2研究设计（标签与主体的关系 × 是否存在共识的跨主体EEG标志）在三个预训练FM（LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE）和四个数据集上进行验证。

Result: (1) 身份陷阱具有普遍性：12/12个模型-数据集对中，冻结表征的主体方差超出随机基线13-89倍，微调后进一步增加（+10到+63 pp）；(2) 擦除主体身份线性轴后，在标签随主体变化的场景下标签解码提升6-12个百分点，外部队列提升4-27个百分点；(3) 无周期1/f是主体身份的重要载体：移除后LaBraM和CBraMod的主体探测准确率下降9-19 pp，而REVE的身份信息已饱和且不依赖无周期成分；(4) 微调仅在具有文献支持的跨主体标志的场景中增强标签相关方差。

Conclusion: 身份陷阱是基于生理的捷径学习实例，其偏好线索具有可测量的生理成分，仅靠subject-disjoint分割无法排除它。FMScope能够将反映真实生物标志的增益与反映个体身份的增益分离开来，为EEG基础模型的可靠评估提供了重要工具。

Original Abstract: Objective. EEG foundation models (FMs) report strong accuracy on clinical resting-state EEG. However, high accuracy under subject-disjoint cross-validation remains ambiguous: it can reflect a genuine clinical biomarker, or subject-identity features that correlate with the label. We name this the Identity Trap and ask whether it can be diagnosed at the representation level before fine-tuning.
  Approach. We propose FMScope, a frozen-representation protocol packaging five diagnostics: variance decomposition, subject-axis erasure, aperiodic 1/f ablation, layer-wise label probing, and within-subject direction consistency. We apply it to three pretrained FMs (LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE) across four datasets in a 2x2 layout: subject relation of label x presence of a consensus cross-subject EEG marker.
  Main results. (i) The Identity Trap is universal: frozen subject-variance is 13-89x a random null in 12/12 pairs, rising in all 12 under fine-tuning (+10 to +63 pp). This dominance is a removable linear axis: erasing it improves label decoding where the label varies within subject (+6 to +12 pp in primary cells; +4 to +27 pp across external cohorts). (ii) Aperiodic 1/f is one subject carrier: removing it drops the subject probe by 9-19 pp on LaBraM and CBraMod. REVE saturates subject identity without measurable aperiodic dependence. (iii) Fine-tuning amplifies label-variance only in cells with a literature-established cross-subject marker.
  Significance. The Identity Trap is a physically-grounded instance of shortcut learning: the preferred cue has a measurable physiological component, and subject-disjoint splitting alone cannot rule it out. FMScope separates gains reflecting a biological marker from those reflecting subject identity.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：EEG基础模型在临床静息态EEG上报告了高准确率。然而，subject-disjoint交叉验证下的高准确率仍具有歧义性：它既可以反映真实的临床生物标志，也可以反映与标签相关的个体身份特征。我们将此命名为“身份陷阱”，并探究在微调之前能否在表征层面诊断它。方法：我们提出FMScope，一个冻结表征协议，包含五种诊断手段：方差分解、主体轴擦除、无周期1/f消融、逐层标签探测以及组内方向一致性。我们将该方法应用于三个预训练基础模型（LaBraM, CBraMod, REVE），在四个数据集上进行2×2布局研究：标签与主体的关系 × 是否存在共识的跨主体EEG标志。主要结果：(i) 身份陷阱具有普遍性：在12/12个模型-数据集对中，冻结表征的主体方差超出随机基线13-89倍，且在所有12个案例中微调后进一步上升（+10到+63个百分点）。这种主导性是一个可移除的线性轴：擦除它在标签随主体变化的情况下改善了标签解码（主要单元中提升6-12个百分点；外部队列中提升4-27个百分点）。(ii) 无周期1/f是主体身份的一个载体：移除它使LaBraM和CBraMod上的主体探测准确率下降9-19个百分点。REVE的主体身份饱和，且无可测量的无周期依赖性。(iii) 微调仅在有文献建立的跨主体标志的单元中放大了标签方差。意义：身份陷阱是基于生理的捷径学习实例：其偏好线索具有可测量的生理成分，仅靠subject-disjoint分割无法排除它。FMScope能够将反映生物标志的增益与反映个体身份的增益分离开来。

</details>


### [95] [Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network for Polymer Classification using THz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06554)
*Roshni Mahtani,Ilán Carretero,Laura Monroy,Aldo Moreno-Oyervides,Oscar Elías Bonilla-Manrique,Rocío del Amor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），结合太赫兹双梳光谱技术实现12种聚合物的高精度分类，准确率达85.2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统分选和光谱技术难以实现聚合物的可靠区分，亟需一种快速、高分辨率、无损的聚合物分类方法。

Method: 提出多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），包含特征门控进行信号重标定、多尺度并行卷积捕获不同频率模式、跨特征注意力和注意力池化以突出最具信息量的太赫兹区域。

Result: MSFAN在12种聚合物（含纯聚合物、多层膜、商业混合物和生物聚合物）分类任务中达到85.2%的准确率，优于现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 将太赫兹双梳光谱与深度学习相结合，可实现高效、可扩展且可解释的聚合物分类，在回收塑料质量与安全检测中具有重要潜力。

Original Abstract: Reliable polymer identification is essential for ensuring the quality and safety of recycled plastics, yet conventional sorting and spectroscopic techniques often struggle to deliver robust discrimination. Terahertz Dual-Comb Spectroscopy (THz-DCS) offers a promising alternative, providing rapid, high-resolution, and non-destructive measurements. In this work, we leverage THz-DCS to classify 12 types of polymers, including pure polymers, multilayer films, commercial blends, and biopolymers. To handle the complexity of these spectral signals, we propose the Multi-Scale Feature Attention Network (MSFAN), a novel deep learning architecture tailored for THz-DCS data. The framework integrates feature gating for signal recalibration and multi-scale parallel convolutions to capture diverse frequency patterns. These features are further refined through cross-feature attention and attention pooling, enabling the model to intrinsically highlight the most informative THz regions. MSFAN consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, reaching a classification accuracy of 85.2%. This study demonstrates the potential of combining THz-DCS with deep learning techniques for effective, scalable, and interpretable polymer classification.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠的聚合物识别对于确保回收塑料的质量和安全至关重要，然而传统分选和光谱技术通常难以实现稳健的区分。太赫兹双梳光谱（THz-DCS）提供了一种有前景的替代方案，可实现快速、高分辨率且无损的测量。在本工作中，我们利用THz-DCS对12种聚合物进行分类，包括纯聚合物、多层膜、商业混合物和生物聚合物。为了处理这些光谱信号的复杂性，我们提出了多尺度特征注意力网络（MSFAN），这是一种针对THz-DCS数据定制的新型深度学习架构。该框架集成了特征门控用于信号重标定，以及多尺度并行卷积以捕获多样的频率模式。这些特征通过跨特征注意力和注意力池化进一步细化，使得模型能够内在突出最具信息量的太赫兹区域。MSFAN持续优于现有最先进模型，达到了85.2%的分类准确率。本研究展示了将THz-DCS与深度学习技术相结合用于高效、可扩展且可解释的聚合物分类的潜力。

</details>


### [96] [MacArena: Benchmarking Computer Use Agents on an Online macOS Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06560)
*Victor Muryn,Maksym Shamrai,Sofiia Mazepa,Yehor Khodysko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MacArena，一个面向 macOS 的 GUI 智能体基准测试，包含 421 个手工验证任务，覆盖 50 个应用。实验表明，现有模型在跨平台任务上表现严重下滑，排名甚至发生反转，揭示当前 GUI 智能体存在任务分布过拟合而非真正跨平台能力的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 GUI 智能体基准测试（如 OSWorld）主要基于 Linux，macOS 严重缺乏评测标准。已有的 macOSWorld 仅覆盖少量第一方应用且任务简单，且运行在 x86 虚拟机上，不兼容 Apple Silicon。作者希望填补这一空白，并验证 macOS 是否构成更具挑战性的 GUI 环境。

Method: 构建 MacArena 基准，包含 421 个手工验证任务，涵盖 50 个应用。任务来源包括：OSWorld 任务的筛选移植、macOSWorld 内容、以及 49 个全新的 macOS 原生任务。所有任务运行在 Apple Silicon 上的 Apple Virtualization 框架中。

Result: 模型在已有基准上的强性能可能反映的是对任务分布的熟悉度而非真正的跨平台 GUI 能力。模型排名在移植任务与 macOS 原生任务之间发生反转，领先模型在 MacArena 子集上的性能落后超过 26%，表明 macOS 对当前 GUI 智能体构成更困难的环境。

Conclusion: macOS 对当前 GUI 智能体来说是一个本质上更难的环境；现有基准评测可能存在任务分布偏差，模型的跨平台泛化能力仍需提升。

Original Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate graphical user interfaces (GUIs) through vision and control primitives, and their capabilities have advanced rapidly, driven in part by standardized online evaluation benchmarks such as OSWorld, which serve both as evaluation tools and as training environments for reinforcement learning. However, macOS remains underserved in this landscape: the only existing benchmark, macOSWorld, covers a narrow slice of first-party applications with simpler tasks, and runs on x86 virtual machines incompatible with Apple Silicon. We introduce MacArena, a benchmark of 421 manually verified tasks spanning 50 applications that combines a curated port of OSWorld tasks, content sourced from macOSWorld, and 49 new macOS-native tasks, all running on Apple's native Virtualization framework on Apple Silicon. We argue that macOS presents distinct GUI challenges beyond what Linux-based benchmarks capture, and our evaluation supports this claim: strong model performance on existing benchmarks can reflect familiarity with task distributions rather than genuine cross-platform GUI competence. Notably, model rankings invert between ported and macOS-native tasks, with a leading model trailing by over 26% on the MacArena subset, suggesting that macOS poses a genuinely harder environment for current GUI agents.

中文摘要翻译: 计算机使用智能体（CUAs）通过视觉和控制原语操作图形用户界面（GUI），其能力在 OSWorld 等标准化在线评测基准的推动下迅速进步——这些基准既作为评测工具，也作为强化学习的训练环境。然而，macOS 在这一领域中仍缺乏足够的支持：现有的唯一基准 macOSWorld 仅覆盖了少数第一方应用中的简单任务，且运行在与 Apple Silicon 不兼容的 x86 虚拟机上。我们提出 MacArena，一个包含 421 个手工验证任务、覆盖 50 个应用的基准测试。它融合了 OSWorld 任务的精选移植、来自 macOSWorld 的内容以及 49 个全新的 macOS 原生任务，所有任务均在 Apple Silicon 上的 Apple 原生虚拟化框架中运行。我们认为 macOS 带来的 GUI 挑战与基于 Linux 的基准测试所捕获的内容截然不同，我们的评测支持这一观点：模型在现有基准上的优异表现可能反映的是对任务分布的熟悉程度，而非真正的跨平台 GUI 能力。值得注意的是，模型排名在移植任务与 macOS 原生任务之间发生反转，领先模型在 MacArena 子集上落后超过 26%，这表明 macOS 对当前 GUI 智能体来说构成一个本质上更困难的环境。

</details>


### [97] [WAV: Multi-Resolution Block Residual Routing for Deep Decoder-Only Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06564)
*Kehan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WAV v1，一种轻量级多分辨率残差路由方法，通过在残差流中引入方向性细节基（相位基和分裂基）来增强深层Transformer的残差路由能力。在48层时显著优于现有方法，验证了方向性残差细节对深度缩放的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Block Attention Residuals方法仅用块级残差汇总（低频总位移）来路由，丢失了方向性结构信息（如注意力与MLP的不平衡、块内早晚期动态差异），限制了深层Transformer的残差路由效果。

Method: WAV v1为每个Transformer块增加两个方向性细节基：相位基（对比注意力和MLP更新）和分裂基（对比早晚期子层更新）。这些基与标准块摘要通过相同的深度方向softmax混合器一起路由，同时采用负细节源初始化和分离的RMS匹配来稳定训练。

Result: 在字符级TinyStories和Text8语言建模任务上，WAV v1在12层时并非一致有益，在24层时具有竞争力，在48层时全面超越所有基线。48层时，相比Block AttnRes，TinyStories验证损失从0.4960降至0.4738，Text8从0.9363降至0.9305，且额外参数可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 方向性残差细节（而非仅块级求和）对于深层Transformer的残差路由缩放至关重要，WAV v1展示了在更深网络中通过多分辨率残差路由获得显著收益的潜力。

Original Abstract: Residual connections are central to training deep Transformers, but standard PreNorm residual streams aggregate sublayer updates with fixed unit weights. Recent Attention Residuals replace this fixed accumulation with content-dependent depth-wise routing, and Block Attention Residuals make the mechanism efficient by routing over block-level residual summaries. However, a single block summary stores only the low-frequency total residual displacement inside a block, discarding directional structure such as attention-vs-MLP imbalance and early-vs-late block dynamics. We propose WAV v1, a lightweight multi-resolution residual routing method for decoder-only Transformers. Instead of representing each block only by its accumulated residual sum, WAV v1 augments every block with two directional detail bases: a phase basis that contrasts attention and MLP updates, and a split basis that contrasts early and late sublayer updates. These bases are routed together with standard block summaries through the same depth-wise softmax mixer, while negative detail-source initialization and detached RMS matching stabilize training. On character-level TinyStories and Text8 language modeling, WAV v1 shows a clear depth-dependent benefit. Although it is not consistently beneficial at 12 layers, it becomes competitive at 24 layers and outperforms all baselines at 48 layers. At 48 layers, WAV v1 reduces validation loss relative to Block AttnRes from 0.4960 to 0.4738 on TinyStories and from 0.9363 to 0.9305 on Text8, with negligible additional parameters. These results suggest that directional residual details, not only block-level sums, are important for scaling residual routing in deeper Transformers.

中文摘要翻译: 残差连接是训练深度Transformer的核心，但标准的PreNorm残差流以固定的单位权重聚合子层更新。最近的Attention Residuals用内容相关的深度方向路由取代了这种固定累加，Block Attention Residuals则通过在块级残差摘要上进行路由来提高效率。然而，单个块摘要仅存储块内低频的总残差位移，丢弃了方向性结构信息，例如注意力与MLP的不平衡以及块内早期与晚期动态差异。我们提出WAV v1，一种用于仅解码器Transformer的轻量级多分辨率残差路由方法。WAV v1并非仅用累加的残差和来表示每个块，而是为每个块增加两个方向性细节基：一个对比注意力和MLP更新的相位基，以及一个对比早期和晚期子层更新的分裂基。这些基通过相同的深度方向softmax混合器与标准块摘要一起进行路由，同时采用负细节源初始化和分离的RMS匹配来稳定训练。在字符级TinyStories和Text8语言建模任务上，WAV v1展现出明显的深度依赖性收益。虽然在12层时并非一致有益，但在24层时变得具有竞争力，并在48层时优于所有基线。在48层时，相对于Block AttnRes，WAV v1在TinyStories上将验证损失从0.4960降至0.4738，在Text8上从0.9363降至0.9305，且额外参数可忽略不计。这些结果表明，方向性残差细节（而非仅块级求和）对于在更深层Transformer中扩展残差路由至关重要。

</details>


### [98] [Are you sure? A Comprehensive and Comprehensible Survey of Uncertainty Quantification in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06567)
*Julia Reuter,Fabricio Olivetti de Franca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统回顾了符号回归（SR）中的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，将其归纳为频率学派、贝叶斯和模型选择三大研究方向，指出UQ在SR中仍未被充分探索，并呼吁进一步研究可靠的UQ方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 符号回归在现实决策中缺乏不确定性量化支持，限制了其应用；UQ能提供模型可靠性信息、避免过拟合并为决策提供依据，但现有文献对此关注不足。

Method: 对符号回归中的不确定性量化方法进行综述，将其系统性地组织为三个研究方向：频率学派方法、贝叶斯方法和模型选择方法。

Result: 梳理了当前SR中UQ研究的三大方向，指出该领域仍处于未充分探索阶段，缺乏成熟的可靠UQ方法。

Conclusion: UQ在SR中至关重要但目前研究不足，亟需进一步研究以开发可靠的符号回归不确定性量化方法。

Original Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is a class of methods that systematically explore the space of mathematical functions to discover models that accurately capture the underlying relationships in a dataset. Despite recent advances in the field, a lack of support for uncertainty quantification (UQ) limits its adoption in real-world decision processes. In regression analysis, UQ provides important information about the model reliability, which can both help to avoid overfitting by accounting for uncertainty in the data, and provide insights for decision-making. This survey is the first to clearly address this issue, with the objective of introducing essential UQ concepts and reviewing the current literature on UQ in SR, which can be broadly organized into three research directions: frequentist, Bayesian, and model selection. Despite its importance, UQ in SR is still underexplored, which motivates further research into reliable UQ methods for SR.

中文摘要翻译: 符号回归是一类系统性探索数学函数空间的方法，旨在发现能够准确捕捉数据集中潜在关系的模型。尽管该领域近年取得了进展，但对不确定性量化的支持不足限制了其在现实决策过程中的应用。在回归分析中，不确定性量化提供了关于模型可靠性的重要信息，既有助于通过考虑数据中的不确定性来避免过拟合，又可为决策提供参考。本文是首篇明确针对该问题的综述，旨在介绍不确定性量化的基本概念，并回顾当前符号回归中不确定性量化的相关文献。这些文献可大致归纳为三个研究方向：频率学派方法、贝叶斯方法和模型选择方法。尽管不确定性量化至关重要，但其在符号回归中仍未被充分探索，这激励了针对符号回归可靠不确定性量化方法的进一步研究。

</details>


### [99] [Generative Models Erode Human Temporal Learning Through Market Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06572)
*Wenjun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前生成式模型（尚未达到通用人工智能水平）通过表面特征模仿人类时间学习（HTL）密集型工作，导致验证成本高于收益，引发“价值崩溃”——人类长期学习投入与低成本AI输出在价格上竞争。通过成本检验框架和跨领域证据（学术出版、法律实务、内容平台、软件安全）展示验证侵蚀四阶段。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示当前生成式模型（低于AGI水平）对知识和文化生产造成的结构性风险，特别是对依赖路径依赖式知识积累的人类学习（HTL）的威胁。

Method: 构建成本检验框架（costly-inspection framework），形式化描述验证侵蚀四阶段，并跨领域（学术出版、法律实务、内容平台、软件安全）提供实证映射。

Result: 跨领域证据显示验证侵蚀呈四阶段递进；模型对齐程度与价值崩溃正交，即更好的对齐反而缩小人机输出差距，使验证更难、竞争压力更大。

Conclusion: 即使单个AI输出质量提升，对齐的成功也会加剧对HTL密集型工作的竞争压力，导致结构性价值崩溃，而这一风险无法通过安全对齐来解决。

Original Abstract: We argue that modern generative models create structural risks for knowledge and cultural production at current, sub-AGI capability levels. We define Human Temporal Learning (HTL) as path-dependent knowledge accumulation through sustained engagement with problems over time. Generative outputs increasingly resemble HTL-intensive work in surface features, so verifying whether a given output reflects genuine human learning grows costly relative to its expected benefit. Once verification loses economic justification, evaluators reward outputs regardless of production mode, and producers who invested years of learning compete on price against outputs that cost almost nothing to generate. We call this pathway value collapse and formalize it through a costly-inspection framework. Cross-domain evidence from academic publishing, legal practice, content platforms, and software security maps onto four stages of verification erosion. Alignment success is orthogonal. Better-aligned models narrow observable gaps between human and AI outputs, making source verification harder and intensifying competitive pressure against HTL-intensive work even when individual AI outputs improve.

中文摘要翻译: 我们认为，在当前的亚通用人工智能能力水平下，现代生成式模型为知识和文化生产带来了结构性风险。我们将人类时间学习（HTL）定义为通过随时间持续投入于问题解决而形成的路径依赖式知识积累。生成式输出在表面特征上日益接近HTL密集型工作的产物，因此验证给定输出是否反映真实人类学习的成本相对于其预期收益不断上升。一旦验证失去经济合理性，评估者将不再区分产出模式而一视同仁地给予回报，投入多年学习时间的人类生产者将被迫与几乎零成本生成的输出进行价格竞争。我们将这一路径称为价值崩溃，并通过一个成本检验框架对其进行形式化。来自学术出版、法律实务、内容平台和软件安全等跨领域的证据映射出了验证侵蚀的四个阶段。模型对齐的成功与这一过程正交。对齐程度更高的模型缩小了人类与AI输出之间的可观察差距，使得来源验证更加困难，并加剧了针对HTL密集型工作的竞争压力——即使单个AI输出的质量有所提升。

</details>


### [100] [Skip a Layer or Loop It? Learning Program-of-Layers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06574)
*Ziyue Li,Yang Li,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型（LLM）中存在一种免训练的灵活动态程序化层执行机制（PoLar），即预训练层可作为模块被跳过或循环执行，为每个输入定制程序。多数输入用更短的程序即可达到相同或更高精度，且原始模型错误预测可通过更少层的替代程序纠正。为此提出轻量级PoLar预测网络，学习动态跳过或重复预训练层，在数学推理基准上一致提升准确性，且收益在分布外评估中依然保持。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统LLM推理按固定深度和顺序非递归执行所有层，但本文发现存在更灵活的潜在执行路径——预训练层可被跳过或循环使用，且不同输入适合不同程序长度与结构。这表明固定深度执行仅捕获了LLM潜在推理能力的一小部分，需要探索更动态、自适应的推理方式。

Method: 提出PoLar框架：（1）将预训练层打包为可跳过或循环的模块；（2）训练一个轻量级PoLar预测网络，为每个输入生成执行程序（即决定哪些层被跳过、哪些层被重复）；（3）在推理时按预测程序动态执行，实现免训练的灵活推理。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，PoLar在准确性上一致优于标准推理和先前的动态深度方法，且通常执行更少的层；这些性能提升在分布外评估中依然保持。

Conclusion: 固定深度执行只利用了LLM潜在推理能力的一小部分；动态程序化层执行（PoLar）能更充分地释放预训练模型的推理潜力，以更少的计算开销获得更好的性能。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform inference by following a fixed depth and order, non-recurrent execution of all layers. We reveal the wide existence of training-free, flexible, dynamic program-of-layers (PoLar), where pretrained layers can be packed as modules and then skipped or looped to form a customized program for each input. For most inputs, substantially shorter program executions can achieve the same or better accuracy, while incorrect predictions of the original LLM can be corrected by alternative programs with fewer layers. These observations indicate that inference admits multiple valid latent computations beyond the standard forward pass. To efficiently achieve PoLar in practice, we propose a lightweight PoLar prediction network, which learns to generate execution programs that dynamically skip or repeat pretrained layers for each input. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PoLar consistently improves accuracy over standard inference and prior dynamic-depth methods, often while executing fewer layers, and that these gains persist under out-of-distribution evaluation. Our results suggest that fixed-depth execution captures only a narrow subset of an LLM's latent reasoning capacity.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）以固定的深度和顺序、非递归地执行所有层进行推理。我们揭示了免训练的、灵活的、动态的程序化层（PoLar）的广泛存在，其中预训练层可以被打包为模块，然后被跳过或循环，从而为每个输入形成定制的程序。对于大多数输入，显著更短的程序执行可以达到相同或更好的准确性，而原始LLM的错误预测可以通过更少层的替代程序来纠正。这些观察表明，推理过程存在超出标准前向传播的多种有效潜在计算路径。为了在实践中高效实现PoLar，我们提出了一个轻量级的PoLar预测网络，该网络学习为每个输入生成执行程序，动态跳过或重复预训练层。在数学推理基准上的实验表明，PoLar在准确性上一致优于标准推理和先前的动态深度方法，且通常执行更少的层，并且这些收益在分布外评估中依然保持。我们的结果表明，固定深度执行仅捕获了LLM潜在推理能力的一小部分。

</details>


### [101] [Gaussian Process Latent Factor Regression for Low-Data, High-Dimensional Output Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06576)
*Edward T. Stevenson,Eric T. Wolf,Mei Ting Mak,N. J. Mayne,Miles Cranmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）模型，通过将高维输出建模为从高斯过程先验中采样的低维潜在状态的线性-高斯解码，并解析边缘化解码器权重，从而在单一目标中同时实现压缩与预测，解决了现有方法（如PCA-GP）优化重建而非预测的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有压缩-预测流水线（如PCA-GP）使用基于重建优化的基，而非面向预测任务，导致预测性能受限。多输出高斯过程虽适合小样本场景，但难以处理高维输出。

Method: 提出GPLFR模型，将每个输出表示为从高斯过程先验中采样的低维潜在状态的线性-高斯解码，通过解析边缘化解码器权重，将压缩与预测耦合在同一优化目标中，从而可扩展至高维输出。

Result: 构建了首个针对岩石系外行星全球气候模型的空间分辨仿真器，验证了模型在高维输出预测上的有效性。

Conclusion: GPLFR通过联合优化压缩与预测，有效克服了传统流水线方法的局限，适用于小样本高维输出回归任务。

Original Abstract: In the sciences, regression tasks often require predicting high-dimensional outputs from few training examples. Multi-output Gaussian processes excel in low-data regimes but typically struggle with high-dimensional outputs. Compress-then-predict pipelines such as PCA-GP (principal component analysis plus Gaussian process regression) handle high dimensionality, but rely on bases optimized for reconstruction rather than prediction. To address this gap, we propose a model that represents each output as a linear-Gaussian decoding of a low-dimensional latent state drawn from a Gaussian process prior. By analytically marginalizing the decoder weights, we couple compression and prediction in a single objective that scales to high-dimensional outputs. We refer to this model as Gaussian process latent factor regression (GPLFR). We demonstrate GPLFR by building the first spatially resolved emulator of global climate models for rocky exoplanets.

中文摘要翻译: 在科学领域中，回归任务通常需要从少量训练样本中预测高维输出。多输出高斯过程在低数据量场景下表现优异，但难以处理高维输出。主成分分析结合高斯过程回归（PCA-GP）等"先压缩再预测"流水线虽能应对高维问题，但其优化的是重建基而非预测基。为填补这一空白，我们提出一种模型，将每个输出表示为从高斯过程先验中采样的低维潜在状态的线性-高斯解码。通过解析边缘化解码器权重，我们将压缩与预测耦合在一个可扩展至髙维输出的单一目标中。我们将此模型称为高斯过程潜在因子回归（GPLFR）。我们通过构建首个针对岩石系外行星全球气候模型的空间分辨仿真器来展示GPLFR的能力。

</details>


### [102] [Principles and Practice of Deep Representation Learning: or a Mathematical Theory of Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06624)
*San Buchanan,Druv Pai,Peng Wang,Yi Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书旨在通过表征学习的视角，揭示大型深度神经网络（“黑箱”）的内部机制，将架构设计从“炼金术”还原为本科水平的线性代数和微积分问题，并实现高效、可解释、可控的新型模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前大型生成模型虽然强大，但内部机制不透明，导致可解释性、可靠性和可控性方面的困难，并引发了过度炒作和恐惧。本书试图打开这一“黑箱”。

Method: 通过优化理论和信息论视角，解释现代神经网络架构的设计原则；将架构开发过程还原为线性代数和微积分练习；进而应用这些原则设计高效、可解释、可控的新型方法和模型。

Result: 提出了在保持甚至超越黑箱模型性能的前提下，通过设计实现可解释和可控的新方法与新模型。

Conclusion: 表征学习是深度学习模型经验性能力的核心因素；通过理解其机制，可以设计出既强大又透明可控的模型。

Original Abstract: In the current era of deep learning and especially generative models, there is significant investment in training very large generative models. Thus far, such models have been "black boxes" that are difficult to understand in the sense that they have opaque internal mechanisms, leading to difficulties in interpretability, reliability, and control. Naturally, this lack of understanding has led to both hype and fear.
  This book is an attempt to "open the black box" and understand the mechanisms of large deep networks, through the perspective of representation learning, which is a major factor - arguably the single most important one - in the empirical power of deep learning models. A brief outline of this book is as follows. Chapter 1 will summarize the threads that underlie the whole text. Chapters 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6 will explain the design principles of modern neural network architectures through optimization and information theory, reducing the process of architecture development (long having been described as a sort of "alchemy") to undergraduate-level linear algebra and calculus exercises once the underlying principles are introduced. Chapters 7 and 8 will discuss applications of these principles to solve problems in more paradigmatic ways, obtaining new methods and models which are efficient, interpretable, and controllable by design, and yet no less - sometimes even more - powerful than the black-box models they resemble. Chapter 9 will discuss potential future directions for deep learning, the role of representation learning, as well as some open problems.

中文摘要翻译: 在当前深度学习和生成模型的时代，训练超大规模生成模型投入巨大。然而，迄今为止，这些模型仍然是难以理解的"黑箱"——其内部机制不透明，导致在可解释性、可靠性和可控性方面存在困难。这种理解的缺失自然引发了既过度炒作又恐惧担忧的情绪。
  本书试图通过表征学习的视角"打开黑箱"，理解大型深度网络的机制，而表征学习是深度学习模型经验能力的一个主要因素——可以说是最重要的因素。本书的简要大纲如下：第1章将总结贯穿全文的主线。第2至6章将通过优化理论和信息论解释现代神经网络架构的设计原则，一旦引入这些基本原则，架构开发（长期以来被称为某种"炼金术"）的过程便被简化为本科水平的线性代数和微积分练习。第7章和第8章将讨论如何应用这些原则以更范式化的方式解决问题，从而获得在设计中就具备高效、可解释和可控的新方法和新模型，这些模型与它们所类似的黑箱模型相比毫不逊色，有时甚至更加强大。第9章将讨论深度学习的潜在未来方向、表征学习的作用以及一些开放性问题。

</details>


### [103] [Capturing non-Markovian dynamics in non-equilibrium stochastic systems using flow matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06658)
*Bhargav Sriram Siddani,John B. Bell,Alejandro L. Garcia,Ishan Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种生成式流匹配方法，直接对粒子模拟中的概率通量分布建模，显式纳入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应，以更准确地捕捉短时动力学和低粒子密度下的系统行为，在克拉默斯首次通过时间问题上优于马尔可夫基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 粗粒化随机偏微分方程（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）无法准确捕捉短时系统动力学（受非马尔可夫效应主导）以及低粒子密度下高度非高斯的分布特性，现有方法存在局限性。

Method: 开发了一种生成式流匹配方法，直接从粒子模拟中对通量的概率分布进行建模，显式地融入非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。

Result: 在非相互作用布朗粒子系统的克拉默斯首次通过时间问题上，该方法准确捕捉了短时行为，并在数密度统计矩的预测上优于马尔可夫基线的正则化DK方程。

Conclusion: 所提出的生成式流匹配方法能够有效建模非马尔可夫和非高斯效应，在短时动力学和低密度场景下显著优于传统马尔可夫粗粒化模型。

Original Abstract: Hydrodynamic models of stochastic particle systems represented by coarse-grained stochastic partial differential equations (SPDE), such as the regularized Dean-Kawasaki (DK) equation, do not accurately capture the short-time system dynamics that is dominated by non-Markovian effects, and low particle density regimes where the distributions are highly non-Gaussian. We develop a generative flow matching method that directly models the probability distribution of fluxes from particle simulations that explicitly incorporates non-Markovian and non-Gaussian effects. As a demonstration, we use this method to simulate the Kramers first passage time problem for a system of non-interacting Brownian particles. We show the model accurately captures the short-time behavior and provides better predictions of the statistical moments of the number density when compared against the solution of the Markovian baseline, regularized DK equation.

中文摘要翻译: 由粗粒化随机偏微分方程（如正则化Dean-Kawasaki方程）表示的随机粒子系统的流体动力学模型不能准确捕捉受非马尔可夫效应主导的短时系统动力学，以及分布高度非高斯的低粒子密度区域。我们开发了一种生成式流匹配方法，该方法直接从粒子模拟中对通量的概率分布进行建模，显式地融合了非马尔可夫和非高斯效应。作为演示，我们使用该方法模拟了非相互作用布朗粒子系统的克拉默斯首次通过时间问题。结果表明，该模型能够准确捕捉短时行为，并且在数密度统计矩的预测上优于马尔可夫基线的正则化DK方程的解。

</details>


### [104] [Explainable Runtime Dependency Tracking for AI-RAN Conflict Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06663)
*Christie Djidjev,Nicholas Kaminski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级监控原语，通过布尔矩阵表示参数-KPI依赖关系，利用流式遥测事件跟踪依赖一致性，为AI-RAN中的冲突诊断和模型刷新提供可解释信号。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在AI-RAN中，多种xApps/rApps和控制功能共享参数与KPI，运行时诊断依赖的参数-KPI关系可能因运行工况变化而失效，仅知道哪些应用已部署不足以监控冲突，系统还需实时验证依赖假设是否仍然有效。

Method: 用布尔矩阵表示活跃的依赖关系，通过布尔矩阵乘法检查近期参数活动事件与KPI响应事件是否与当前依赖估计一致；采用滑动窗口推理，当观测一致时复用当前估计，当结构变化时重新计算依赖矩阵。

Result: 在受控布尔事件流上的实验表明，该方法能够在依赖变化和布尔观测噪声下实现高效且准确的依赖跟踪。

Conclusion: 该追踪器可作为冲突诊断和慢循环模型刷新的可解释信号，而非自主缓解机制，有效辅助AI-RAN运行时的依赖一致性监控。

Original Abstract: Future AI-integrated Radio Access Networks (AI-RAN) will combine open programmability with learning-enabled xApps, rApps, and control functions that act on shared parameters and key performance indicators (KPIs). For conflict monitoring, it is not enough to know which applications are deployed; the system must also know whether the parameter--KPI dependencies assumed by runtime diagnosis remain valid under the current operating regime. This paper studies a lightweight monitoring primitive for that purpose: tracking an interpretable dependency representation from streaming telemetry events.
  We represent active dependencies by a Boolean matrix and use Boolean matrix multiplication to check whether recent parameter-activity and KPI-response events are consistent with the current estimate. We propose a sliding-window inference procedure that reuses the estimate when it remains consistent and recomputes it when recent observations indicate structural change. The tracker is intended as an explainable signal for conflict diagnosis and slow-loop model refresh, not as an autonomous mitigation mechanism. Experiments on controlled Boolean event streams show efficient and accurate tracking under dependency changes and Boolean observation noise.

中文摘要翻译: 未来集成人工智能的无线接入网络（AI-RAN）将把开放可编程性与支持学习的xApps、rApps及控制功能相结合，这些功能作用于共享的参数和关键性能指标（KPI）。对于冲突监控而言，仅知道哪些应用已部署是不够的；系统还必须知道运行时诊断所依赖的参数-KPI关系在当前运行工况下是否仍然有效。本文研究了一种用于该目的的轻量级监控原语：通过流式遥测事件跟踪一个可解释的依赖表示。我们将活跃的依赖关系表示为一个布尔矩阵，并使用布尔矩阵乘法来检查近期的参数活动事件和KPI响应事件是否与当前的依赖估计一致。我们提出了一种滑动窗口推理过程：当估计与观测一致时重复使用当前估计，而当近期观测表明结构变化时则重新计算。该追踪器旨在作为冲突诊断和慢循环模型刷新的可解释信号，而非自主缓解机制。在受控布尔事件流上的实验表明，该方法能够在依赖变化和布尔观测噪声下实现高效且准确的跟踪。

</details>


### [105] [Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping for Sparse-Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06673)
*Ujjwal Bhatta,Utsabi Dangol,Sumaly Bajracharya,Rodrigue Rizk,KC Santosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种不确定性感知的LLM引导策略塑形框架（ULPS），通过将校准后的大语言模型与PPO算法相结合，利用A*生成的符号轨迹微调BERT模型，并基于MC Dropout的不确定性估计自适应平衡LLM引导与策略学习，有效解决了稀疏奖励和异质任务序列下的强化学习收敛慢、泛化弱和探索效率低的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前强化学习在稀疏奖励和异质任务序列场景下存在收敛速度慢、泛化能力弱、探索效率低下等持久挑战，现有方法缺乏结构化的先验知识引导和不确定性感知的调控机制。

Method: (1) 使用A*算法作为符号规划器生成最优符号轨迹；(2) 利用这些轨迹微调BERT语言模型；(3) 在RL训练过程中，通过MC Dropout估计认知不确定性，并基于熵的混合机制自适应地平衡LLM引导与PPO策略学习。

Result: 在MiniGridUnlockPickup基准上，ULPS相比未引导、未校准和标准RL基线，在成功率、奖励效率和样本复杂度上均取得一致提升；微调后执行准确率提升超过9%，所需环境交互更少，奖励AUC更高。

Conclusion: 将符号A*轨迹、预训练语言先验和不确定性感知控制相结合，为稀疏奖励领域中的多任务强化学习提供了一种原则性且有效的方法，并具有扩展到部分可观测和多智能体场景的潜力。

Original Abstract: Sparse rewards and heterogeneous task sequences remain persistent challenges in Reinforcement Learning (RL), often resulting in slow convergence, weak generalization, and inefficient exploration. We propose Uncertainty-Aware LLM-Guided Policy Shaping (ULPS), a novel framework that integrates a calibrated Large Language Model (LLM) into the RL training loop to provide structured, uncertainty-modulated behavioral guidance. ULPS employs an A*-based oracle to synthesize optimal symbolic trajectories, which are used to fine-tune a BERT-based language model. During training, this model supplies action suggestions whose influence is conditioned on epistemic uncertainty estimated via Monte Carlo (MC) dropout. An entropy-based blending mechanism adaptively balances LLM guidance and the learned policy (via Proximal Policy Optimization, PPO), allowing the agent to prioritize reliable priors while preserving adaptability. We evaluate ULPS on the MiniGridUnlockPickup benchmark and observe consistent improvements in success rate, reward efficiency, and sample complexity over unguided, uncalibrated, and standard RL baselines. ULPS achieves more than 9% improvement in execution accuracy after fine-tuning, requires fewer environment interactions, and yields higher reward AUC. Our results demonstrate that integrating symbolic A* trajectories, pretrained language priors, and uncertainty-aware control offers a principled and effective approach to multi-task reinforcement learning in sparse-reward domains, with potential extensibility to partially observable and multi-agent settings.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏奖励和异质任务序列仍然是强化学习中的持久挑战，常导致收敛缓慢、泛化能力弱和探索效率低下。我们提出不确定性感知的LLM引导策略塑形（ULPS），一种新颖的框架，将校准后的大语言模型融入RL训练循环，以提供结构化、不确定性调节的行为引导。ULPS采用基于A*的预言机来合成最优符号轨迹，用于微调基于BERT的语言模型。在训练过程中，该模型提供动作建议，其影响程度由通过蒙特卡洛Dropout估计的认知不确定性决定。一种基于熵的混合机制自适应地平衡LLM引导与学习策略（通过PPO），使智能体能够优先利用可靠的先验知识，同时保持适应性。我们在MiniGridUnlockPickup基准上评估ULPS，观察到其在成功率、奖励效率和样本复杂度上相较于未引导、未校准和标准RL基线均有显著提升。ULPS在微调后执行准确率提升超过9%，需要更少的环境交互，并获得更高的奖励AUC。我们的结果表明，集成符号A*轨迹、预训练语言先验和不确定性感知控制为稀疏奖励领域中的多任务强化学习提供了一种原则性且有效的方法，并具有扩展到部分可观测和多智能体场景的潜力。

</details>


### [106] [Spatiotemporal Imputation with Graph-Informed Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06682)
*Zepeng Zhang,Aref Einizade,Jhony H. Giraldo,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GiFlow，一种图信息引导的流匹配框架，用于时空数据插补，通过图信息先验替代高斯先验，结合混合向量场模型，显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时空插补方法存在两大局限：(1) 传统RNN/GNN通过迭代传播易累积误差；(2) 扩散方法虽缓解误差传播但需迭代采样且依赖问题无关的高斯先验，效率和效果均受限。

Method: GiFlow包含两个核心设计：(1) 图信息先验——通过时空滤波从观测信号中构建，使得源分布与目标分布更对齐，简化生成轨迹；(2) 混合向量场模型——融合空间注意力、时间注意力和时空传播，联合建模时空依赖关系。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，GiFlow在时空插补任务上全面超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GiFlow通过图信息先验和混合向量场模型有效解决了现有方法误差累积与采样效率低的问题，为时空数据插补提供了一种更高效且更精确的方案。

Original Abstract: Missing data is a common challenge in spatiotemporal systems, arising in applications such as air quality monitoring and urban traffic management. Traditional machine learning approaches, like recurrent and graph neural networks, rely on iterative propagation, which tends to accumulate errors over time and space. Recent diffusion-based methods mitigate error propagation but require iterative sampling and often depend on problem-agnostic Gaussian priors, limiting both efficiency and effectiveness. To address these limitations, we propose GiFlow, a Graph-Informed Flow Matching framework for spatiotemporal imputation. GiFlow replaces the typical Gaussian prior with a graph-informed prior constructed via spatiotemporal filtering of observable signals, which better aligns the source distribution to the target and thereby simplifies the generation trajectory. The flow field is parameterized by a hybrid vector field model that integrates spatial attention, temporal attention, and spatiotemporal propagation, enabling joint modeling of spatial and temporal dependencies. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed GiFlow outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in spatiotemporal imputation. The code is available at https://github.com/zepengzhang/GiFlow.

中文摘要翻译: 缺失数据是时空系统中常见的挑战，出现在空气质量监测和城市交通管理等应用中。传统的机器学习方法，如循环神经网络和图神经网络，依赖于迭代传播，这倾向于在时间和空间上累积误差。最近的基于扩散的方法缓解了误差传播，但需要迭代采样，并且通常依赖于与问题无关的高斯先验，限制了效率和有效性。为了解决这些局限性，我们提出了GiFlow，一个用于时空插补的图信息引导的流匹配框架。GiFlow用通过时空滤波从可观测信号构建的图信息先验替代典型的高斯先验，这使得源分布与目标分布更好地对齐，从而简化了生成轨迹。流场由一个混合向量场模型参数化，该模型集成了空间注意力、时间注意力和时空传播，实现了时空依赖关系的联合建模。在合成数据集和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，提出的GiFlow在时空插补上优于最先进的方法。代码可在https://github.com/zepengzhang/GiFlow获取。

</details>


### [107] [Towards Serverless Semi-Decentralized Federated Learning with Heterogeneous Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06687)
*Su Wang,Mung Chiang,H. Vincent Poor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无服务器半去中心化联邦学习（SSD-FL）框架，通过轻量级D2D初始化实现集群形成，并利用有效损失函数和Cheeger不等式优化异构优化器下的集群划分，在收敛速度和通信效率上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 去中心化联邦学习中集群形成在异构ML优化器下的问题尚未被充分探索，网络图结构、本地数据异质性和不同本地优化器之间的复杂耦合使得集群优化极具挑战性，现有集中式FL的聚类方法无法直接适用于完全去中心化环境。

Method: 提出SSD-FL：1) 通过一次性轻量级D2D初始化阶段实现集群形成；2) 将全局轮次划分为集群内和集群间两个阶段；3) 设计"有效损失函数"整合设备特定ML优化器与基于网络图的正则化；4) 利用Cheeger不等式通过共识间隙推导迭代聚类算法；5) 提出评分指标量化数据和优化器异质性。

Result: 实验验证SSD-FL在多种网络图、数据集和本地优化器场景下，相比三类去中心化FL方法，同时提升了收敛速度和通信效率。

Conclusion: SSD-FL有效解决了去中心化FL中异构优化器下的集群优化问题，实现了无需持久服务器基础设施的高效联邦学习，且集群形成仅需一次性初始化。

Original Abstract: We investigate cluster formation, involving the number and composition of clusters, in decentralized federated learning (FL) with heterogeneous machine learning (ML) optimizers. While clustering in centralized FL has enabled scalability and resource savings, its value and development in fully decentralized environments have yet to be explored. Optimizing cluster formation in such environments is challenging, especially due to the complex coupling between network graph structures, local data heterogeneity, and different local ML model optimizers. To address these challenges, we propose serverless semi-decentralized FL (SSD-FL), a methodology requiring no persistent server infrastructure. In SSD-FL, cluster formation occurs via a lightweight, one-time device-to-device (D2D) initialization phase, after which actual ML model training (alongside consensus and convergence processes) is fully serverless. Functionally, SSD-FL segments global rounds into intra-cluster and inter-cluster regimes, ensuring global convergence and consensus through novel "effective loss functions" that integrate device-specific ML optimizers with network graph-based regularization. Next, SSD-FL leverages the consensus gap via the Cheeger inequality to develop an iterative clustering algorithm evaluated against our derived convergence and consensus bounds, which incorporate a unique scoring metric to quantify data and optimizer heterogeneity across devices. Finally, experimental evaluation against three categories of decentralized FL methodologies validate that SSD-FL improves both convergence speeds and communication efficiency across various network graphs, datasets, and local optimizer regimes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在具有异构机器学习（ML）优化器的去中心化联邦学习（FL）中的集群形成问题，涉及集群的数量和组成。尽管集中式FL中的聚类已经实现了可扩展性和资源节约，但其在完全去中心化环境中的价值和开发尚未被探索。在这种环境中优化集群形成极具挑战性，尤其是由于网络图结构、本地数据异质性和不同本地ML模型优化器之间的复杂耦合。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了无服务器半去中心化FL（SSD-FL），一种不需要持久服务器基础设施的方法。在SSD-FL中，集群形成通过一次轻量级的设备到设备（D2D）初始化阶段完成，之后实际的ML模型训练（连同共识和收敛过程）完全无服务器化。在功能上，SSD-FL将全局轮次划分为集群内和集群间两个阶段，通过新颖的"有效损失函数"实现全局收敛和共识，该函数将设备特定的ML优化器与基于网络图的正则化相结合。接着，SSD-FL利用Cheeger不等式通过共识间隙开发了一种迭代聚类算法，并根据我们推导的收敛和共识边界进行评估，该边界包含一种独特的评分指标来量化设备间的数据和优化器异质性。最后，针对三类去中心化FL方法的实验评估验证了SSD-FL在各种网络图、数据集和本地优化器场景下同时提升了收敛速度和通信效率。

</details>


### [108] [The Geography of Algorithmic Judgment: LLM Intermediaries, Place Identity, and Racial Steering in Housing Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06694)
*Hana Samad,Trung Lam,Christoph Mügge-Durum,Michael Akinwumi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过行为审计实验，发现大型语言模型在住房推荐中存在种族导向行为，这种导向是模型解释权产生的涌现行为，而非静态属性，且受用户身份、偏好表达和城市空间逻辑的交互影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究已发现大语言模型可能存在种族导向问题，但对其产生机制、在不同条件下的变化规律以及城市间差异缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在探究LLM在住房推荐场景中的种族导向行为是否受用户身份和偏好表达影响，以及这种行为能否跨城市泛化。

Method: 对7种开源和闭源大语言模型进行行为审计，覆盖美国四个城市。设计三种迭代提示条件（逐步添加生活偏好语境），采用公平住房配对测试方法，测试模型的位置推荐是否存在种族差异。

Result: 导向行为存在于多数模型中，但其方向和强度在不同提示条件下不一致。偏好条件化测试相比基线条件增加或改变了表现出导向行为的模型数量，说明LLM可能根据用户种族身份对相同的住房偏好做出不同解读。不同城市的测试结果无法相互泛化。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的种族导向是解释权产生的涌现行为，城市不是中立的测试单元。在采用AI工具调节空间访问时，必须结合本地和领域专业知识，以确保公平住房的法律和制度承诺不被削弱。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly assuming an intermediary role in housing search through the integration of listing platforms within conversational interfaces, mediating access to information, search, and recommendations within urban settings. We expand on prior work on racial steering in LLMs by conducting a behavioral audit of seven open-weight and closed-source LLMs across four U.S. cities, testing location recommendations across three iterative prompting conditions that progressively add lifestyle preference context and reflect fair housing paired-testing methodologies. We find that steering is an emergent behavior of the model's interpretive license rather than primarily a static property. Steering results from the interaction of a user's identity, preference articulation, and the spatial logic that a model has internalized about learned representations of place, preference, and opportunity in a given city, and how different types of users relate to it. While steering was present, it was not uniform in direction or magnitude across evaluated conditions. Preference-conditioned testing often increased or reconfigured the number of models that exhibited steering behaviors relative to baseline conditions, suggesting that LLMs may interpret what the same housing preference means differently depending on the racial identity of the user. Our findings also demonstrate that the city is not a neutral testing unit for LLM evaluation in place-based sectors, and results from one local market cannot be assumed to generalize to another. Local and domain expertise will be required in the housing sector to ensure that legal and institutional commitments to fair housing are not undermined while adopting AI tools that mediate spatial access.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）正通过将房源平台集成到对话界面中，迅速承担住房搜索的中介角色，调节城市环境中信息获取、搜索和推荐的访问。我们扩展了关于LLM中种族导向的先前研究，对美国四个城市的七种开源和闭源LLM进行了行为审计，测试了三种迭代提示条件下的位置推荐，这些条件逐步添加了生活偏好语境，并反映了公平住房配对测试方法。我们发现，导向是模型解释权的一种涌现行为，而非主要是一种静态属性。导向源于用户身份、偏好表达以及模型对给定城市中地方、偏好和机会的学习表征所内化的空间逻辑之间的交互作用，以及不同类型用户与之关联的方式。虽然导向存在，但其方向和强度在评估条件下并不一致。偏好条件化测试相比基线条件，往往增加或改变了表现出导向行为的模型数量，这表明LLM可能根据用户的种族身份对相同的住房偏好做出不同解读。我们的发现还表明，在城市相关领域中，城市并不是LLM评估的中立测试单元，一个本地市场的结果不能假定能推广到另一个市场。在住房领域，需要具备本地和领域专业知识，以确保公平住房的法律和制度承诺在采用调节空间访问的AI工具时不被削弱。

</details>


### [109] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本流程，利用合成生产历史数据使时间序列基础模型（TSFM）能够在光伏电站冷启动（无历史观测）场景下进行预测，实验表明协变量感知的TSFM在440个电站数据集上比传统基线提升约1.7-2倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 光伏电站在并网时缺乏历史观测数据，无法直接使用标准监督式预测模型，因此需要一种无需目标站点历史数据的冷启动预测方法。

Method: 提出零样本冷启动预测流程：从电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成发电历史数据，然后利用时间序列基础模型（TSFM）通过推理时条件化进行预测。评估了五种TSFM，并设计了三种策略：严格冷启动基线、真实反馈和自预测反馈。

Result: 在440个光伏站点、四个数据集上的实验表明：协变量感知的TSFM优于传统基线约1.7-2倍；其中TabPFN-TS在真实反馈策略下表现最佳（MAE 0.514, RMSE 0.721 kWh kWp⁻¹ d⁻¹），Chronos-2在自预测反馈策略下最鲁棒；性能对合成历史数据的来源不敏感。

Conclusion: 合成生产历史数据能有效支持TSFM在光伏冷启动场景下的预测，预测精度主要取决于合理时间上下文的可用性而非具体生成器，协变量感知的TSFM是解决该问题的最佳选择。

Original Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

中文摘要翻译: 在并网调试阶段，光伏运营商必须在目标站点观测数据可用之前进行发电量预测，这限制了标准监督式预测方法的直接使用。针对这一冷启动场景，本文提出了一种零样本流程：从电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成发电历史数据，使时间序列基础模型（TSFM）能够通过推理时条件化进行预测。在严格冷启动基线、真实反馈和自预测反馈三种策略下，对五种TSFM与经典基线进行了基准测试。评估覆盖了四个数据集中440个光伏站点及多种气候区域。协变量感知的基础模型优于基线约1.7-2倍：其中TabPFN-TS在真实反馈策略下取得最低误差（MAE 0.514, RMSE 0.721 kWh kWp⁻¹ d⁻¹），而Chronos-2在自预测反馈策略下最为鲁棒。模型性能对合成历史数据的来源基本不敏感，这表明预测精度更多取决于合理时间上下文的可用性，而非具体的生成器。

</details>


### [110] [RECAP: Regression Evaluation for Continual Adaptation of Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06698)
*Harsh Deshpande,Kushal Chawla,Sangwoo Cho,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RECAP是一个基准测试，用于衡量在严格的主动适应-再测试协议下，约束级别的持续学习现象（遗忘、回归、前向迁移）。通过评估六种方法在四个大语言模型和三种约束演变调度上的表现，发现这些方法在主动适应范式下性能无显著提升，且额外增加了延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准测试要么假设静态约束集，要么采用具有评估反馈的反应式协议，但生产环境中的智能体系统经常面临动态变化的约束，且必须在下一轮交互中立即合规，现有方法无法满足这种严格主动适应的需求。

Method: 提出RECAP基准测试，采用严格的主动适应-再测试协议：提示优化方法仅接收约束规范，必须在未见任何测试数据的情况下进行泛化。评估六种方法在四个LLM和三种约束演变调度上的表现，衡量约束级别的持续学习现象。

Result: 六种方法在主动适应范式下均未表现出显著性能提升，即使付出了更高的延迟代价。这些为离线或反应式场景设计的方法在主动适应范式下表现不足。

Conclusion: 研究强调了设计主动提示适应方法的迫切需求，使模型在部署中能够对持续变化的需求保持鲁棒性。现有方法不足以应对主动适应范式。

Original Abstract: Production agentic systems routinely face evolving constraints and must comply from the very next interaction. Scenarios like a tool-call notification changing a compliance threshold or a policy update adding disclosure requirements fit this criteria, having close to no room for errors in production. This proactive adaptation setting is common in deployment, but absent from current benchmarks, which assume either static constraint sets or reactive protocols with evaluation feedback. We introduce RECAP, a benchmark that measures continual-learning phenomena (forgetting, regression, forward transfer) at the constraint level under a strictly proactive adapt-then-test protocol: prompt optimization methods receive only the constraint specification and must generalize before seeing any test data. Evaluating six methods across four LLMs and three schedules with evolving constraints, we find that these methods show no significant improvement in performance, even after incurring a higher latency. These methods, designed for offline or reactive settings, are inadequate for the proactive paradigm. Our work emphasizes the growing need for designing proactive prompt adaptation methods, where the models must remain robust to evolving needs in deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 生产环境中的智能体系统经常面临不断变化的约束，且必须从下一次交互开始立即合规。工具调用通知改变合规阈值、策略更新增加披露要求等场景均符合这一标准，在生产中几乎没有容错空间。这种主动适应设置在实际部署中很常见，但在现有基准测试中却是缺失的——现有基准要么假设静态约束集，要么采用带有评估反馈的反应式协议。我们提出了RECAP，一个在严格的主动适应-再测试协议下，在约束层面衡量持续学习现象（遗忘、回归、前向迁移）的基准测试：提示优化方法仅接收约束规范，必须在未见任何测试数据的情况下进行泛化。通过评估六种方法在四个大语言模型和三种具有演变约束的调度上的表现，我们发现这些方法即使在付出了更高延迟代价后，性能也未见显著提升。这些为离线或反应式场景设计的方法在主动适应范式下表现不足。我们的工作强调了设计主动提示适应方法的迫切需求，使模型在部署中能够对持续变化的需求保持鲁棒性。

</details>


### [111] [ShallowBench: Benchmarking Generative Drug Design Models on Shallow-Pocket Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06717)
*Saket Reddy,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ShallowBench基准，用于评估生成式AI模型在浅口袋（低可药性）靶标上的药物设计能力，发现现有模型在这些靶标上预测结合亲和力较弱。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于结构的药物设计生成模型主要适用于深度结合口袋，难以处理KRAS和MYC等历史上“不可成药”的低可药性靶标，因此需要专门针对浅口袋靶标的基准测试。

Method: 从CrossDocked2020中筛选出5,780个浅口袋靶标构建ShallowBench；通过计算Alpha Shape“盖子”体积与底层蛋白质原子体素体积的差值，分离出低凹度但表面面积足够的靶标。

Result: 评估多种最先进的生成模型后发现，这些模型在低凹度界面上预测的结合亲和力较弱。

Conclusion: ShallowBench为生成生物学模型提供了严格的基准测试，凸显了针对这些困难靶标设计新型架构或损失函数的必要性。

Original Abstract: While generative AI models have demonstrated remarkable success in structure-based drug design, they predominantly rely on deep binding pockets and struggle to sample effective ligands for challenging low-pocketability targets, such as the historically "undruggable" oncology targets KRAS and MYC. To address this gap, we introduce ShallowBench, a strictly curated benchmark of 5,780 shallow-pocket targets extracted from CrossDocked2020. By computing the difference between an Alpha Shape "lid" volume and the underlying protein atom voxel volume, we successfully isolated targets with low concavity while ensuring sufficient surface area for binding. Evaluating various state-of-the-art generative models reveals weaker predicted binding affinity on these low-concavity interfaces. ShallowBench therefore provides a rigorous benchmark for generative biology models and highlights the necessity of new architectural innovations or loss functions capable of navigating these challenging targets.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管生成式AI模型在基于结构的药物设计中取得了显著成功，但它们主要依赖于深部结合口袋，难以对具有挑战性的低可药性靶标（如历史上“不可成药”的肿瘤靶标KRAS和MYC）进行有效配体采样。为填补这一空白，我们提出了ShallowBench——一个从CrossDocked2020中严格筛选出的5,780个浅口袋靶标基准集。通过计算Alpha Shape“盖子”体积与底层蛋白质原子体素体积的差值，我们成功分离出低凹度且表面面积足够的靶标。对多种最先进生成模型的评估显示，这些模型在这些低凹度界面上的预测结合亲和力较弱。因此，ShallowBench为生成生物学模型提供了严格的基准测试，并凸显了设计能够应对这些困难靶标的新型架构或损失函数的必要性。

</details>


### [112] [MSAIC-Net: A Multi-Scale Attention and Imbalance-Aware Contrastive Network for ECG-Based Myocardial Substrate Abnormality Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06718)
*Canyu Lei,Fenglin Zhang,Derek Bivona,Cristiane Singulane,Jonathan Pan,Kenneth Bilchick,Amit R. Patel,Jianxin Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多尺度注意力增强卷积网络（MSAIC-Net），结合不平衡感知监督对比学习和导联置换重要性分析，实现基于心电图的、可解释的心肌基质异常检测，在UVA和PTB-XL数据集上均优于基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 心电图（ECG）是检测心肌瘢痕和心肌梗死等心肌基质异常的低成本广泛可用工具，但由于导联依赖性表现异质、高维多导联信号、类别不平衡以及深度学习模型可解释性差，基于心电图的检测仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出MSAIC-Net：1）采用并行空洞卷积分支提取多时间感受野的ECG特征；2）引入通道注意力机制自适应重加权导联维和特征通道表示；3）提出不平衡感知监督对比学习策略，增强同类样本紧致性并扩大异常与正常样本间的分离度；4）引入导联置换重要性分析量化每导联贡献以提升可解释性。

Result: 在UVA（小样本心肌瘢痕）和PTB-XL（大规模MI识别）两个数据集上评估，MSAIC-Net优于基线模型，在低数据量的UVA队列上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为基于心电图的检测心肌基质异常提供了一种有效且可解释的方法。

Original Abstract: Myocardial substrate abnormalities, such as myocardial scar and myocardial infarction (MI), are associated with adverse cardiovascular outcomes. Electrocardiography (ECG) provides a low-cost and widely available tool for detecting these abnormalities, but ECG-based detection remains challenging due to heterogeneous lead-dependent manifestations, high-dimensional multi-lead signals, class imbalance, and the limited interpretability of deep learning models. We propose a multi-scale attention-enhanced convolutional network (MSAIC-Net) for ECG-based myocardial substrate abnormality detection. MSAIC-Net employs parallel atrous convolutional branches to extract ECG features across multiple temporal receptive fields. %, enabling the model to capture both local and longer-range temporal patterns. Channel attention is then used to adaptively reweight informative lead-wise and feature-channel representations. To address class imbalance and improve feature separability, we introduce a novel imbalance-aware supervised contrastive learning strategy that encourages samples from the same class to form compact representations while increasing separation between abnormal and normal samples. Lead-wise permutation importance is further incorporated to quantify the contribution of each ECG lead and improve model interpretability. The proposed method was evaluated on two complementary datasets: a low-data institutional cohort from the University of Virginia (UVA) Health System for myocardial scar classification and the large-scale public PTB-XL dataset from PhysioNet for MI identification. Experimental results show that MSAIC-Net outperforms baseline models, with particularly pronounced improvements in the low-data UVA cohort. Overall, the proposed framework provides an effective and interpretable approach for ECG-based detection of myocardial substrate abnormalities.

中文摘要翻译: 心肌基质异常，如心肌瘢痕和心肌梗死（MI），与不良心血管结局相关。心电图（ECG）为检测这些异常提供了一种低成本且广泛可用的工具，但由于导联依赖性的异质表现、高维多导联信号、类别不平衡以及深度学习模型的可解释性有限，基于ECG的检测仍然具有挑战性。我们提出了一种多尺度注意力增强卷积网络（MSAIC-Net），用于基于ECG的心肌基质异常检测。MSAIC-Net采用并行空洞卷积分支，在多个时间感受野上提取ECG特征，使模型能够捕获局部和更长时间范围的时间模式。然后利用通道注意力自适应地重新加权信息性的导联维度和特征通道表示。为了解决类别不平衡问题并提高特征可分性，我们引入了一种新的不平衡感知监督对比学习策略，鼓励同一类别的样本形成紧凑的表示，同时增加异常样本与正常样本之间的分离度。进一步引入导联置换重要性，以量化每个ECG导联的贡献并提高模型可解释性。该方法在两个互补数据集上进行了评估：来自弗吉尼亚大学（UVA）健康系统的低数据量机构队列用于心肌瘢痕分类，以及来自PhysioNet的大规模公共PTB-XL数据集用于MI识别。实验结果表明，MSAIC-Net优于基线模型，在低数据量的UVA队列中改善尤为显著。总体而言，所提出的框架为基于ECG的心肌基质异常检测提供了一种有效且可解释的方法。

</details>


### [113] [Flatland: The Adventures of Gradient Descent with Large Step Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06722)
*Leonardo Galli,Curtis Fox,Wiebke Bartolomaeus,Mark Schmidt,Holger Rauhut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对神经网络训练中目标函数非全局L-光滑时，梯度下降（GD）的最大可行步长这一长期未解决问题，提出了一种统一的“大步长”定义（仅需梯度局部Lipschitz或Hölder连续），并设计了自适应的单步长一阶方法，证明了该方法能从训练开始就处于稳定性边缘（EoS），使损失非单调下降、步长与锐度乘积保持在EoS阈值2以上，并能将锐度最小化至全局最小值。研究发现过早进入全局平坦区域会降低收敛速度和泛化能力，而利用自稳定化机制让GD进入略锐的谷底可化失败训练为成功。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解决深度学习中一个长期悬而未决的理论与实践问题：当目标函数非全局L-光滑时，如何确定梯度下降能够收敛的最大可行步长，并给出兼具理论保证和实际效果的自适应步长方法。

Method: 提出了仅依赖梯度局部Lipschitz（甚至Hölder）连续性的“大步长”统一定义；设计了自适应的单步长一阶优化方法，使步长与锐度（Hessian最大特征值）的乘积在整个训练过程中始终保持在EoS阈值2以上，利用自稳定化论证允许GD进入略尖锐的谷底区域。

Result: 该方法从训练一开始就处于稳定性边缘，损失非单调下降；能够将锐度最小化至全局最小值；发现过早进入全局平坦区域会减慢收敛并损害泛化能力；通过自稳定化机制，可使原本失败的训练转为成功。

Conclusion: 提出了一种适用于非全局L-光滑目标的梯度下降大步长理论与自适应方法，揭示了稳定性边缘、锐度优化与泛化之间的新关系，为深度学习优化提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Original Abstract: The training of neural networks often entails objective functions that are not globally $L$-smooth. For these functions, it is both theoretically and practically difficult to reply to the question: what is the largest possible step size that ensures the convergence of gradient descent (GD)? We address this longstanding open question in deep learning by providing a unifying definition of "large" step sizes that requires only local Lipschitz (or even Hölder) continuity of the gradient. We design first-order adaptive methods that provably yield large step sizes and show that they operate at the edge of stability (EoS) right from the start of the training. In particular, the loss decreases nonmonotonically and the product between the step size and sharpness, i.e., the largest eigenvalue of the Hessian, stays above the EoS threshold of 2 throughout training. Using our method, we are also able to minimize the sharpness all the way down to its global minimum. Contrary to expectation, we find that encountering globally-flat regions too early in the training may both slow down convergence and jeopardize the generalization ability of the network. Exploiting a self-stabilization argument, we allow GD to enter slightly sharper valleys and turn unsuccessful training runs into very successful ones.

中文摘要翻译: 神经网络的训练通常涉及非全局L-光滑的目标函数。对于这些函数，无论在理论上还是实践上，都难以回答这样一个问题：确保梯度下降（GD）收敛的最大可能步长是多少？我们通过提供一个统一的“大”步长定义来解决深度学习中这一长期悬而未决的问题，该定义仅需梯度的局部Lipschitz（甚至Hölder）连续性。我们设计了能可证明地产生大步长的自适应性一阶方法，并表明这些方法从训练一开始就处于稳定性边缘（EoS）。具体而言，损失函数非单调下降，并且步长与锐度（即Hessian矩阵的最大特征值）的乘积在整个训练过程中始终保持在EoS阈值2以上。使用我们的方法，还能将锐度最小化至其全局最小值。与预期相反，我们发现过早遇到全局平坦区域既会减缓收敛速度，又可能损害网络的泛化能力。利用自稳定化论证，我们允许GD进入略尖锐的谷底，从而将原本失败的训练转化为非常成功的训练。

</details>


### [114] [Synthics: Synthetic Physics-like Datasets for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06724)
*Jari Vepsäläinen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法（Bayesian PCFG）的合成回归数据生成方法，能够生成在结构上类似物理方程（如费曼方程语料库）的合成数据集，并在下游超参数调优任务中达到与真实数据调优相当的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实场景中收集足够多的真实样本往往不可行，而有限的数据会阻碍模型的泛化能力。合成数据生成提供了一种实用方案，但前提是生成的数据能忠实反映真实观测的结构。本文旨在解决如何生成结构上接近真实物理方程的合成回归数据集这一核心问题。

Method: 使用贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法（Bayesian PCFG）从给定的方程语料库中捕捉底层代数结构，并从中采样生成新方程；通过非侵入式探测方法为每个方程刻画适用域（applicability domain），恢复变量间约束；在有效域内使用均匀分布与截断正态分布的混合分布随机采样输入，模拟真实实验条件。

Result: 在费曼方程语料库上，所提方法在全部8项结构特征上匹配语料库分布，而未经平滑的纯概率文法仅匹配2项。在下游超参数调优任务中，使用合成数据调优的梯度提升回归器在20种配置中平均选出第6优的配置，这一结果与使用真实数据调优相当，显著优于随机表达式树（第10优）和噪声（第19优）。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯先验对于在有限语料库规模下保持结构保真度至关重要；所提方法能够生成结构忠实且物理意义合理的合成回归数据，并在下游任务中取得与真实数据调优媲美的效果。

Original Abstract: Representative data is fundamental in machine learning, as limited data hinders generalisation. Collecting sufficient real-world samples is often infeasible. Synthetic data generation offers a practical solution, but only if the generated data faithfully reflects the structure of real observations. In this paper, a method for generating synthetic regression datasets that structurally resemble physics equations from a given equation corpus is presented. The approach uses a Bayesian Probabilistic Context-Free Grammar to capture the underlying algebraic structure of the corpus, from which novel equations are sampled. To ensure the generated inputs lie within a physically meaningful domain, the applicability domain is characterised for each equation through non-intrusive probing, also recovering inter-variable constraints. Input sampling further mimics realistic experimental conditions by drawing from random sub-ranges of the valid domain with mixed uniform and truncated normal distributions. The generated data is statistically validated against the Feynman equation corpus using Kolmogorov-Smirnov tests. The generated equations match the corpus on all of the eight studied structural features, compared to only two for an unsmoothed purely probabilistic grammar, demonstrating that the Bayesian prior is essential for structural fidelity given the size of the corpus. In a downstream hyperparameter-tuning task, a gradient-boosted regressor tuned on the synthetic data picks, on average, the 6th-best configuration out of 20 on real data, matching the result of tuning on real data itself and substantially outperforming random expression trees (10th) and noise (19th).

中文摘要翻译: 代表性数据是机器学习的基础，因为有限的数据会阻碍泛化。收集足够的真实样本往往不可行。合成数据生成提供了一种实用方案，但前提是生成的数据能忠实反映真实观测的结构。本文提出了一种方法，用于生成在结构上类似于给定方程语料库中物理方程的合成回归数据集。该方法使用贝叶斯概率上下文无关文法捕捉语料库的底层代数结构，并从中采样新的方程。为确保生成的输入位于物理有意义的域内，通过非侵入式探测为每个方程刻画适用域，同时恢复变量间约束。输入采样进一步通过从有效域的随机子范围内分别以混合均匀分布和截断正态分布进行抽取，以模拟真实的实验条件。利用科尔莫戈罗夫-斯米尔诺夫检验，在费曼方程语料库上对生成的数据进行统计验证。生成的方程在全部八个研究的结构特征上与语料库匹配，而未经平滑的纯概率文法仅匹配两个特征，这表明在给定语料库规模下，贝叶斯先验对于结构保真度至关重要。在下游超参数调优任务中，使用合成数据调优的梯度提升回归器在真实数据上平均从20个配置中选出第6优的配置，与在真实数据上调优的结果相当，并显著优于随机表达式树（第10优）和噪声（第19优）。

</details>


### [115] [TorchKM: A GPU-Oriented Library for Kernel Learning and Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06742)
*Yikai Zhang,Gaoxiang Jia,Jie Ding,Boxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TorchKM是一个开源核机器库，支持SVM、核逻辑回归和核分位数回归，通过GPU加速和智能重用矩阵运算，实现快速训练和模型选择，在保持竞争力的同时显著加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有核机器实现缺乏GPU加速和高效的模型选择机制，训练和超参数调优速度慢，难以应用于大规模数据。

Method: 设计GPU友好的线性代数算子，通过智能重用矩阵运算来加速整个训练和模型选择流程，采用scikit-learn风格API。

Result: 基准测试表明，TorchKM在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，相比标准基线实现了显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: TorchKM通过GPU加速和矩阵运算重用，有效解决了核机器训练和模型选择的效率问题，且易于安装和使用。

Original Abstract: TorchKM is an open-source library for kernel machines, including support vector machines, kernel logistic regression, and kernel quantile regression, with GPU acceleration. The library features a scikit-learn-style API and is designed to exploit GPU-friendly linear algebra, accelerating the full training and model-selection pipeline through intelligent reuse of matrix operations. Benchmarks show competitive predictive performance together with substantial speedups over standard baselines. Code and documentation are available at https://github.com/YikaiZhang95/torchkm, and the package can be easily installed via PyPI.

中文摘要翻译: TorchKM是一个用于核机器的开源库，包括支持向量机、核逻辑回归和核分位数回归，并支持GPU加速。该库采用scikit-learn风格的API，通过利用GPU友好的线性代数以及智能重用矩阵运算，加速了完整的训练和模型选择流程。基准测试表明，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时，相比标准基线实现了显著的加速。代码和文档可在https://github.com/YikaiZhang95/torchkm获取，该包也可通过PyPI轻松安装。

</details>


### [116] [Learn to Match: Two-Sided Matching with Temporally Extended Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06744)
*Haijing Zong,Yancheng Liang,Boyang Zhou,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于动态双边匹配市场的多智能体强化学习基准框架Learn2Match，该框架将匹配过程建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈，支持面试、匹配和解约等时序决策。实验表明独立PPO在累积社会福利和遗憾方面优于bandit基线，但信息摩擦损失更高，揭示了端到端MARL在匹配市场中的优势与不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有双边匹配模型通常将信息交互简化为即时次高斯反馈，忽略了随时间逐步揭示的支付相关信息（如面试、重复互动、学习与分离）对未来匹配决策的影响，缺乏能捕捉这些时序扩展反馈的建模框架和基准。

Method: 将动态双边匹配建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈（POMG），引入时序扩展反馈、昂贵的面试筛选、有噪声的匹配后观测、演化中的潜在特征以及内生延续或解散机制。在此框架下构建Learn2Match基准，支持多智能体强化学习（MARL）决策。

Result: 独立PPO在时序扩展反馈下比bandit风格的CA-ETC基线取得了更高的累积社会福利和更低的累积遗憾；但PPO的信息摩擦损失更高，说明端到端MARL尚未具备匹配bandit方法的协调探索结构。

Conclusion: Learn2Match可作为开发下一代匹配市场算法的基准——这些算法应像RL智能体一样自适应、像bandit算法一样具备统计纪律、像稳定匹配机制一样具有结构意识。

Original Abstract: Two-sided matching markets often involve information that unfolds over time through interviews, repeated interaction, learning, and separation. Existing matching models typically reduce this process to immediate sub-Gaussian feedback about fixed preferences, missing settings where payoff-relevant information is revealed gradually and changes future matching decisions. We introduce a framework with temporally extended feedback, that formulates two-sided matching as a partially observable Markov game with costly pre-match screening, noisy post-match observations, evolving latent profiles, and endogenous continuation or dissolution. We instantiate this framework in Learn2Match, a multi-agent reinforcement-learning benchmark for dynamic matching markets. Learn2Match supports decentralized decision making over whom to interview, whom to match with, and when to dissolve a match, while evaluating policies using regret, social welfare, and an information-friction loss that measures the welfare gap caused by incomplete revelation of latent preferences. We find that independent PPO achieves higher cumulative social welfare and lower cumulative regret than the bandit-style CA-ETC baseline under temporally extended feedback, demonstrating the promise of MARL for dynamic matching markets. However, PPO still incurs higher information-friction loss, revealing that end-to-end MARL does not yet provide the coordinated exploration structure of matching-bandit methods. These results position Learn2Match as a benchmark for developing the next generation of matching-market algorithms: methods that are adaptive like RL agents, statistically disciplined like bandit algorithms, and structurally aware like stable-matching mechanisms.

中文摘要翻译: 双边匹配市场通常涉及通过面试、重复互动、学习和分离随时间展开的信息。现有的匹配模型通常将此过程简化为关于固定偏好的即时次高斯反馈，忽略了支付相关信息逐渐揭示并改变未来匹配决策的场景。我们引入了一个具有时序扩展反馈的框架，将双边匹配建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈，包含昂贵的面试前筛选、有噪声的匹配后观测、演化的潜在特征以及内生的延续或解散机制。我们在Learn2Match中实例化该框架，这是一个用于动态匹配市场的多智能体强化学习基准。Learn2Match支持关于面试谁、与谁匹配以及何时解散匹配的去中心化决策，并使用遗憾、社会福利以及信息摩擦损失（衡量因潜在偏好不完全揭示造成的福利差距）来评估策略。我们发现，在时序扩展反馈下，独立PPO比bandit风格的CA-ETC基线实现了更高的累积社会福利和更低的累积遗憾，展示了MARL在动态匹配市场中的前景。然而，PPO仍产生了更高的信息摩擦损失，这表明端到端MARL尚未提供匹配bandit方法所具有的协调探索结构。这些结果将Learn2Match定位为开发下一代匹配市场算法的基准：这些算法应像RL智能体一样自适应，像bandit算法一样具有统计纪律性，并像稳定匹配机制一样具备结构意识。

</details>


### [117] [Performance Variation in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06746)
*Haruto Tanaka,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出深度强化学习算法逐次运行性能变化大、传统不确定性指标存在局限，提出基于百分位数的Min-Max IPR统计量和逐运行百分位高亮可视化方法，并通过三个案例验证了该方法能有效评估和展示算法性能变化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度强化学习算法在不同运行间存在显著性能变化，但现有研究缺乏有效的评估方法，传统的不确定性估计（如均值置信区间）存在目标错位和低估风险的问题，亟需一种更合适的统计和可视化工具来量化运行间性能变化。

Method: 提出基于样本百分位数性质的两个工具：1）Min-Max IPR（最小-最大百分位距）统计量，用于量化逐运行性能变化；2）逐运行百分位高亮可视化方法，用于直观展示性能分布特征。

Result: 三个案例研究结果：1）LayerNorm和倒数第二层归一化能缩窄PPO的性能变化，但对SAC影响不大；2）TD-MPC在四种算法中性能变化最小且数据效率最高；3）DQN和Rainbow在五个Atari环境中性能变化水平相近。

Conclusion: 基于百分位数的统计和可视化方法易于解释，能提供丰富的运行间性能变化信息，是对传统不确定性估计的有效补充，有助于RL社区更好地理解和报告算法稳定性。

Original Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms often suffer from low run-to-run robustness, manifesting as significant performance variation across independent runs of identically configured agents. Although this issue poses a spectrum of challenges across research and practice, relatively few studies develop methods to evaluate it; RL research instead often reports uncertainty in the estimated mean performance. In this paper, we outline the limitations of conventional uncertainty and variation estimates, particularly their misalignment with purpose and the risk of underreporting. We then propose an alternative percentile-based statistic and visualization method, min-max IPR and run-wise percentile highlighting, respectively. These percentile-based tools are easy to interpret and rely on standard properties of sample percentiles, providing rich information about run-to-run performance variation. We demonstrate this through three case studies. First, we show that LayerNorm and penultimate-layer normalizations narrow performance variation in PPO, whereas the variation is mostly unchanged in SAC. Second, we compare PPO, SAC, TD-MPC, and TD-MPC2, and show TD-MPC exhibits the least variation while being the most data efficient among the four. Finally, in a comparison of DQN and Rainbow on five Atari environments, we show that both algorithms exhibit similar levels of performance variation.

中文摘要翻译: 深度强化学习算法通常存在逐次运行鲁棒性低的问题，表现为相同配置的智能体在独立运行中产生显著的性能差异。尽管这一问题在研究和实践中带来诸多挑战，但专门针对其评估方法的研究却相对较少；强化学习研究通常仅报告估计平均性能的不确定性。本文阐述了传统不确定性和变异估计的局限性，特别是它们与目标的错位以及低估风险。我们随后提出了一种基于百分位数的替代统计量和可视化方法，分别称为Min-Max IPR和逐运行百分位高亮。这些基于百分位数的工具易于解释，依赖于样本百分位数的标准性质，能够提供关于逐运行性能变化的丰富信息。我们通过三个案例研究对此进行了验证。首先，我们发现LayerNorm和倒数第二层归一化缩窄了PPO的性能变化，而SAC中的变化基本不变。其次，我们比较了PPO、SAC、TD-MPC和TD-MPC2，结果表明TD-MPC在四种算法中变化最小且数据效率最高。最后，在五个Atari环境中对DQN和Rainbow的比较中，我们发现两种算法表现出相近的性能变化水平。

</details>


### [118] [A Rolling-Window Framework for Churn Prediction and Behavioral Driver Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06776)
*Muhammad Jawad Mufti,Omar Hammad,Haitham Saleh,Muqaddas Gull*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于滚动行为窗口的时间显式流失预测框架，在非契约式服务环境中实现了持续、实例级的流失风险评估。实验表明，精心设计的时间框架比模型复杂度本身更为关键，特征模型准确率达87.6%、ROC-AUC达0.94，序列模型召回率高达96.1%，且在时移下无需重训仍保持鲁棒性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流失预测方法依赖简化的时间假设或单点行为表征，难以支持持续风险评估、可解释性及实际部署需求。特别是在非契约式按需付费服务环境中，用户流失并未被明确观察，需从行为非活跃中推断，亟需一种时间显式的、支持动态风险评估的流失预测框架。

Method: 提出时间显式流失预测框架：使用固定30天滚动行为窗口汇总用户行为，随后设置30天未来流失评估窗口，确保行为证据与流失结果间存在清晰的时间隔离。框架在统一时间设计下集成了基于特征的学习方法和基于序列的学习方法。

Result: 在大规模真实非契约式服务平台数据集上，特征模型准确率87.6%、ROC-AUC 0.94；序列模型召回率高达96.1%。在未见过的未来数据上，准确率保持在83%以上，ROC-AUC超过0.91，无需模型重训。

Conclusion: 精心设计的时间框架（而非模型复杂度本身）是实现鲁棒、可解释且可部署的流失预测的关键。该研究为动态服务环境中的流失导向决策支持提供了实用基础。

Original Abstract: Customer churn prediction is a central task in customer analytics, particularly in non-contractual, pay-per-use service environments where disengagement is not explicitly observed and must be inferred from behavioral inactivity. Existing churn prediction approaches often rely on simplified temporal assumptions or single-point representations of customer behavior, which limit their ability to support continuous risk assessment, interpretability, and realistic deployment over time. This study proposes a temporally explicit churn prediction framework that models customer behavior using rolling behavioral windows, enabling repeated and instance-level churn risk estimation as customer activity evolves. Customer behavior is summarized within a fixed 30-day observation window, followed by a 30-day future churn evaluation window, ensuring a clear temporal separation between behavioral evidence and churn outcomes. The framework integrates feature-based and sequence-based learning approaches within a unified temporal design. The proposed approach is evaluated on a large-scale, real-world dataset from a non-contractual service platform. Empirical results demonstrate strong and stable predictive performance, with accuracy reaching 87.6% and ROC-AUC of 0.94 for the feature-based model, while the sequence-based model achieves recall as high as 96.1% by capturing temporal disengagement patterns. Evaluation on future unseen data confirms meaningful robustness under temporal shift, with accuracy remaining above 83% and ROC-AUC exceeding 0.91 without model retraining. Overall, the findings highlight that carefully designed temporal framing, rather than model complexity alone, is critical for achieving robust, interpretable, and deployment-ready churn prediction. The study provides a practical foundation for churn-oriented decision support in dynamic service environments.

中文摘要翻译: 客户流失预测是客户分析中的核心任务，尤其是在非契约式、按需付费的服务环境中，用户脱离行为未被明确观测，必须从行为非活跃中推断。现有的流失预测方法通常依赖简化的时间假设或客户行为的单点表征，这限制了它们在持续风险评估、可解释性以及随时间的实际部署方面的能力。本研究提出了一种时间显式的流失预测框架，利用滚动行为窗口对客户行为进行建模，从而能够随着客户活动的演变进行重复且实例级的流失风险估计。客户行为在固定的30天观测窗口内被汇总，随后设置30天的未来流失评估窗口，确保行为证据与流失结果之间存在清晰的时间隔离。该框架在统一的时间设计下集成了基于特征和基于序列的学习方法。所提出的方法在一个大规模、真实的非契约式服务平台数据集上进行了评估。实证结果表明，该方法具有强大且稳定的预测性能：基于特征的模型准确率达到87.6%，ROC-AUC为0.94；而基于序列的模型通过捕获时间维度的脱离模式，召回率高达96.1%。在未见过的未来数据上的评估证实了模型在时间偏移下具有显著的鲁棒性，准确率保持在83%以上，ROC-AUC超过0.91，且无需模型重新训练。总体而言，研究结果强调，精心设计的时间框架（而非仅仅模型复杂度）对于实现鲁棒、可解释且可直接部署的流失预测至关重要。该研究为动态服务环境中的流失导向决策支持提供了实用基础。

</details>


### [119] [Federated Foundation Models over Vehicular Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06786)
*Kasra Borazjani,Fardis Nadimi,Payam Abdisarabshali,Owen Palinski,Allan Salihovic,Dinh Nguyen,Minghui Liwang,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将多模态多任务联邦基础模型（M3T FedFMs）集成到车载网络中的前瞻性愿景，通过案例研究（Waymo Open Dataset）验证了其潜力，并发布了开源实现以促进可复现性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将多模态多任务基础模型（M3T FMs）的表达能力与联邦学习（FL）的隐私保护和分布式学习能力相统一，应用于车载网络，推动下一代车载智能。

Method: 首先介绍M3T FedFMs的基本训练/微调原理，然后讨论其在车载网络中的代表性用例，识别车载环境中的关键约束并提出前瞻性研究方向，最后在Waymo Open Dataset上进行案例研究验证。

Result: 通过在真实车载数据集（Waymo Open Dataset）上的案例研究，展示了M3T FedFMs在车载网络中的巨大潜力，并提供了开源实现代码仓库以促进可复现性和后续研究。

Conclusion: M3T FedFMs有望赋能下一代车载智能，尽管面临车载环境的诸多约束挑战，但通过前瞻性研究方向可逐步解决；该工作为这一新兴领域奠定了初步基础。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a forward-looking vision for integrating the emerging multi-modal multi-task federated foundation models (M3T FedFMs) into vehicular networks, with the goal of unifying the expressive power of multi-modal multi-task foundation models (M3T FMs) with the privacy-preserving and distributed learning capabilities of federated learning (FL). Given the largely underexplored nature of this research direction, we first introduce the fundamental training/fine-tuning principles of M3T FedFMs. We then discuss a range of their representative use cases in vehicular networks, illustrating the significant potential of M3T FedFMs to enable next-generation vehicular intelligence. Afterwards, we identify key constraints inherent to vehicular environments that challenge the practical deployment of M3T FedFMs, and articulate a set of forward-looking research directions to address these challenges. Furthermore, through a case study conducted on a real-world vehicular dataset (i.e., Waymo Open Dataset), we demonstrate the promise of M3T FedFMs for vehicular networks and release our implementation to facilitate reproducibility and stimulate research in this emerging area (repository: https://github.com/KasraBorazjani/vehicular-fedfm)

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一个前瞻性愿景，旨在将新兴的多模态多任务联邦基础模型（M3T FedFMs）集成到车载网络中，其目标是将多模态多任务基础模型（M3T FMs）的强大表达能力与联邦学习（FL）的隐私保护和分布式学习能力相统一。鉴于这一研究方向尚处于很大程度上未被探索的阶段，我们首先介绍了M3T FedFMs的基本训练/微调原理。随后，我们讨论了其在车载网络中的一系列代表性用例，展示了M3T FedFMs在赋能下一代车载智能方面的巨大潜力。接着，我们识别了车载环境固有的关键约束条件，这些条件对M3T FedFMs的实际部署构成了挑战，并阐述了一系列前瞻性研究方向以应对这些挑战。此外，通过在真实车载数据集（即Waymo Open Dataset）上进行的案例研究，我们展示了M3T FedFMs在车载网络中的应用前景，并发布了相关实现代码（仓库地址：https://github.com/KasraBorazjani/vehicular-fedfm），以促进可复现性并推动这一新兴领域的研究。

</details>


### [120] [Interpreting Learning Under Competing Models: Joint and Stepwise Approaches for Dynamic Cognitive Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06804)
*Yawen Ma,Sahoko Ishida,Kate Cain,Gabriel Wallin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在数字学习环境中，当试题-技能结构（Q矩阵）未知时，采用联合估计（Joint analysis）与分步估计（Stepwise analysis）两种不同策略对学习者技能发展结论的影响。通过动态认知诊断模型分析阅读游戏数据发现，两种方法虽然都认为大多数学习者趋向掌握两种技能，但在“部分熟练”学习者比例上存在显著分歧，模拟实验表明当试题-技能结构不确定且试题池随年级变化时，联合分析方法更可靠。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数字学习环境记录了学习者对单个试题的响应，使得研究特定技能的发展成为可能。当每个试题所测量的技能（Q矩阵）未知时，研究者需要决定是联合估计Q矩阵与学习过程，还是先确定Q矩阵再研究学习。这两种决策可能导致不同的实质性结论，因此需要系统比较两种方法的差异与适用条件。

Method: 采用动态认知诊断模型，分析二年级到三年级两个阅读游戏（测量词汇和阅读理解）的数据。利用试题文本嵌入提供Q矩阵先验信息。比较了联合分析（joint analysis）与偏差校正的分步分析（bias-corrected stepwise analysis）两种方法，并通过模拟实验识别两种分析产生分歧的条件。

Result: 1) 两种分析方法都同意大多数学习者趋向同时掌握词汇和理解两种技能；2) 但在三年级时“仅部分熟练”的学习者比例上存在显著分歧，这会影响阅读进展的报告方式；3) 模拟实验表明，当试题-技能结构不确定且试题池随年级变化时，联合分析更可靠。

Conclusion: 在数字学习环境中分析技能发展时，联合估计Q矩阵与学习过程比先确定Q矩阵再分析学习更为可靠，尤其是在试题-技能结构不确定且试题池随年级变化的情况下。研究者应谨慎选择分析策略，因为这一决策会直接影响关于学习者发展的实质性结论。

Original Abstract: Digital learning environments record learners' responses to individual items, making it possible to study the development of specific skills rather than overall scores. Drawing conclusions about learning from these data requires a model that links responses to latent skills and tracks how mastery changes over time. When the skills measured by each item are unknown, the analyst must decide whether to estimate this structure, the Q-matrix, jointly with the learning process, or to establish it first and study learning afterwards. We show that this decision can change substantive conclusions about how learners develop. Using dynamic cognitive diagnostic models, we analyse data from two reading games measuring vocabulary and comprehension from Grade 2 to Grade 3, with item-text embeddings providing prior information for the unknown Q-matrix. A joint analysis and a bias-corrected stepwise analysis agree that most learners move toward mastering both skills, but disagree about how many remain only partially proficient at Grade 3, changing how reading progress would be reported. A simulation study identifies when the two analyses diverge and shows that joint analysis is more reliable when the item-skill structure is uncertain and the item pool changes between grades. We provide R code for both analyses.

中文摘要翻译: 数字学习环境记录了学习者对单个试题的响应，使得研究特定技能的发展而非总体分数成为可能。从这些数据中得出关于学习的结论需要一个将响应与潜在技能联系起来并追踪掌握程度随时间变化的模型。当每个试题所测量的技能未知时，分析者必须决定是联合估计这一结构（Q矩阵）与学习过程，还是先确定Q矩阵再研究学习。我们证明这一决策会改变关于学习者如何发展的实质性结论。利用动态认知诊断模型，我们分析了从二年级到三年级两个测量词汇和理解的阅读游戏数据，并使用试题文本嵌入为未知的Q矩阵提供先验信息。联合分析和偏差校正的分步分析都同意大多数学习者趋向掌握两种技能，但在三年级时有多少人仍然仅部分熟练的问题上存在分歧，从而改变了阅读进展的报告方式。模拟研究识别了两种分析产生分歧的条件，并表明当试题-技能结构不确定且试题池随年级变化时，联合分析更可靠。我们提供了两种分析的R代码。

</details>


### [121] [SCALE: Scalable Cross-Attention Learning with Extrapolation for Agentic Workflow Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06820)
*Zhifei Xu,Jierui Lan,Zixuan Liang,Aiji Liang,Jinxi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCALE，一种基于交叉注意力指针网络和结构化表示正则化的DRL调度器，能在无需微调的情况下泛化到不同规模的集群，在扩展到48节点时相较无正则化版本将平均响应时间降低8.9%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度强化学习调度器固定于特定集群规模，当服务器数量变化时必须重新训练，缺乏跨规模泛化能力。

Method: 采用交叉注意力指针网络架构（任务特征查询服务器特征），天然支持任意数量服务器；提出结构化表示正则化（SRR），包含去相关损失和向标准正态分布的KL散度惩罚，使注意力特征统计量在不同输入规模下保持稳定。

Result: 在16节点上训练，直接测试于32和48节点。在N=48时，SCALE相较无SRR的相同架构将平均响应时间降低8.9%。

Conclusion: 仅靠置换不变性架构不足以保证新规模下的良好性能，显式正则化（SRR）是弥合规模泛化差距的必要手段。

Original Abstract: Agentic Large Language Model (LLM) systems decompose complex tasks into workflow Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) whose primitives must be scheduled on heterogeneous clusters. Existing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) schedulers are tied to a fixed cluster size and require retraining whenever the number of servers changes. We propose SCALE (Scalable Cross-Attention Learning with Extrapolation), a DRL scheduler that generalizes to unseen cluster scales without fine-tuning. SCALE employs a cross-attention pointer network where task features query against server features, so the architecture accepts any number of servers by construction. We observe, however, that permutation-invariant architecture alone does not guarantee good performance at new scales - the attention feature undergoes distribution shift as the server count grows. To counter this, we introduce Structured Representation Regularization (SRR): a decorrelation loss combined with a KL penalty toward the standard normal, which keeps feature statistics stable regardless of input size. Trained on 16 nodes and tested directly on 32 and 48 nodes, SCALE reduces average response time by 8.9% at N=48 relative to the same architecture without SRR, confirming that explicit regularization is necessary to close the scale-generalization gap.
