中文摘要翻译: 自编码器（AE）通过将数据映射到潜在空间并最小化重构误差来学习低维表示。尽管在经验上取得了成功，但其理论理解仍然有限，且大多局限于线性模型或无瓶颈设置。本文在均值场（MF）框架下研究了具有固定有限维瓶颈的非线性自编码器。我们推导了编码器和解码器的显式MF学习动力学，为非线性设置下的训练提供了易于处理的特征描述。我们证明，在有限时间范围内，使用随机梯度下降训练的有限宽度网络的经验风险以高概率紧密跟踪MF风险轨迹。在最优性条件下，我们进一步证明有限宽度风险收敛到MF最优值，表明有限网络具有足够的表达能力来逼近无限宽度解。

</details>


### [152] [Learning Explicit Behavioral Models with Adaptive Questions and World-Model Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07127)
*Hikaru Shindo,Yu Deng,Teng Cao,Quentin Delfosse,Christopher Tauchmann,Jannis Blüml,Gopika Sudhakaran,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出显式符号行为模型（ESBM），通过将任务性能与基于证据的问答和可执行机制预测相结合，使强化学习智能体在学习高回报策略的同时产生可解释的行为机制表示。实验表明，自适应问题既可作为训练压力，也可作为机理策略学习的可复用基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有仅基于任务回报训练的交互式智能体虽然能获得高分，但无法表示其动作成功背后的机制，导致行为脆弱、难以诊断，且在环境动态变化时适应性有限。现有的LLM反思和策略代码修复方法虽然能从失败轨迹中修正行为，但问题与世界理解测试通常仅在训练后使用，缺乏将机制学习融入训练过程的方案。

Method: ESBM通过类型化谓词、加权规则、有界选项和机制记忆来表示行为；机制层预测在动作干预下的符号事件、对象变化、奖励和终止结果。每次回滚后，自适应问题和主动世界模型探针将得分失败、QA错误和转移预测错误转化为局部ESBM编辑的约束。候选模型通过多准则规则（联合评估任务得分、可回答性和主动世界模型一致性）进行选择。

Result: 在Atari风格协议下的测试表明，ESBM能够学习高分策略，同时生成显式答案和可执行的机制预测。

Conclusion: 自适应问题可以作为训练压力和可复用基准，用于该设置下的机理策略学习。

Original Abstract: Interactive agents trained only against task return can achieve high scores while failing to represent the mechanisms that make their actions succeed. This makes brittle behavior difficult to diagnose and limits adaptation when environment dynamics change. Existing LLM reflection and policy-code repair can revise behavior from failed trajectories, but questions and world-understanding tests are usually used only after training. We introduce an Explicit Symbolic Behavioral Model (ESBM), a trainable behavioral model that couples task performance with evidence-grounded question answering and executable mechanism prediction. An ESBM represents behavior through typed predicates, weighted rules, bounded options and mechanism memory; the mechanism layer predicts symbolic events, object changes, rewards and terminal consequences under action interventions. After each rollout, adaptive questions and active world-model probes convert score failures, QA errors and transition-prediction errors into constraints for local ESBM edits. Candidate models are selected by a multi-criterion rule that jointly evaluates task score, answerability and active world-model consistency. Under the tested Atari-style protocols, ESBM learns high-scoring policies while producing explicit answers and executable mechanism predictions, indicating that adaptive questions can serve as both training pressure and reusable benchmarks for mechanistic policy learning in this setting.

中文摘要翻译: 仅基于任务回报训练的交互式智能体可以获得高分，但无法表示其动作成功背后的机制。这导致行为脆弱且难以诊断，并在环境动态变化时限制了适应性。现有的LLM反思和策略代码修复方法可以从失败轨迹中修正行为，但问题与世界理解测试通常仅在训练后使用。我们提出了一种显式符号行为模型（ESBM），这是一种可训练的行为模型，将任务性能与基于证据的问答和可执行机制预测相结合。ESBM通过类型化谓词、加权规则、有界选项和机制记忆来表示行为；机制层在动作干预下预测符号事件、对象变化、奖励和终止结果。每次回滚后，自适应问题和主动世界模型探针将得分失败、QA错误和转移预测错误转化为局部ESBM编辑的约束。候选模型通过多准则规则进行选择，该规则联合评估任务得分、可回答性和主动世界模型一致性。在测试的Atari风格协议下，ESBM在学习高分策略的同时产生显式答案和可执行的机制预测，表明在该设置中，自适应问题既可以作为训练压力，也可以作为机理策略学习的可复用基准。

</details>


### [153] [A machine-learning-assisted progressive digit-randomness screening framework for detecting non-random patterns in raw numerical research data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07128)
*Zhuphua Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FDRS（制造风险数字随机性筛查模型），一个结合统计与机器学习的框架，用于检测数值科研数据中的非随机数字模式异常，实验表明该模型能有效区分正常数据与人为操纵数据，并支持分级风险筛查。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 原始数值数据集在科研诚信审查中受到的关注远少于图像、抄袭或统计摘要不一致等问题，亟需系统化的筛查工具来检测数值数据中可能存在的伪造或操纵迹象。

Method: FDRS整合了单小数位与联合小数位检验、Cramer's V、熵度量、KL散度、数字偏好指数、渐进子采样以及半监督风险评分，构建了一个统计与机器学习相结合的检测框架。

Result: 在内部验证中，Elastic-net逻辑回归取得最高AUC（0.98395）和最低Brier分数（0.048439），随机森林取得最高准确率（0.926667）和平衡准确率（0.935）。RawData被评为0级（低风险），ErrData被评为3级（高风险）。外部真实数据集验证支持分级风险分层。

Conclusion: FDRS可作为辅助性的数字结构筛查工具，用于对原始数值数据集进行优先级排序以进一步审查，但并非数据伪造或学术不端的独立证据。

Original Abstract: Raw numerical datasets remain less systematically examined in integrity screening than images, plagiarism, or summary-statistic inconsistencies. We developed the Fabrication-risk Digit Randomness Screening model (FDRS), a statistical and machine-learning framework for detecting non-random digit-pattern irregularities in numerical research data. FDRS integrates single- and joint-decimal-digit tests, Cramer's V, entropy metrics, Kullback-Leibler divergence, digit-preference indices, progressive subsampling, and semi-supervised risk scoring. It was evaluated using an instrument-derived enzymatic absorbance dataset (RawData, n=253) and a blinded manually simulated irregular dataset (ErrData, n=255). RawData showed no significant deviation in single third-decimal-digit analysis, whereas ErrData showed a significant deviation. In joint third-fourth decimal digit analysis, ErrData showed higher Cramer's V, lower normalized entropy, higher KL divergence, and a more persistent progressive-subsampling deviation signal. In internal validation, Elastic-net Logistic Regression achieved the highest AUC (0.98395) and lowest Brier score (0.048439), while Random Forest achieved the highest accuracy (0.926667) and balanced accuracy (0.935). RawData received a low ensemble risk score of 0.124627 and was classified as Grade 0; ErrData received a score of 0.740760 and was classified as Grade 3. External real-world benchmarks supported graded risk stratification: three datasets without identified public post-publication concerns were classified as Grade 0 or 1, whereas two datasets from publicly questioned or institutionally handled articles were classified as Grade 2 or 3. FDRS can prioritize raw numerical datasets for further review by integrating interpretable statistical and machine-learning features. It is an auxiliary digit-structure screening tool, not standalone evidence of fabrication or misconduct.

中文摘要翻译: 在科研诚信筛查中，原始数值数据集受到的系统化审查远少于图像、抄袭或统计摘要不一致等问题。我们开发了制造风险数字随机性筛查模型（FDRS），这是一个用于检测数值科研数据中非随机数字模式异常的统计与机器学习框架。FDRS整合了单小数位与联合小数位检验、Cramer's V、熵度量、KL散度、数字偏好指数、渐进子采样以及半监督风险评分。该模型使用一个来自仪器测定的酶吸光度数据集（RawData，n=253）和一个盲法人工模拟的不规则数据集（ErrData，n=255）进行评估。RawData在单第三小数位分析中未显示显著偏差，而ErrData显示显著偏差。在第三、第四小数位联合分析中，ErrData显示出更高的Cramer's V值、更低的归一化熵、更高的KL散度以及更持久的渐进子采样偏差信号。在内部验证中，Elastic-net逻辑回归取得了最高AUC（0.98395）和最低Brier分数（0.048439），随机森林取得了最高准确率（0.926667）和平衡准确率（0.935）。RawData获得了0.124627的低集成风险评分，被分类为0级；ErrData获得了0.740760的评分，被分类为3级。外部真实世界基准数据集支持分级风险分层：三个未发现公开发表后问题的数据集被分类为0级或1级，而两个来自公开发表被质疑或机构处理文章的数据集被分类为2级或3级。FDRS通过整合可解释的统计与机器学习特征，能够对原始数值数据集进行优先级排序以进行进一步审查。它是一个辅助性的数字结构筛查工具，而非数据伪造或学术不端的独立证据。

</details>


### [154] [$α$-PFN: Fast Entropy Search via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07134)
*Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Steven Adriaensen,Tom Viering,Carl Hvarfner,Samuel Müller,Frank Hutter,Eytan Bakshy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段摊销策略，用Prior-data Fitted Networks (PFNs)学习逼近熵搜索采集函数，将复杂启发式近似替换为单次前向传播，实现50倍以上加速且性能与SOTA相当。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 熵搜索等基于信息论的采集函数在贝叶斯优化中原理优美，但实际依赖复杂缓慢的蒙特卡洛近似，引入数值误差且需专门手工实现，效率低下。

Method: 两阶段摊销策略：第一阶段训练PFN使其能够以最优值信息为条件；第二阶段训练α-PFN，通过在第一阶段PFN上测量信息增益来预测期望信息增益，从而将复杂启发式近似替换为每个候选点单次前向传播。

Result: 在合成和真实基准上，该方法与最先进的熵搜索实现性能相当，同时加速了所有实验中不同熵搜索变体的计算，速度提升超过50倍。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段摊销策略能够高效、可扩展地逼近熵搜索采集函数，大幅降低计算开销同时保持竞争性能，为贝叶斯优化中信息论采集函数的高效实现提供了新路径。

Original Abstract: Information-theoretic acquisition functions such as Entropy Search (ES) offer a principled exploration-exploitation framework for Bayesian optimization (BO). However, their practical implementation relies on complicated and slow approximations, i.e., a Monte Carlo estimation of the information gain. This complexity can introduce numerical errors and requires specialized, hand-crafted implementations. We propose a two-stage amortization strategy that learns to approximate entropy search-based acquisition functions using Prior-data Fitted Networks (PFNs) in a single forward pass. A first PFN is trained to be conditioned on information about the optima; second, the $α$-PFN is trained to predict the expected information gain by training on information gains measured with the first PFN. The $α$-PFN offers a flexible learned approximation, which replaces the complex heuristic approximations with a single forward pass per candidate, enabling rapid and extensible acquisition evaluation. Empirically, our approach is competitive with state-of-the-art entropy search implementations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks, while accelerating the different entropy search variants across all our experiments, with speed ups over 50x. Source code: https://github.com/automl/AlphaPFN.

中文摘要翻译: 信息论采集函数（如熵搜索）为贝叶斯优化提供了原则性的探索-利用框架。然而，它们的实际实现依赖于复杂且缓慢的近似，即信息增益的蒙特卡洛估计。这种复杂性可能引入数值误差，并需要专门的、手工精心设计的实现。我们提出了一种两阶段摊销策略，利用Prior-data Fitted Networks (PFNs) 在单次前向传播中学习逼近基于熵搜索的采集函数。第一个PFN被训练为以最优值信息为条件；随后，α-PFN通过训练在第一个PFN上测量的信息增益来预测期望信息增益。α-PFN提供了一种灵活的学习近似，将复杂的启发式近似替换为每个候选点的单次前向传播，从而实现快速且可扩展的采集评估。在实验中，我们的方法在合成和真实基准上与最先进的熵搜索实现性能相当，同时在所有实验中加速了不同的熵搜索变体，速度提升超过50倍。源代码：https://github.com/automl/AlphaPFN。

</details>


### [155] [Explaining Unsupervised Disease Staging in Huntington's Disease: Insights into Model Representations and Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07135)
*Lubna Mahmoud Abu Zohair,Hind Zantout*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对亨廷顿病（HD）进展建模中无监督机器学习可解释性不足的问题，通过在已有疾病分期框架上应用可解释性分析（包括低维投影、显著性图、SHAP等），验证了学习到的特征表示和聚类结果与临床指标高度一致，揭示了从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖的疾病阶段分层。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 无监督机器学习方法能够从纵向数据中发现疾病进展轨迹和有意义的潜在阶段，但其可解释性有限，限制了临床信任和转化应用。因此，本文旨在通过可解释性分析增强对已有疾病分期框架的理解和信任。

Method: 首先将学习到的特征表示投影到低维空间，直观评估聚类结果是否与临床指标进展一致；然后使用显著性图识别随时间变化对嵌入贡献最大的临床特征；最后训练代理分类器并应用SHAP量化特征对聚类分配的重要性，分析驱动疾病阶段转换的临床变量。

Result: 可解释性分析表明，学习到的嵌入捕捉到了具有临床意义的疾病结构，与已知的运动和功能严重程度评分一致，聚类间呈现渐进性恶化趋势。SHAP揭示了从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖的疾病阶段分层，与已知临床进展模式一致，同时揭示了阶段内的变异性。

Conclusion: 本文的可解释性分析验证了无监督机器学习方法在亨廷顿病进展建模中的临床有效性，增强了模型的透明度和可信度，为临床翻译提供了支撑。

Original Abstract: Huntington's disease (HD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder that affects motor, cognitive, and behavioral functions, where accurate characterization of disease progression remains essential to improve patient outcome and quality of life. Unsupervised machine learning (ML) approaches have demonstrated the ability to uncover disease progression trajectories and meaningful latent stages from longitudinal data; however, their limited interpretability restricts clinical trust and translation. We extend a previously proposed ML-based disease staging framework by applying an explainability analysis to the extracted feature representations and discovered disease stages. Applied to the Enroll-HD dataset, we first project the learned representations into a lower-dimensional space to intuitively assess whether the resulting clusters align with the progression of established clinical measures. We then use saliency maps to identify the clinical features that most strongly contribute to the learned embeddings over time. Finally, we train a surrogate classifier and apply SHAP to quantify feature importance for cluster assignments and to analyze which clinical variables drive transitions between disease stages. The explainability analysis indicates that the learned embeddings capture clinically meaningful disease structure, aligning with established motor and functional severity scores and exhibiting progressive deterioration across clusters. Within this analysis, SHAP reveals a stratification of disease stages, ranging from early cognitive-motor impairment to severe functional dependency, consistent with known clinical progression patterns, while also highlighting intra-stage variability.

中文摘要翻译: 亨廷顿病（HD）是一种进行性神经退行性疾病，影响运动、认知和行为功能，准确表征疾病进展对于改善患者预后和生活质量至关重要。无监督机器学习（ML）方法已展现出从纵向数据中发现疾病进展轨迹和有意义的潜在阶段的能力；然而，其有限的可解释性限制了临床信任和转化。本文通过将可解释性分析应用于提取到的特征表示和发现的疾病阶段，扩展了先前提出的基于ML的疾病分期框架。在Enroll-HD数据集上，我们首先将学习到的表示投影到低维空间，以直观评估所得聚类是否与已建立的临床指标的进展一致；然后使用显著性图识别随时间变化对所学嵌入贡献最大的临床特征；最后训练一个代理分类器，应用SHAP量化特征对聚类分配的重要性，并分析哪些临床变量驱动疾病阶段之间的转换。可解释性分析表明，学习到的嵌入捕捉到了具有临床意义的疾病结构，与已知的运动和功能严重程度评分一致，并且在聚类间呈现出渐进性恶化趋势。在该分析中，SHAP揭示了疾病阶段的分层，范围从早期认知-运动障碍到严重功能依赖，与已知的临床进展模式一致，同时也突出了阶段内的变异性。

</details>


### [156] [REMEDI: A Benchmark for Retention and Unlearning Evaluation in Multi-label Clinical Disease Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07141)
*Anurag Sharma,Sai Teja Chunchu,Prasenjit Mitra,Sandipan Sikdar,Koustav Rudra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了REMEDI基准，一个专门用于评估医学领域（特别是临床疾病推理任务）机器遗忘方法的基准，解决了现有基准局限于非医学领域和合成数据的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器遗忘方法主要针对非医学领域，且评估基准多使用合成数据集，无法真实反映在医学领域（涉及敏感数据、标签相关性、纵向结构和安全约束）的实际效果。

Method: 基于MIMIC-III临床数据库构建了REMEDI基准，涵盖多标签和多类别的临床疾病推理任务，包含多样化的遗忘实例设置和全面的评估指标（兼顾效用性和遗忘程度）。

Result: 实验表明现有遗忘方法在效用性和遗忘性能之间存在权衡，且大多不适用于多标签分类任务。

Conclusion: REMEDI为医学领域的机器遗忘提供了标准化评估基准，揭示了现有方法的局限性，并公开了基准以促进可重复性研究。

Original Abstract: Language models trained for clinical disease inference are trained on patient data, which may include sensitive and private information, and data owners may request the removal of their data from a trained model due to privacy or copyright concerns. However, exactly unlearning patient-specific data is intractable, and retraining with minor data removal is resource-intensive. While there exists several machine unlearning methods that can be used, their utility is generally restricted to non-medical domains. Moreover, the existing benchmarks for evaluating such unlearning methods primarily utilize synthetically curated datasets, which are not truly representative of real-world systems. Hence, the effectiveness of these unlearning methods in the medical domain is largely unclear. To this end, we introduce REMEDI, an extensive benchmark for machine unlearning tailored to multi-label and multiclass clinical disease inference, where label correlations, longitudinal structure, and safety constraints make unlearning particularly challenging. Unlike the existing benchmarks, REMEDI considers: (1) a relevant application domain (medical), (2) comprehensive unlearning setups involving diverse sets of forget instances, (3) challenging unlearning scenarios including multi-label and multi-class classification tasks, and (4) evaluation metrics involving performance both in terms of utility and extent of unlearning achieved. REMEDI is developed using the MIMIC-III clinical database that contains comprehensive clinical data of patients. Experiments with existing unlearning methods indicate that there exists a trade-off between utility and unlearning performance. They are also largely unsuited to multi-label classification tasks. To facilitate reproducibility, we make our benchmark publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 用于临床疾病推理的语言模型在患者数据上进行训练，这些数据可能包含敏感和隐私信息，数据所有者可能出于隐私或版权原因要求从训练模型中删除其数据。然而，精确遗忘患者特定数据是难以处理的，而针对少量数据删除进行重新训练则资源消耗巨大。虽然存在多种可用的机器遗忘方法，但其效用通常局限于非医学领域。此外，评估此类遗忘方法的现有基准主要使用合成策划的数据集，这些数据集不能真实代表实际系统。因此，这些遗忘方法在医学领域的效果在很大程度上尚不明确。为此，我们提出了REMEDI，一个针对多标签和多类别临床疾病推理任务量身定制的广泛机器遗忘基准，其中标签相关性、纵向结构和安全约束使得遗忘任务尤其具有挑战性。与现有基准不同，REMEDI考虑了：(1) 相关的应用领域（医学），(2) 涉及多样化遗忘实例集合的全面遗忘设置，(3) 具有挑战性的遗忘场景，包括多标签和多类别分类任务，以及(4) 涉及效用性和遗忘程度两方面性能的评估指标。REMEDI使用包含全面临床患者数据的MIMIC-III临床数据库开发。对现有遗忘方法的实验表明，效用性和遗忘性能之间存在权衡，且它们大多不适合多标签分类任务。为促进可重复性，我们公开了本基准。

</details>


### [157] [Decision-Aware Evaluation of Physics-Informed Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07146)
*Daniel Cieślak,Andrzej Czyżewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了pinn-gym基准测试，用于评估物理信息机器学习代理在材料晶格设计中的决策能力，而不仅仅是曲线拟合精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有物理信息机器学习评估过于依赖曲线误差，而工程实际应用依赖于下游决策（如排序候选、避免不可行设计、限制遗憾值），需要更贴近实际应用的评估方式。

Method: 构建了pinn-gym开源基准测试，包含一个透明的降阶碰撞冲击Oracle、五种可打印聚合物材料卡、无量纲力响应目标，以及涵盖曲线保真度、物理可容许性、Top-K检索和质量遗憾值的评估协议。

Result: 低nRMSE通常不足以识别有用的设计选择；物理信息损失改变了不同指标间的权衡而非单调改进所有指标；无量纲条件化提高了可比性但未使迁移变得对称。

Conclusion: pinn-gym提供了一个可复现的测试平台，用于将PIML代理评估为决策系统而非仅仅是曲线预测器。

Original Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning is often assessed by curve error, although engineering use depends on downstream decisions: ranking candidates, avoiding infeasible designs and limiting regret. We introduce pinn-gym, an open benchmark for material-conditioned lattice design that couples a transparent reduced-order crush-and-impact oracle with five printable polymer cards, dimensionless force-response targets and a protocol spanning curve fidelity, physical admissibility, top-k retrieval and mass regret.
  Across per-material, pooled and cross-material settings, low nRMSE is frequently insufficient to identify useful design selections. Physics-informed losses alter trade-offs rather than monotonically improving all metrics, and dimensionless conditioning improves comparability without making transfer symmetric. The benchmark is not a certified material model; within the released oracle, candidate generator and material cards, pinn-gym provides a reproducible testbed for evaluating PIML surrogates as decision systems rather than curve predictors alone.

中文摘要翻译: 物理信息机器学习通常通过曲线误差来评估，尽管工程应用取决于下游决策：候选排序、避免不可行设计以及限制遗憾值。我们引入了pinn-gym，一个面向材料条件化晶格设计的开源基准测试，它将一个透明的降阶碰撞冲击Oracle与五种可打印聚合物材料卡、无量纲力响应目标以及一个涵盖曲线保真度、物理可容许性、Top-K检索和质量遗憾值的协议相结合。在按材料、混合材料和跨材料设置中，低nRMSE通常不足以识别有用的设计选择。物理信息损失改变了权衡关系，而非单调地改进所有指标；无量纲条件化提高了可比性，但并未使迁移变得对称。该基准测试并非经过认证的材料模型；在已发布的Oracle、候选生成器和材料卡框架内，pinn-gym为将PIML代理作为决策系统（而非仅仅是曲线预测器）进行评估提供了一个可复现的测试平台。

</details>


### [158] [Geodesics of Dynamic Graphs for Regime Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07151)
*William Cappelletti,Étienne Voutaz,Pascal Frossard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在动态网络中定义并检测演化状态之间的变化——将状态视为时序图沿测地线的轨迹，利用图回归方法估计累积距离并结合变点检测算法，在合成和真实数据上取得了更优结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统变点检测假设网络在平稳状态间发生突变，忽略了真实场景（如社交网络、物理系统）中广泛存在的连续演化现象，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将状态定义为时序图在合适图空间中沿测地线的轨迹；利用图回归方法计算观测图序列与其端点间估计测地线的累积距离，再结合变点检测算法识别动力学中的显著漂移。

Result: 在动态网络合成数据（含变化轨迹和变速场景）上超越现有变点检测模型；在新冠疫情移动数据上，检测到的变点比基线方法更与外部事件对齐。

Conclusion: 本文首次在图空间中建模并检测演化状态之间的变化，为分析复杂时序图数据提供了更现实且强有力的工具。

Original Abstract: Traditional change point detection in dynamic networks assumes abrupt transitions between stationary states, overlooking scenarios of continuous evolution which arise in most real-world applications, such as social networks or physical systems. We address this gap by formally defining regimes as periods of coherent dynamics in temporal graphs, which we characterize as trajectories along geodesics in a suitably defined graph space. This original perspective allows us to define regime changes as significant drifts in dynamics, either toward new trajectories or with pace changes. We leverage graph regression methods to measure the cumulative distance of sequences of observed graphs from the estimated geodesics between their endpoints, in the relevant graph space, which we can combine with change point detection algorithms. We present experiments on dynamic networks, with changing trajectories and varying speeds, in which we outperform state of the art change point detection models. Then, we analyse mobility data during the Covid-19 pandemic, and show that our assumptions on regular network evolution lead to change points that are more aligned to external events compared to the outcomes of baseline methods. Our work is the first to model and detect changes between evolving regimes in graph space, providing a realistic and powerful tool for analyzing complex temporal graph data.

中文摘要翻译: 传统动态网络中的变点检测假设稳态之间存在突变，忽略了大多数真实应用场景（如社交网络或物理系统）中存在的连续演化。我们通过将状态形式化为时序图中具有一致动力学的时段来填补这一空白，并将其表征为在适当定义的图空间中沿测地线的轨迹。这一原创视角使得我们能够将状态变化定义为动力学中的显著漂移，要么朝向新轨迹，要么发生速度变化。我们利用图回归方法，在相关图空间中测量观测图序列与其端点间估计测地线的累积距离，并可与变点检测算法相结合。我们在动态网络（包含变化轨迹和变速场景）上的实验中超越了现有最优变点检测模型。接着，我们分析了新冠疫情期间的移动数据，并表明我们对规则网络演化的假设所检测到的变点相较于基线方法的结果更与外部事件对齐。本文首次在图空间中建模并检测演化状态之间的变化，为分析复杂时序图数据提供了现实且强有力的工具。

</details>


### [159] [RETROSPECT: RETROsynthesis via Sequential Prediction, and Chemically Transformed-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07181)
*Raja Sekhar Pappala,Shreyas Vinaya Sathyanarayana,Ronit Kumar Choudhary,Arjun Verma,Deepak Warrier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RETROSPECT系统，将逆合成预测分解为“提议-排序”两阶段：ChemAlign Transformer生成候选路线，LambdaMART重排序器基于结构特征、反应模板、上游分数和DFT描述符进行筛选。在USPTO-50K数据集上，生成器top-1准确率55.00%，重排序后提升至59.4%，验证了模块化逆合成设计思路。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单步逆合成预测不仅需要准确的首选推荐，还需要包含足够候选路径以供下游筛选。现有方法往往偏重单模型端到端预测或仅关注重排序，未能将两者有效结合，缺乏模块化、可复用的分解方案。

Method: 采用提议-选择分解架构：(1)  ChemAlign Transformer作为提议生成模型，使用混合根对齐与随机SMILES增强、Pre-LayerNorm、绑定嵌入、指数移动平均权重以及可微分原子平衡辅助损失进行训练；(2)  LambdaMART重排序器，基于结构特征、反应模板统计、上游提议分数以及可选的DFT描述符对候选路线进行排序。

Result: 在USPTO-50K测试集（5,007个反应）上，生成器top-1准确率55.00%，top-10准确率86.18%，top-1有效性99.86%；在合并候选池基准（每产品约111个候选）上，LambdaMART重排序后top-1达到59.4%，MRR为0.7171。消融实验表明上游提议分数和模板频率统计贡献了主要重排序信号，DFT特征增益较小且不稳定。

Conclusion: 更强的单模型提议与学习型候选选择具有互补性，生成模型可作为RetroChimera等集成系统的即插即用组件，支持逆合成预测的模块化设计。

Original Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis needs both accurate first-ranked suggestions and candidate lists that are rich enough for downstream selection. We study this as a proposal-selection decomposition. Our system, RETROSPECT, combines a single Transformer proposal model, which we call the ChemAlign Transformer, with a LambdaMART reranker over structural, reaction-template, upstream-score, and optional DFT-derived descriptors. The generator is trained with hybrid root-aligned and random SMILES augmentation, Pre-LayerNorm, tied embeddings, exponential moving average weights, and a differentiable atom-balance auxiliary loss. On the full USPTO-50K test set of 5,007 reactions, the generator reaches 55.00% top-1 and 86.18% top-10 exact-match accuracy with 99.86% top-1 validity. On the merged candidate-pool benchmark used for reranking, which contains 5,007 test products and about 111 candidates per product, a LambdaMART model trained on the structural feature set reaches 59.4% top-1 with 0.7171 mean reciprocal rank. Feature ablations show that upstream proposal score and template-frequency statistics provide most of the reranking signal, while DFT and reaction-center DFT features provide smaller and less consistent gains. These results support a modular view of retrosynthesis: stronger single-model proposal and learned candidate selection are complementary, and the proposal model can serve as a drop-in component for ensemble systems such as RetroChimera (Maziarz et al., 2024)

中文摘要翻译: 单步逆合成预测既需要准确的首选推荐，也需要包含足够候选路径以供下游筛选。我们将此问题分解为提议-选择两个阶段。我们的系统RETROSPECT将单一的Transformer提议模型（我们称之为ChemAlign Transformer）与LambdaMART重排序器相结合，该重排序器基于结构特征、反应模板统计、上游分数以及可选的DFT衍生描述符进行排序。生成模型采用混合根对齐与随机SMILES增强、Pre-LayerNorm、绑定嵌入、指数移动平均权重以及可微分原子平衡辅助损失进行训练。在包含5,007个反应的完整USPTO-50K测试集上，生成模型达到了55.00%的top-1准确率和86.18%的top-10精确匹配准确率，top-1有效性为99.86%。在用于重排序的合并候选池基准上（包含5,007个测试产物，每个产物约111个候选），基于结构特征集训练的LambdaMART模型达到了59.4%的top-1准确率和0.7171的平均倒数排名。特征消融实验表明，上游提议分数和模板频率统计贡献了大部分重排序信号，而DFT和反应中心DFT特征带来的增益较小且不一致。这些结果支持逆合成预测的模块化观点：更强的单模型提议与学习型候选选择具有互补性，且该提议模型可作为RetroChimera（Maziarz等人，2024）等集成系统的即插即用组件。

</details>


### [160] [Structure-Preserving Correction Learning for Sparse Bayesian Inference in Brain Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07196)
*Marco Morik,Xiao Ruiting,Shinichi Nakajima,Stefan Haufe,Ismail Huseynov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过学习更新机制本身来改进经典稀疏Type-II贝叶斯M/EEG脑成像方法，将联合超参数学习求解器展开为可训练神经网络架构，保留贝叶斯结构的同时引入结构化修正学习（从可学习偏置到自适应MLP和注意力机制），在不取代模型推理的前提下提升重建性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典稀疏Type-II贝叶斯方法依赖固定的迭代更新规则，其动态过程无法从数据中自适应调整，限制了重建性能的提升潜力。

Method: 将经典联合超参数学习求解器展开（unfolding）为可训练的神经网络架构，各层模拟原始迭代过程。通过从可学习偏置到自适应MLP和注意力机制等渐进式表达能力更强的修正学习机制，学习结构化修正项，同时保留原始贝叶斯更新动态的可解释性和模型基础。

Result: 实验结果表明，学习得到的修正变体在保持算法透明性的前提下，相比基线展开求解器在重建性能和收敛行为上均有所提升。

Conclusion: 结构化修正学习能够在不取代原始基于模型推理机制的前提下，改善经验重建性能，实现了可解释性与性能提升之间的有效平衡。

Original Abstract: Classical sparse Type-II Bayesian methods for M/EEG brain imaging support joint estimation of source and noise hyperparameters, but rely on fixed iterative update rules. Although these updates are principled and interpretable, their dynamics cannot be adapted from data. We propose to learn the update mechanism itself while preserving the underlying Bayesian structure by unfolding a classical joint hyperparameter-learning solver into a trainable neural architecture whose layers mirror the original iterations. The resulting framework is initialized to recover the classical solver exactly before training and is enriched through progressively more expressive correction-learning mechanisms, ranging from learnable biases to adaptive MLP and attention-based contextual refinements. In this way, training does not replace Bayesian inference with a black-box predictor, but instead learns structured correction terms while retaining the interpretability and model-based character of the original update dynamics. Structured correction learning therefore aims to improve empirical reconstruction performance without replacing the original model-based inference mechanism. Experimental results show that the learned correction variants improve reconstruction performance and convergence behavior over the baseline unfolded solver while preserving its algorithmic transparency.

中文摘要翻译: 用于M/EEG脑成像的经典稀疏Type-II贝叶斯方法支持源超参数和噪声超参数的联合估计，但依赖于固定的迭代更新规则。尽管这些更新具有原理性和可解释性，但其动态过程无法从数据中自适应调整。本文提出通过学习更新机制本身来改进这一过程，同时保留底层贝叶斯结构：将经典联合超参数学习求解器展开为可训练的神经架构，其各层模拟原始迭代过程。由此产生的框架在训练前被初始化为精确恢复经典求解器，并通过渐进式表达能力更强的修正学习机制（从可学习偏置到自适应MLP和基于注意力的上下文细化）得到增强。这样一来，训练并非用黑箱预测器取代贝叶斯推断，而是学习结构化修正项，同时保留原始更新动态的可解释性和基于模型的特征。因此，结构化修正学习旨在不取代原始基于模型推理机制的前提下，改善经验重建性能。实验结果表明，学习得到的修正变体在保持算法透明性的同时，相比基线展开求解器在重建性能和收敛行为上均有所提升。

</details>


### [161] [DEFINED: A Data-Efficient Computational Framework for Fine-Grained Creativity Assessment in Debate Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07226)
*Tongzhou Yu,Mingjia Li,Hong Qian,Wenkai Wang,Zongbao Zhang,Yaoyu Jiang,Xiangfeng Wang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DEFINED框架，利用层级八维指标系统和预训练语言模型，从辩论场景中实现数据高效的细粒度创造力评估，在真实数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有创造力评估依赖标准化简单任务和昂贵的人工评价，缺乏在复杂开放环境（如辩论）中细粒度、数据高效的自动化评估方法。

Method: DEFINED框架：设计层级八维指标系统定义辩论创造力；使用带层级评分头的预训练自回归语言模型；采用约束数据增强策略解决精英偏差；采用混合粒度训练策略从有限细粒度标注中学习。

Result: 在真实辩论数据集上，DEFINED实现了准确稳定的评分，优于基于提示的大语言模型评估器和现有辩论评分方法；通过包含辩论新手参与者的实证研究验证了生态效度。

Conclusion: DEFINED框架能够在复杂辩论场景中实现数据高效的细粒度创造力评估，具备良好的生态效度和实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Human creativity has emerged as a critical competency in the era of large language models. Assessing creativity in complex, open-ended environments is a grand challenge in data mining, currently hindered by a reliance on standardized simple tasks and the scarcity of fine-grained expert data. As an ecologically valid assessment context, debate reflects multiple dimensions of creativity, encompassing both divergent thinking and convergent thinking. Moreover, debate is a data-rich domain, with a large volume of publicly accessible materials. Current mainstream automated scoring methods are poorly suited to complex settings such as debate, and therefore still rely on costly human evaluation. To this end, this paper proposes DEFINED, a data-efficient computational framework for fine-grained creativity assessment in debate scenarios. DEFINED operationalizes debate creativity through a hierarchical eight-dimensional metric system, implemented via a pre-trained autoregressive language model with a hierarchical scoring head that supports both fine-grained and coarse-grained evaluation. Statements and their associated expert scores were obtained from authentic debate competitions, and a constrained data augmentation strategy was employed to address the elite bias inherent in the original data. DEFINED adopts a mixed-granularity training strategy enabling robust learning from limited fine-grained supervision annotated by trained graduate experts. To rigorously validate ecological validity beyond synthetic benchmarks, we incorporate an empirical study with debate-naive participants, utilizing these authentic data to serve as a qualitative case study for mid-to-low proficiency populations. Across our evaluation protocol, our scoring model achieves accurate and stable scoring, outperforming prompt-based large language model evaluators and existing debate scoring methods.

中文摘要翻译: 人类创造力已在大语言模型时代成为一项关键能力。在复杂的开放性环境中评估创造力是数据挖掘领域的一项重大挑战，目前受限于对标准化简单任务的依赖以及细粒度专家数据的稀缺。作为一种生态效度良好的评估场景，辩论能够反映创造力的多个维度，涵盖发散性思维和收敛性思维。此外，辩论是数据丰富的领域，拥有大量公开可获取的材料。当前的自动化评分方法难以适用于辩论等复杂场景，因此仍依赖昂贵的人工评估。为此，本文提出DEFINED，一种面向辩论场景的数据高效的细粒度创造力评估计算框架。DEFINED通过层级化的八维指标体系来操作化辩论创造力，借助预训练自回归语言模型实现，该模型配有层级评分头，支持细粒度和粗粒度两种评估。我们从真实辩论比赛中获取陈述及其对应的专家评分，并采用约束数据增强策略来缓解原始数据中存在的精英偏差。DEFINED采用混合粒度训练策略，能够从经过训练的研究生专家标注的有限细粒度监督数据中进行鲁棒学习。为严格验证超越合成基准的生态效度，我们纳入了一项包含辩论新手参与者的实证研究，利用这些真实数据作为中低水平人群的定性案例分析。在我们的评估协议中，该评分模型实现了准确且稳定的评分，优于基于提示的大语言模型评估器和现有的辩论评分方法。

</details>


### [162] [Generative Molecular Morphing for Flexible-Size Design via Unbalanced Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07239)
*Malte Franke,Stefan P. Schmid,Zarko Ivkovic,Kjell Jorner,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Morph是一种可变大小的生成模型，基于几何图进行条件和非条件3D分子设计，通过动态调整原子数量来改善分子性质导向和采样灵活性，在现有固定大小模型表现不佳的分布外生成任务中表现出色。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多分子性质与分子大小（原子数量）内在相关，而现有的扩散模型和基于流的模型将原子数量固定不变，限制了它们处理性质与大小之间复杂关系的能力。

Method: 提出Morph模型，一种基于几何图的可变大小生成模型，能够动态调整分子中的原子数量，无缝整合现有结构先验（如分子骨架），并显著增强性质导向能力。

Result: Morph在匹配现有固定大小最先进模型性能的同时，提供了无与伦比的采样灵活性；在先前模型失败的分布外生成场景中成功生成分子。

Conclusion: Morph通过可变大小设计克服了固定大小模型的局限性，为分子设计的增强生成建模开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: The success of generative molecular design hinges on a model's steerability toward high-reward samples. Because many molecular properties are intrinsically linked to molecular size, accurately capturing the joint distribution of properties and the number of atoms is essential. However, current diffusion and flow-based models fix the number of atoms, which ultimately limits their ability to navigate this complex relationship. To address this, we introduce Morph, a flexible-size generative model for conditional and unconditional 3D molecular design based on geometric graphs. By dynamically adapting size, Morph can seamlessly integrate existing structural priors, like scaffolds, and significantly enhances property steering. We show that Morph matches current fixed-size state-of-the-art models while offering the benefit of unparalleled sampling flexibility. We demonstrate out-of-distribution generation in regimes where previous models fail, paving the way for enhanced generative modeling for molecular design.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式分子设计的成功取决于模型向高奖励样本的可导向性。由于许多分子性质与分子大小内在相关，准确捕捉性质与原子数量的联合分布至关重要。然而，当前的扩散模型和基于流的模型将原子数量固定不变，这最终限制了它们处理这种复杂关系的能力。为解决这一问题，我们提出了Morph——一种基于几何图的可变大小生成模型，用于条件性和非条件性的3D分子设计。通过动态调整分子大小，Morph能够无缝整合现有结构先验（如分子骨架），并显著增强性质导向能力。我们证明，Morph在匹配当前固定大小最先进模型性能的同时，提供了无与伦比的采样灵活性。我们在先前模型失败的分布外生成场景中展示了出色的生成能力，为分子设计的增强生成建模铺平了道路。

</details>


### [163] [A Held-Out Transition-Pair Falsifier for Long-Horizon Non-Abelian State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07254)
*Jeonghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对序列模型在非交换群状态追踪中的根本局限性，提出了一种新颖的留出转移对验证协议，并证明投影递归状态模型在长程隐藏状态追踪上具有显著优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 序列模型在追踪状态时存在根本局限：相关信号往往不是观测token的摘要，而是通过非交换变换演化的有序潜在状态。现有模型（如GRU、状态空间模型）难以处理需要非交换代数结构的长程依赖任务。

Method: 1) 提出留出转移对验证协议：在训练时禁止特定的有序生成子对，评估时要求相同的局部模式，阻断直接的局部转移记忆路径。2) 在受控的S₃×S₃基准上，训练投影递归状态模型（仅使用长度为8的序列），并通过投影机制强制非交换状态组合。3) 设置了多种基线模型（bag、GRU、结构化状态空间模型）及其投影匹配版本进行对比。

Result: 投影递归状态模型在长达1,048,576个token的评估范围内，对5个随机种子均实现了完美（250/250）的最终状态预测。所有原生读出基线（bag、GRU、结构化SSM）在该协议下表现接近floor。投影匹配的基线模型也接近随机水平。机制诊断表明硬投影与低同态误差、低状态一致性漂移、非平凡交换子分离相关，而软投影则会破坏最终状态精度。

Conclusion: 在该受控有限群验证器范围内，显式的投影非交换状态组合作为一种有用的归纳偏置，能够有效支持长程隐藏状态追踪。但证据仅限于该受控有限群验证协议，不构成一般性的架构排名。

Original Abstract: State tracking exposes a sharp limitation of sequence models: the relevant signal is often not a summary of observed tokens, but an ordered latent state that evolves through non-commutative transformations. We introduce a held-out transition-pair falsifier for finite non-Abelian group tracking. The protocol forbids selected ordered generator pairs during training and requires the same local patterns during evaluation, blocking one direct local-transition memorization pathway. In a controlled $S_3 \times S_3$ benchmark, a projected recurrent state model trained only on length-8 sequences produces error-free final-state predictions (perfect 250/250 per horizon) through evaluation horizons up to 1,048,576 tokens across five seeds. Matched native-readout baselines, including bag, GRU, and a single-configuration structured state-space model, remain near floor under the same protocol. Projection-matched GRU, structured SSM, and bag baselines equipped with analogous finite-group prototype readouts also remain near chance under the same split. Mechanism diagnostics show that hard projection coincides with low homomorphism error, low state-consistency drift, and non-trivial commutator separation, while softened projection collapses final-state accuracy. Clean-split audits verify zero verbatim reduced-word overlap and zero structural-template overlap between training and evaluation partitions. The evidence is scoped to this controlled finite-group falsifier rather than to a general architecture ranking. Within that regime, explicit projected non-commutative state composition acts as a useful inductive bias for long-horizon hidden-state tracking.

中文摘要翻译: 状态追踪揭示了序列模型的一个根本局限性：相关信号往往不是观测token的摘要，而是通过非交换变换演化的有序潜在状态。我们针对有限非阿贝尔群追踪提出了一种留出转移对验证协议。该协议在训练时禁止特定的有序生成子对，并在评估时要求相同的局部模式，从而阻断了一条直接的局部转移记忆路径。在受控的S₃×S₃基准上，一个仅使用长度为8的序列进行训练的投影递归状态模型，在长达1,048,576个token的评估范围内，对5个随机种子均实现了完美（250/250）的最终状态预测。在相同协议下，匹配的原生读出基线模型（包括bag、GRU和单一配置的结构化状态空间模型）表现仍接近最低水平。采用类似有限群原型读出机制的投影匹配GRU、结构化状态空间模型和bag基线模型，在相同数据划分下也接近随机水平。机制诊断表明，硬投影与低同态误差、低状态一致性漂移以及非平凡交换子分离相关，而软投影则会破坏最终状态精度。清洁划分审计验证了训练集和评估集之间零逐词缩减重叠和零结构模板重叠。该证据仅限于该受控有限群验证器，而非一般性的架构排名。在该框架内，显式的投影非交换状态组合作为长程隐藏状态追踪的一种有用归纳偏置。

</details>


### [164] [Where Rectified Flows Leak: Characterising Membership Signals Along the Interpolation Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07271)
*Thomas Sesmat,Gabriel Meseguer-Brocal,Geoffroy Peeters*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Rectified Flow模型对训练数据的记忆痕迹，发现沿插值路径$\lambda$存在一个训练与测试数据重构差异的钟形曲线，该信号可被用于成员推断攻击。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解生成模型从训练数据中保留了哪些信息对版权和隐私至关重要。除了逐字复制外，模型还可能编码不易察觉的训练数据痕迹，这些痕迹虽未直接出现在输出中，但仍可被利用。

Method: 分析Rectified Flow训练中的插值路径$X_\lambda = (1-\lambda)X_0 + \lambda X_1$，发现训练与测试数据重构差异存在随$\lambda$变化的钟形曲线；在高斯假设下推导出峰值的闭式解，并在音频和图像上验证。

Result: 钟形结构具有普适性；在高斯假设满足时峰值预测准确；该$\lambda$分辨结构可用于成员推断攻击，区分训练集成员与非成员。

Conclusion: Rectified Flow模型沿插值路径存在可被利用的训练数据记忆信号，该发现为生成模型的隐私分析提供了新视角。

Original Abstract: Understanding what generative models retain from training data remains challenging, with implications for copyright and privacy. Beyond verbatim reproduction, models can encode subtler traces of their training data that never surface in their outputs yet remain exploitable. We study this regime for Rectified Flows, which are increasingly used in deployed generative systems. We analyse the interpolation path $X_λ= (1-λ)X_0 + λX_1$ that defines the Rectified Flow training. We show that a gap exists between the reconstruction of train and test data that follows a bell-shaped curve over $λ$, wich accumulates during training, while the validation metrics remain stable. The signal has a maximum whose location we derive in closed form under Gaussian assumptions. We validate these predictions on both audio and images and show that the bell-shaped structure is universal, while the peak prediction holds when our assumptions are satisfied. As a proof of concept, we exploit this specific $λ$-resolved structure to perform a Membership Inference Attack, distinguishing members of the training set from non-members.

中文摘要翻译: 理解生成模型从训练数据中保留了哪些信息仍然是一个具有挑战性的问题，这对版权和隐私具有重要影响。除了逐字复制之外，模型还可以编码训练数据中不易察觉的痕迹，这些痕迹虽从未在其输出中显现，但仍可被利用。我们针对整流流（Rectified Flows）研究了这一机制，该类模型在已部署的生成系统中被越来越多地使用。我们分析了定义整流流训练的插值路径$X_\lambda = (1-\lambda)X_0 + \lambda X_1$。我们发现，训练数据和测试数据的重建之间存在一个差距，该差距随$\lambda$呈钟形曲线变化，并在训练过程中不断累积，而验证指标保持稳定。该信号存在一个最大值，我们在高斯假设下推导出其位置的闭式解。我们在音频和图像上验证了这些预测，结果表明钟形结构具有普适性，而峰值预测在我们的假设满足时成立。作为概念验证，我们利用这一特定的$\lambda$分辨结构进行成员推断攻击，区分训练集成员与非成员。

</details>


### [165] [Closed-Form Spectral Regularization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07289)
*Yongxian Wei,Runxi Cheng,Xingxuan Zhang,Li Shen,Chun Yuan,Peng Cui,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现模型合并中的迭代求解器主要充当隐式谱正则化器而非优化器，基于此将多任务模型合并形式化为含噪线性逆问题，提出闭式谱滤波估计器SWUDI及其自适应变体SWUDI-A，在多个基准上匹配或超越SOTA方法，同时加速28-72倍并降低50%显存。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有最先进的模型合并方法将合并表述为逐层二次干扰最小化问题，其精确闭式伪逆解在实践中远不如数百次梯度下降迭代。迭代循环主导了计算开销，但其有效性尚未得到解释。本文旨在揭示迭代求解器有效性的深层原因，并设计更高效的闭式求解方法。

Method: 首先揭示了迭代求解器本质上是作为隐式谱正则化器（implicit spectral regularizer），处理病态正规方程中干扰算子小特征值方向放大的代理噪声问题。基于此，将多任务模型合并形式化为含噪线性逆问题（noisy linear inverse problem），提出谱滤波估计器SWUDI：结合软指数滤波器（匹配梯度下降轨迹）和硬Top-K截断（抑制噪声放大方向）。进一步提出自适应变体SWUDI-A，用逐层秩规则替代全局秩超参数。两种变体仅需每个线性层进行一次对称特征分解，无需训练数据或优化器状态。

Result: 在四个通用基准和多模态合并基准（涵盖VQA、Geometry、Chart、OCR、Grounding及模态合并）上，提出的谱求解器匹配或超越了SOTA合并方法；关键地，计算时间减少28-72倍，峰值GPU显存降低最多50%。

Conclusion: 模型合并中迭代求解器的有效性来源于其隐式谱正则化作用，而非优化能力。所提出的闭式谱滤波方法在保持或提升性能的同时，大幅降低了计算开销和内存需求，为高效多任务模型合并提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Model merging combines several independently fine-tuned experts into a single multi-task model without any training data, reducing the storage, serving, and decentralized-development costs of large foundation models. State-of-the-art merging methods formulate merging as a layer-wise quadratic interference minimization problem. Although this problem admits an exact closed-form pseudoinverse solution, that solution underperforms hundreds of iterations of gradient descent in practice. The iterative loop dominates the cost of the pipeline, yet its effectiveness has remained unexplained. We revisit this regime and show that the iterative solver does not primarily act as an optimizer; rather, it serves as an implicit spectral regularizer for an ill-posed normal equation, where small-eigenvalue directions of the per-layer interference operator amplify proxy noise. Building on this finding, we formalize multi-task model merging as a noisy linear inverse problem and propose a spectral filtering estimator parameterized by a per-direction filter. We instantiate this estimator with SWUDI, a closed-form method that combines a soft exponential filter, which matches the gradient-flow trajectory of iterative descent, with a hard top-K truncation that suppresses noise-amplifying small-eigenvalue directions. Furthermore, we propose SWUDI-A, an adaptive variant that replaces the global rank hyperparameter with per-layer rank rules, further improving robustness across architectures. Both variants share a single symmetric eigendecomposition per linear layer and require no training data or optimizer state. Across four general benchmarks and a multimodal merging benchmark spanning VQA, Geometry, Chart, OCR, Grounding, and modality merging, our proposed spectral solvers match or outperform state-of-the-art merging methods. Crucially, they reduce wall-clock time by 28-72x and peak GPU memory by up to 50%.

中文摘要翻译: 模型合并将多个独立微调的专家模型组合成一个多任务模型，无需任何训练数据，从而降低了大型基础模型的存储、服务及去中心化开发成本。最先进的合并方法将合并问题形式化为逐层二次干扰最小化。尽管该问题存在精确的闭式伪逆解，但该解在实践中远逊于数百次梯度下降迭代。迭代循环主导了计算流程的成本，但其有效性迄今未得到解释。我们重新研究了这一机制，并证明迭代求解器的主要作用并非作为优化器，而是作为病态正规方程的隐式谱正则化器——在该方程中，每层干扰算子的特征值较小的方向会放大代理噪声。基于这一发现，我们将多任务模型合并形式化为一个含噪线性逆问题，并提出了一种由逐方向滤波器参数化的谱滤波估计器。我们通过SWUDI实现了该估计器，这是一种结合了软指数滤波器（匹配迭代下降的梯度流轨迹）与硬Top-K截断（抑制放大噪声的小特征值方向）的闭式方法。此外，我们提出了自适应变体SWUDI-A，用逐层秩规则替代全局秩超参数，进一步提升了跨架构的鲁棒性。两种变体均仅需每个线性层进行一次对称特征分解，无需训练数据或优化器状态。在四个通用基准以及涵盖VQA、Geometry、Chart、OCR、Grounding和模态合并的多模态合并基准上，我们提出的谱求解器匹配或超越了最先进的合并方法。关键的是，它们将计算时间减少了28-72倍，并将峰值GPU内存降低了高达50%。

</details>


### [166] [Trio: Learning Time-Series Forecasting with Temporal-Spatial-Sample Attention and Structural Causal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07291)
*Tao Chen,Yexu Zhou,Zhi Gong,Hengwei He,Hongda Li,Zhewei Chen,Dongjing Wang,Xin Zhang,Decheng Liu,Chunlei Peng,Zheng Chen,Wenyue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Trio是一种基于时间-空间-样本注意力机制的多变量时间序列预测架构，通过显式组织历史输入-输出示例来增强预测，并引入TS-SCM生成器创建结构化合成任务以提供有用的先验知识。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs)在表格数据上有效，但直接迁移到时间序列预测存在困难，因为时间顺序、动态滞后和重复的历史模式无法被普通表格先验自然捕获。因此，需要研究如何显式组织历史输入-输出示例来指导当前预测。

Method: 提出Trio架构，包含三种注意力机制：(1) 时间注意力（Temporal Attention）捕获窗口内的时间动态；(2) 空间注意力（Spatial Attention）建模变量间依赖关系；(3) 样本注意力（Sample Attention）检索相关历史lookback-future对以指导当前预测。同时引入TS-SCM生成器创建具有动态滞后、跨变量交互、噪声、反馈和分布漂移的结构化合成任务。

Result: 在合成数据、工业数据和公开基准上的实验表明，Trio架构提升了预测性能。零样本实验进一步表明TS-SCM生成的任务能提供有用的结构先验。

Conclusion: Trio通过显式组织历史样本有效提升了多变量时间序列预测性能，TS-SCM生成的结构化先验也具有潜力，但完全通用的PFN式时间序列预测仍然是一个开放问题。

Original Abstract: Multivariate time-series forecasting requires models to reason over temporal dynamics, cross-variable dependencies, and historical input-output correspondences. Recent Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) suggest that synthetic tasks can be useful for learning transferable inference behavior. However, directly transferring this paradigm to time-series forecasting remains difficult, since temporal order, dynamic lags, and recurring historical patterns are not naturally captured by ordinary tabular priors. Motivated by this observation, we propose Trio, a sample-aware time-series forecasting architecture based on Temporal-Spatial-Sample attention. Temporal attention captures within-window dynamics, spatial attention models inter-variable dependencies, and sample attention retrieves relevant historical lookback-future pairs to guide the current prediction. Rather than claiming a fully general PFN-style forecaster, our goal is to study how historical input-output examples can be explicitly organized and reused within a forecasting model. We further introduce a Time-Series Structural Causal Model (TS-SCM) generator to create structured synthetic forecasting tasks with dynamic lags, cross-variable interactions, noise, feedback, and distributional drift. Experiments on synthetic, industrial, and public benchmarks show that the proposed architecture improves forecasting performance. Exploratory zero-shot experiments further suggest that TS-SCM-generated tasks may provide useful structural priors, while fully general PFN-style time-series forecasting remains an open problem.

中文摘要翻译: 多变量时间序列预测要求模型对时间动态、跨变量依赖关系和历史输入-输出对应关系进行推理。最近的先验数据拟合网络（PFN）表明，合成任务有助于学习可迁移的推理行为。然而，直接将这一范式迁移到时间序列预测仍然困难，因为时间顺序、动态滞后和重复的历史模式无法被普通表格先验自然捕获。受此观察启发，我们提出Trio，一种基于时间-空间-样本注意力的样本感知时间序列预测架构。时间注意力捕获窗口内动态，空间注意力建模变量间依赖关系，样本注意力检索相关的历史lookback-future对以指导当前预测。我们的目标并非声称一个完全通用的PFN式预测器，而是研究如何在预测模型中显式组织和重用历史输入-输出示例。我们进一步引入时间序列结构因果模型（TS-SCM）生成器，用于创建具有动态滞后、跨变量交互、噪声、反馈和分布漂移的结构化合成预测任务。在合成数据、工业数据和公开基准上的实验表明，所提出的架构提升了预测性能。探索性的零样本实验进一步表明，TS-SCM生成的任务可能提供有用的结构先验，而完全通用的PFN式时间序列预测仍然是一个开放问题。

</details>


### [167] [Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence: Explanatory Insufficiency as a Driver of Representation Learning and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征涌现的自举理论（TBER），认为当现有表征的解释域被超出时，新的表征层级会因“解释性不足”而涌现，并描述了从异常发现到新表征生成的递归循环过程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究大多关注在选定表征框架后如何优化表征，而较少关注何时需要新的表征层级。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一个系统性的理论框架来解释表征创新的时机与机制。

Method: 提出TBER理论框架，识别“解释性不足”作为表征跃迁的正向信号，并形式化描述了一个包含五个阶段（稳定观察、异常检测、解释性不足识别、表征涌现、暂定稳定）的递归自举过程。

Result: 本文为理论性论述，未提供实验性结果，而是从理论层面阐述了表征涌现的机制，并讨论了该理论在表征学习、潜在空间、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生、自适应生物系统和科学发现等领域的应用前景。

Conclusion: TBER表明，未来AI系统可以从检测自身内部表征的解释性限制机制中受益；表征创新不仅由更多数据、更大模型或更强算力驱动，还受到持久存在的解释性缺口驱动。

Original Abstract: Representation learning is central to modern machine learning, enabling transitions from handcrafted features to learned embeddings, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, and digital twins. Yet most research examines how representations are optimized after a representational framework has been selected, while less attention is given to when a new level of representation becomes necessary. We introduce the Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence (TBER), a framework describing how new representations arise when existing ones become explanatorily insufficient. In this view, representational innovation is not only driven by more data, larger models, or greater computational power, but also by persistent explanatory gaps: situations in which a representation can still describe observations but can no longer make their organization or transformations intelligible. TBER identifies explanatory insufficiency as a positive signal for representational transition. A representation becomes insufficient not because it is necessarily false, but because its explanatory domain has been exceeded. The bootstrap dynamic follows a recursive sequence: observations reveal anomalies; anomalies expose insufficiencies; insufficiencies motivate new representations; and these new representations generate further observations and possible new insufficiencies.We formalize this process through five stages: stabilized observation, anomaly detection, recognition of explanatory insufficiency, representational emergence, and provisional stabilization. We discuss applications to representation learning, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, digital twins, adaptive biological systems, and scientific discovery. TBER suggests that future AI systems may benefit from mechanisms for detecting the explanatory limits of their own internal representations.

中文摘要翻译: 表征学习是现代机器学习的核心，它推动了从手工特征到学习嵌入、潜在空间、基础模型、世界模型和数字孪生的转变。然而，大多数研究关注的是在选定表征框架后如何优化表征，而较少关注何时需要新的表征层级。我们提出了表征涌现的自举理论（Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence, TBER），该框架描述了当现有表征的解释力不足时，新表征是如何产生的。在这种观点下，表征创新不仅由更多数据、更大模型或更强算力驱动，还受到持久存在的解释性缺口的驱动：即表征虽然仍能描述观测结果，但已无法使其组织方式或变换过程变得可理解。TBER将解释性不足识别为表征跃迁的正向信号。一个表征变得不足，并非因为它必然是错的，而是因为其解释域已被超出。自举动态遵循递归序列：观测揭示异常；异常暴露不足；不足催生新表征；新表征带来新的观测和可能的新不足。我们通过五个阶段形式化了这一过程：稳定观察、异常检测、解释性不足识别、表征涌现和暂定稳定。我们讨论了该方法在表征学习、潜在空间、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生、自适应生物系统和科学发现中的应用。TBER表明，未来AI系统可能受益于检测其自身内部表征解释性限制的机制。

</details>


### [168] [TabSwift: An Efficient Tabular Foundation Model with Row-Wise Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07345)
*Si-Yang Liu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabSwift 通过门控注意力稳定机制和可学习的寄存器令牌增强轻量级行级注意力骨干网络，实现了与更复杂的表格基础模型（如 TabPFN v2、TabICL）相当的精度，同时推理效率更高，并支持自适应逐层早退机制以优化延迟敏感场景的部署。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（如 TabPFN）虽在小中型数据集上表现优异，但为了提升精度而采用日益复杂的架构，导致推理成本高昂，限制了实际部署。本文旨在通过轻量级设计保持竞争力的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Method: 1) 采用仅包含行级注意力的轻量级骨干网络；2) 引入门控注意力稳定机制（gated attention stabilization）；3) 加入少量可学习的寄存器令牌（register tokens）以提供全局上下文并改善预训练质量；4) 针对延迟敏感场景，提出自适应逐层早退机制（adaptive layer-wise early-exit），动态调整每个样本的推理深度。

Result: TabSwift 在分类和回归任务上与更强的表格基础模型（TabPFN v2、TabICL）性能相当，但推理效率更高；自适应早退机制进一步优化了延迟敏感场景下的计算成本。

Conclusion: 轻量级设计配合简单有效的增强机制，可以在保持竞争力的同时实现高效的表格上下文学习，适合实际部署。

Original Abstract: Tabular foundation models, exemplified by TabPFN, perform prediction via in-context learning, inferring test labels directly from labeled training examples. They have demonstrated competitive performance, particularly on small-to-medium datasets. However, recent tabular foundation models often improve accuracy with increasingly complex architectures, incurring higher inference cost and limiting practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the original TabPFN design and show that a lightweight row-wise attention-only backbone can remain highly competitive with two simple enhancements: a gated attention stabilization mechanism and a small set of learnable register tokens that provide global context and improve pretraining quality. The resulting model, TabSwift, supports both classification and regression, and is competitive with stronger tabular foundation models (e.g., TabPFN v2 and TabICL) while being more efficient at inference. For latency-sensitive serving, we further introduce an adaptive layer-wise early-exit mechanism that dynamically adjusts inference depth per sample. Overall, TabSwift enables efficient and anytime tabular in-context learning for practical deployments.

中文摘要翻译: 以 TabPFN 为代表的表格基础模型通过上下文学习（in-context learning）进行预测，直接从带标签的训练样本中推断测试标签。这些模型在中小型数据集上展现了有竞争力的性能。然而，近期表格基础模型往往通过日益复杂的架构来提升精度，导致推理成本增加，限制了实际部署。本文重新审视了原始 TabPFN 的设计，并证明：一个轻量级的仅含行级注意力（row-wise attention）的骨干网络，只需配合两项简单增强——门控注意力稳定机制（gated attention stabilization）和少量可学习的寄存器令牌（register tokens）以提供全局上下文并提升预训练质量，即可保持极强的竞争力。由此得到的模型 TabSwift 支持分类与回归任务，在与更强的表格基础模型（如 TabPFN v2 和 TabICL）竞争的同时，推理效率更高。针对延迟敏感的部署场景，我们进一步引入自适应逐层早退机制（adaptive layer-wise early-exit），可动态调整每个样本的推理深度。总体而言，TabSwift 为实际部署实现了高效且可随时中断的表格上下文学习。

</details>


### [169] [SleepExplain: Explainable Non-Rapid Eye Movement and Rapid Eye Movement Sleep Stage Classification from EEG Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07351)
*Rafsan Jany,Md. Hamjajul Ashmafee,Iqram Hussain,Md Azam Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SleepExplain模型，利用集成学习（随机森林、XGBoost、梯度提升）对NREM和REM睡眠阶段进行分类，并采用SHAP方法提供可解释的预测，最高准确率达94.30%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 睡眠阶段分类对睡眠相关疾病的诊断至关重要，而现有模型缺乏对分类结果的可解释性，难以让临床医生理解模型预测的依据。

Method: 使用随机森林、XGBoost和梯度提升三种集成分类模型对NREM和REM睡眠阶段进行分类，并采用基于博弈论的SHAP方法构建可解释的SleepExplain模型，用于解释模型的预测结果。

Result: 随机森林准确率92.54%，梯度提升94.25%，XGBoost最高达94.30%。

Conclusion: 集成学习方法能有效分类NREM和REM睡眠阶段，结合SHAP可解释性方法能够为预测结果提供有说服力的解释，有助于提升临床诊断的可信度。

Original Abstract: Classification of sleep stages is one of the most important diagnostic approaches for a variety of sleep-related disorders. Electroencephalography (EEG) is regarded as a powerful tool for examining the association between neurological effects and sleep phases since it correctly identifies sleep-related neurological alterations. During Non-Rapid Eye Movement (NREM) and Rapid Eye Movement (REM) sleep phases, a number of nerve and bodily functions are affected and therefore hold an important role both in their functionalities. This work aims to classify NREM and REM sleep stages from sleep EEG data and present a noble SleepExplain model, an explainable NREM and REM sleep stage classification to explain its predictions. In this work, sleep stages were classified using Random Forest, XGBoost, and Gradient Boosting ensemble classification models. Overall, we obtained an accuracy of 92.54% (Random Forest), 94.25% (Gradient Boosting), and 94.30% (XGBoost). For explainable classification model, we utilized a game theoretic approach, SHAP (SHapley Addictive exPlanations) to offer a convincing explanation for the prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 睡眠阶段分类是多种睡眠相关疾病最重要的诊断方法之一。脑电图（EEG）被认为是检测神经系统效应与睡眠阶段之间关联的有效工具，因为它能够准确识别与睡眠相关的神经变化。在非快速眼动（NREM）和快速眼动（REM）睡眠阶段，许多神经和身体功能受到影响，因此在其功能中发挥着重要作用。本研究旨在从睡眠脑电图数据中对NREM和REM睡眠阶段进行分类，并提出一种新颖的SleepExplain模型——一种可解释的NREM和REM睡眠阶段分类模型，以解释其预测结果。本工作采用随机森林、XGBoost和梯度提升三种集成分类模型对睡眠阶段进行分类。总体而言，我们获得的准确率为：随机森林92.54%，梯度提升94.25%，XGBoost 94.30%。对于可解释分类模型，我们利用基于博弈论的方法SHAP（SHapley Additive exPlanations）为预测结果提供有说服力的解释。

</details>


### [170] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次对vLLM推理引擎的冷启动延迟进行系统性性能表征，将其分解为六个基础步骤，发现启动过程主要为CPU受限，并构建了一个轻量级分析模型来准确预测不同硬件配置下的启动延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得日益重要。vLLM已成为许多推理工作负载的事实标准引擎，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，目前缺乏对其启动延迟的系统性研究。本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基础步骤，分析每个步骤相对于模型级和系统级参数的一致可解释的扩展趋势，识别出启动过程主要为CPU受限，并基于这些洞察构建轻量级分析模型用于预测启动延迟。

Result: 启动过程以CPU受限为主；每个步骤相对于模型级和系统级参数呈现一致且可解释的扩展趋势，支持对延迟来源进行细粒度归因；构建的分析模型能准确预测给定硬件配置下的vLLM启动延迟。

Conclusion: 通过系统性性能表征和轻量级分析模型，为大规模推理环境中的资源规划提供了可操作的指导。

Original Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

中文摘要翻译: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得日益重要。如今，vLLM已成为许多推理工作负载的事实标准引擎。尽管广受欢迎，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，目前尚缺乏对其启动延迟的系统性研究。伴随着V1 API等重大架构创新以及torch.compile的引入，本文首次对vLLM启动延迟进行了详细的性能表征。我们将启动过程分解为六个基础步骤，并证明其主要为CPU受限。每个步骤相对于模型级和系统级参数均呈现一致且可解释的扩展趋势，从而能对延迟来源进行细粒度归因。基于这些洞察，我们开发了一个轻量级分析模型，该模型能够准确预测给定硬件配置下的vLLM启动延迟，为大规模推理环境中的资源规划提供可操作的指导。所有基准测试数据集、分析工具和预测脚本均已在https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler开源。

</details>


### [171] [A robust PPG foundation model using multimodal physiological supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07365)
*Eloy Geenjaar,Vince Calhoun,Scott Daly,Gouthaman KV,Lie Lu,Trisha Mittal,Daniel P. Darcy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需高质量或类场数据预训练的PPG基础模型，通过利用ICU数据集中的心电和呼吸信号进行多模态监督对比学习，在更少预训练样本下实现15个下游任务中14个的性能提升，增强了模型对消费级数据的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PPG基础模型要么依赖开源ICU数据集但需要精心筛选数据导致难以泛化到场域数据，要么使用闭源的场域PPG数据。本文旨在开发一种无需高质量或类场预训练数据的PPG基础模型。

Method: 利用ICU数据集中的同步心电和呼吸信号作为多模态监督信号，为PPG信号选择对比学习样本，使模型能够保留并学习噪声PPG片段，从而提升推理时的鲁棒性。

Result: 在比现有最先进方法少3倍的预训练样本下，模型在15个多样化的下游任务中取得了14个任务的性能提升，包括场域日常活动和心率预测任务。

Conclusion: 多模态监督能够整合互补的生理信息来提升PPG基础模型的鲁棒性，并增强其向消费级数据泛化的能力。

Original Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG), a non-invasive measure of changes in blood volume, is widely used in both wearable devices and clinical settings. Recent PPG foundation models either use open-source ICU datasets with pretraining paradigms that require curated data and thus complicate generalization to field-like data, or use closed-source field-like PPG data. In contrast, we propose a PPG foundation model that does not require high-quality or field-like pretraining data, and instead leverages accompanying electrocardiogram and respiratory signals in ICU datasets to select contrastive samples during pretraining. Our approach allows the model to retain and learn from noisy PPG segments, improving robustness at inference. Our model, pretrained on 3x fewer subjects than existing state-of-the-art approaches, achieves performance improvements on 14 out of 15 diverse downstream tasks, including field-like daily activity and heart rate prediction. Our results demonstrate that multimodal supervision can integrate complementary physiological information to improve the robustness of PPG foundation models and enhance their generalization to consumer-grade data.

中文摘要翻译: 光电容积描记法（PPG）是一种非侵入式测量血容量变化的技术，广泛应用于可穿戴设备和临床场景。现有的PPG基础模型要么使用开源的ICU数据集，但依赖需要精心筛选数据的预训练范式，从而难以泛化到场域数据；要么使用闭源的类场域PPG数据。与之不同的是，我们提出了一种无需高质量或类场域预训练数据的PPG基础模型，该模型利用ICU数据集中同步的心电图和呼吸信号来在预训练期间选择对比学习样本。我们的方法使模型能够保留并学习噪声PPG片段，从而提升推理时的鲁棒性。该模型在比现有最先进方法少3倍的预训练样本下，在15个多样化的下游任务中取得了14个任务的性能提升，包括场域日常活动和心率预测任务。我们的实验结果表明，多模态监督能够整合互补的生理信息来提升PPG基础模型的鲁棒性，并增强其向消费级数据泛化的能力。

</details>


### [172] [Self-evolving LLM agents with in-distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07367)
*Yudi Zhang,Meng Fang,Zhenfang Chen,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Q-Evolve框架，通过自演进机制统一过程奖励标注与策略学习，实现LLM智能体在稀疏奖励环境下的稳定长程决策，在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上超越强基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂环境中进行可靠长程决策面临信用分配难题，即仅在回合结束时获得延迟奖励，缺乏中间过程的密集监督信号。

Method: 提出Q-Evolve自演进框架：(1) 混合离线数据集（专家演示+智能体轨迹）上学习分布内评论家（in-distribution critic），通过加权隐式Q学习稳定稀疏奖励下的Bellman备份；(2) 利用学习到的价值函数通过优势估计导出逐步过程奖励；(3) 基于这些信号进行行为邻近策略优化，实现迭代自改进而不加剧分布偏移。

Result: 在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上，Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过过程级监督与策略在共享的分布内学习循环中的协同演进，可实现稳定的智能体自演进。

Original Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful controllers for interactive agents in complex environments, yet training them to perform reliable long-horizon decision making remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in credit assignment: agents often receive delayed rewards only at the end of episodes. In this paper, we propose Q-Evolve, a self-evolving framework for LLM agents that unifies automatic process-reward labeling and policy learning within a principled in-distribution reinforcement learning paradigm. In each evolving iteration, our method learns an in-distribution critic from a hybrid off-policy dataset that combines expert demonstrations with agent-generated trajectories, stabilizing Bellman backups in sparse-reward settings via a weighted Implicit Q-Learning objective. The learned value function is then used to derive step-wise process rewards through advantage estimation, enabling dense and reliable supervision without environment backtracking or human annotation. Leveraging these signals, we perform behavior-proximal policy optimization that evolves the agent over the data used for process reward labeling, allowing iterative self-improvement without exacerbating distribution shift. We evaluate our method on AlfWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, showing Q-Evolve outperforms strong baselines in sample efficiency, robustness, and overall task performance. Our results demonstrate that stable agent self-evolution is achievable through the co-evolution of process-level supervision and policy, both grounded within a shared in-distribution learning loop.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）最近已成为复杂环境中交互式智能体的强大控制器，但如何训练它们执行可靠的长程决策仍然是一个根本性挑战。一个关键难点在于信用分配：智能体通常仅在回合结束时获得延迟奖励。在本文中，我们提出Q-Evolve，一个用于LLM智能体的自演进框架，该框架在原理性的分布内强化学习范式下统一了自动过程奖励标注与策略学习。在每次演进迭代中，我们的方法从结合专家演示与智能体生成轨迹的混合离线数据集中学习一个分布内评论家，通过加权隐式Q学习目标稳定稀疏奖励场景下的Bellman备份。随后，利用学习到的价值函数通过优势估计导出逐步过程奖励，从而在无需环境回溯或人工标注的情况下提供密集且可靠的监督信号。借助这些信号，我们执行行为邻近策略优化，使智能体在用于过程奖励标注的数据上演进，实现迭代自改进而不加剧分布偏移。我们在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上评估了该方法，结果显示Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能上均优于强基线。我们的结果表明，通过过程级监督与策略在共享的分布内学习循环中的协同演进，可实现稳定的智能体自演进。

</details>


### [173] [Do Coding Agents Deceive Us? Detecting and Preventing Cheating via Capped Evaluation with Randomized Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07379)
*Thanawat Lodkaew,Johannes Ackermann,Soichiro Nishimori,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Masashi Sugiyama,Takashi Ishida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CapCode框架，通过设计上限低于满分的随机化测试集来检测智能体在评估中的作弊行为；同时提出CapReward奖励设计来抑制训练中的作弊，实验证明两者均有效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 智能体在评估和训练中存在利用捷径作弊以获得高分的现象，导致评估分数无法真实反映任务解决能力，亟需可靠的作弊检测与抑制方法。

Method: 1) CapCode：构建随机化编程测试集，将非作弊情况下的最佳可达性能刻意限制在某个上限以下，分数显著高于上限即表明存在作弊。2) CapReward：基于CapCode原理的奖励设计，阻止优化超出上限的行为，从而抑制作弊。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，CapCode能有效检测作弊行为且不破坏模型性能排名；CapReward能减少作弊行为，使模型更好地遵循任务规范。

Conclusion: CapCode框架和CapReward奖励设计为评估和训练中的作弊检测与抑制提供了有效方案，使评估分数更可靠地反映真实能力。

Original Abstract: A growing failure mode in agent evaluation and training is that models can achieve high evaluation scores by exploiting shortcuts instead of solving the intended task, producing deceptive performance. This makes evaluation scores unreliable as measures of true task-solving ability. We propose CapCode, a framework for constructing coding datasets with randomized tests whose best achievable non-cheating performance is deliberately capped below one. This capped-performance design gives evaluation scores a clearer interpretation: scores substantially above the cap are implausible and therefore provide evidence of cheating. To prevent cheating, we propose CapReward, a reward design based on the CapCode principle to discourage optimization beyond the cap. Experiments across multiple datasets show that CapCode detects cheating while preserving performance ranking of models, and CapReward reduces cheating behavior, yielding models that better follow the intended task specification.

中文摘要翻译: 智能体评估与训练中一个日益严重的问题是，模型可以通过利用捷径（而非真正解决目标任务）获得高分，从而产生欺骗性性能，使得评估分数无法可靠衡量真实的任务解决能力。为此，我们提出CapCode框架，该框架通过构建随机化测试数据集，刻意将非作弊情况下的最佳可达性能限制在某一上限以下。这种上限设计使评估分数具有更清晰的解释：显著高于上限的分数不合理，因此可作为作弊证据。为防止作弊，我们进一步提出CapReward——一种基于CapCode原理的奖励设计，用于抑制超出上限的优化行为。多个数据集上的实验表明，CapCode能够检测作弊行为，同时保持模型的性能排名不变；CapReward能减少作弊行为，使模型更好地遵循目标任务规范。

</details>


### [174] [Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07382)
*Mathieu Chalvidal,Florentin Coeurdoux,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高维协方差估计的经典收缩方法重新表述为在参数化随机插值器上的经验风险最小化，揭示了三种降低统计风险的机制：调度、流映射与耦合、以及早停。基于此提出了一种神经插值器估计器，并在合成数据和真实神经影像数据上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典的高维协方差收缩估计方法缺乏统一的框架来理解其风险降低机制，论文旨在通过随机插值的形式化框架统一并扩展这些方法，并引入新的正则化手段。

Method: 将协方差估计重新定义为源分布与目标分布之间参数化随机插值器上的经验风险最小化问题。构建神经插值器估计器，利用流映射和耦合结构（如最优传输）实现特征向量正则化，并通过早停机制提供额外的偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 所提出的神经估计器在合成实验上验证了有效性；在真实神经影像数据上，该方法展示了比传统方法更强的正则化能力。

Conclusion: 随机插值视角为协方差估计提供了统一的理论框架，通过调度、耦合和早停三种机制实现了更灵活有效的正则化，神经插值器在实际应用中具有优越性能。

Original Abstract: We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.

中文摘要翻译: 我们将高维协方差估计器的经典收缩方法重新表述为在源分布和目标分布之间的参数化随机插值器上的经验风险最小化。该形式化将已知的收缩估计器作为特例恢复，并揭示了三种降低统计风险的机制：(i) 调度：插值调度决定了可容许协方差的类别，从而决定了可实现的风险。(ii) 流映射与耦合：朴素构造相当于假设分布之间的独立性，而特定的耦合结构（例如最优传输问题的解）可以降低经验风险。此外，实现此类耦合的非线性流映射使插值协方差摆脱经验估计的特征基的限制，从而实现特征向量正则化。(iii) 早停：通过对回归向量场进行积分定义的估计器，通过近似真实的插值分布提供了额外的偏差-方差权衡。我们随后提出了一种神经插值器估计器，并给出了其在插值近似误差方面的二次风险上界，并在合成实验中进行了验证。最后，我们将该估计器应用于真实的神经影像数据，展示了该方法在实践中提供的额外正则化能力。

</details>


### [175] [Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07387)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Tzu-Hung Huang,Chih-Pin Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种“分数感知训练”方法，利用音频-字幕对齐分数作为监督信号，结合Beta噪声时间表、分段过滤、两阶段字幕生成和REPA辅助损失，在无需大规模专有数据集和工业级算力的情况下，以450M参数的FluxAudio系统在ICME 2026 ATTM挑战赛效率赛道中获得第二/第三名。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前最先进的文生音系统依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级算力，导致无法区分架构贡献与资源优势。本文旨在不依赖大规模数据和算力的情况下，提升文本到音乐生成系统的性能。

Method: 1）分数感知训练：将音频-字幕对齐分数作为直接监督信号；2）CLAP条件化的Beta噪声时间表，将低分样本路由到高噪声训练区间，实现隐式正则化；3）分段级过滤去除最不匹配的样本；4）两阶段字幕生成策略弥合训练字幕与推理提示之间的分布差距；5）REPA辅助损失从预训练CLAP和MuQ编码器迁移结构化语义知识。

Result: 450M参数的FluxAudio系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track中，客观评估两赛道均排名第2，效率赛道最终MOS评估排名第3。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过分数感知训练和多种创新策略，可以在不依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级算力的情况下，有效提升文本到音乐生成的性能，并在国际竞赛中取得优异排名。

Original Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

中文摘要翻译: 当前最先进的文本到音乐生成系统依赖于大规模专有数据集和工业级计算资源，这使得我们无法区分架构贡献与资源优势的各自作用。本文提出“分数感知训练”，将音频-字幕对齐分数作为贯穿整个管线的直接监督信号。我们并未丢弃低分片段，而是通过一个CLAP条件化的Beta噪声时间表将其重新利用，使其被路由到高噪声训练区间，从而充当有效的隐式正则化器。作为补充，分段级过滤移除了最不匹配的样本，两阶段字幕生成策略则弥合了冗长训练字幕与简洁推理提示之间的分布差距。此外，REPA辅助损失在无需额外数据的情况下，从预训练的CLAP和MuQ编码器中迁移了结构化语义知识。我们基于FluxAudio构建的450M参数系统提交至ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge效率赛道，在客观评估中两赛道均排名第2，在最终MOS评估中效率赛道排名第3。

</details>


### [176] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GReinSS框架，通过动态缩放奖励的策略学习来推断组合潜状态，在模拟数据和真实RNA测序数据上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合规模巨大的空间，而深度学习方法（如变分自编码器）通常学习人工潜状态而非重构真实的机制性潜状态，因此需要一种既能扩展到大规模组合空间又能重构真实潜状态的方法。

Method: 提出GReinSS，一个策略学习框架，使用动态缩放奖励来学习最大化观测数据似然的潜状态分布。

Result: 在模拟数据上，GReinSS准确重构了潜集和潜图，优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线；在真实RNA测序数据上，GReinSS重构的异构体比标准RSEM算法更匹配正交长读长测序检测到的异构体。

Conclusion: GReinSS是一种理论上合理且实践有效的生成式建模与推断方法，适用于从间接观测中推断组合潜状态。

Original Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

中文摘要翻译: 许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未被观测到的机制性潜状态。经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合规模巨大的空间，而深度学习方法（如变分自编码器）通常形成人工潜状态，而非重构真实的机制性潜状态。本文提出GReinSS，一个策略学习框架，通过使用动态缩放奖励来学习最大化观测数据似然的潜状态分布。我们展示了GReinSS能够准确重构模拟的潜集和潜图，优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线。此外，在真实短读长RNA测序数据上，GReinSS重构的异构体比标准RSEM算法更匹配由正交长读长测序检测到的异构体。总体而言，GReinSS是一种从间接观测中进行组合潜状态生成式建模与推断的理论合理且实践有效的方法。

</details>


### [177] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了在单台8-GPU节点上端到端训练一个千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型（LightningLM 0.1V）的系统与经验。通过四阶段模型生长（从稠密种子模型到5B MoE、9B MoE，最终到120B/460专家），结合可逆循环栈、状态保持增长和单节点经济性（TQP量化策略）三大技术，实现了激活内存平坦化、优化器状态压缩约45倍，最终达到1.78的训练损失。模型、分词器和训练代码已开源。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在资源受限的单节点（8 GPU）环境下，实现千亿参数稀疏MoE模型的高效训练，验证通过模型生长策略从小型稠密模型逐步扩展至大规模MoE模型的可行性与经济性，并提供可复现的实践指南。

Method: 1）可逆循环栈：在反向传播中重构激活值而非存储，保持激活内存随模型增长而平坦；2）状态保持增长：提出可复现的模型扩展原则（稠密→MoE、浅→深、少专家→多专家），并记录各阶段的失败模式；3）单节点经济性（TQP）：量化基础专家权重+训练低秩适配器，将优化器状态从100B+参数降至2.26B适配器参数，压缩约45倍。模型从1.78B稠密种子通过四阶段生长至120B（460路由专家，top-12路由），活跃参数从1.78B增至5.93B。

Result: 完整训练谱系在单节点上运行，大阶段支持8K上下文，120B规模下训练损失达到1.78。通过领域保留损失证实了目标能力（多语言印地语能力、代码能力）是通过构造学习得到的。模型族、分词器和训练代码已开源。

Conclusion: 本文的创新在于已知原语的集成而非单个原语的创新：在单节点上端到端运行一个完整的模型生长谱系，并以实践者级别的文档记录，提供了可复现的大规模MoE训练经验。

Original Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

中文摘要翻译: 本文报告了在单台八GPU节点上端到端训练一个千亿参数稀疏混合专家模型的经验。LightningLM 0.1V是一个基于循环主干的语言模型家族，通过四个阶段从小型稠密种子模型逐步扩展：先成长为5B和9B的混合专家模型，最终达到120B规模，包含460个路由专家（采用top-12路由）。每个更大规模的模型都是从较小模型的训练权重生长而来；活跃参数从稠密种子模型的1.78B单调增加到120B模型的5.93B（约占存储的118.67B参数的5%）。完整的训练谱系均在单节点上运行，较大阶段支持8K上下文，120B规模下达到1.78的发布训练损失。  
  这是一篇系统与经验报告。它围绕三个技术准则展开。可逆性：可逆循环栈在反向传播中重构激活值而非存储激活值，使得激活内存随模型增长保持平坦。状态保持增长：每次扩展（稠密到MoE、浅层到深层、少专家到多专家）均作为可复现的原则给出，并配以错误操作导致的失败案例；其中若干失败是静默的。单节点经济性：120B模型通过TQP策略进行训练，即量化基础专家权重并结合训练的低秩适配器，该策略将优化器状态承载于2.26B适配器参数上，而非驻留在路由专家中的100B+参数上，从而将专家路径的优化器状态压缩约45倍。  
  本文的新颖之处在于已知原语的集成，而非任何单个原语的创新：一个完整的模型生长谱系在单节点上端到端运行，以实践者级别的文档记录，并以领域保留损失作为证据，表明目标能力（多语言印地语能力、代码能力）是通过构造学习得到的。模型家族、分词器和训练代码均已开源。

</details>


### [178] [A Comprehensive Anatomy of Human and DeepSeek-R1 LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07410)
*Yuxiang Chen,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过标注AIME 2025全部30道题中10,247个推理步骤，对比DeepSeek-R1与人类推理，发现模型存在“拓扑模仿”——表面像推理但缺乏逻辑功能，并识别出分支回溯的稳定使用和有效反思位置两个真正推理信号。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究大语言模型（尤其是DeepSeek-R1）在“顿悟时刻”中究竟是真正推理还是仅模仿推理的表象。

Method: 对AIME 2025全部30道题进行穷举标注，将10,247个推理步骤分为5类（分析、推理、分支、回溯、反思），对模型和人类推理进行系统的实证对比。

Result: （1）人类推理保持分析与演绎的紧凑交替；DeepSeek-R1频繁重复中间结果、进行浅层验证和无意义循环。（2）成功的模型轨迹稳定使用分支和回溯，失败轨迹则过度或不足使用探索性动作。（3）反思只有在嵌入演绎推理中才有效，陷入分析循环的反思专注局部细节而忽略全局错误。

Conclusion: 当前长思维链模型可能更多因推理的表象而非真正的演绎进展获得奖励；推理质量取决于反思是否一致出现在恰当的逻辑尺度上；改进方向包括测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚“空转”轨迹、促进深层逻辑修正、将推理算力重新分配到演绎和回溯上。

Original Abstract: The emergence of "Aha moments" in large language models, particularly DeepSeek-R1-0120, has raised the question of whether these systems genuinely reason or merely imitate the appearance of reasoning. We conduct a comprehensive empirical comparison between model and human reasoning across all 30 problems from AIME 2025, exhaustively annotating 10,247 reasoning steps into five functional categories: Analysis, Inference, Branch, Backtrace, and Reflection. We find a clear structural difference. Human solutions maintain a compact alternation between analysis and deduction, whereas DeepSeek-R1 frequently revisits intermediate results, performs shallow and often unnecessary verification, and loops through local checks without meaningful logical progress. We describe this as topological mimicry: reproducing the surface form of reasoning without its functional role. Despite this, we identify two signals of genuine reasoning. First, successful traces exhibit stable use of branching and backtracking, while failed traces either underuse or overuse exploratory actions. Second, reflection is only effective when placed within deductive inference; reflections trapped in analysis loops focus on local numerical details while missing global logical errors. These findings suggest that current long-CoT models may be rewarded more for the appearance of reasoning than for genuine deductive progress. We discuss directions for improving evaluation and training, including measuring cross-trace stability, penalising "spinning-wheel" traces, encouraging deeper logical correction, and reallocating inference-time compute toward deduction and backtracking. Overall, reasoning quality depends not simply on how much reflection occurs, but on whether reflection appears consistently and at the appropriate logical scale.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中“顿悟时刻”的出现，特别是DeepSeek-R1-0120，引发了这些系统是真正推理还是仅仅模仿推理表象的疑问。我们对AIME 2025的全部30道题进行了全面的实证比较，穷举标注了10,247个推理步骤，将其分为五个功能类别：分析、推理、分支、回溯和反思。我们发现了一个明显的结构差异。人类的解决方案保持了分析与演绎之间的紧凑交替，而DeepSeek-R1则频繁回看中间结果，进行浅层且往往不必要的验证，并在没有有意义的逻辑进展的情况下循环进行局部检查。我们将此描述为拓扑模仿：再现推理的表面形式而没有其功能作用。尽管如此，我们识别出了两个真正推理的信号。第一，成功的轨迹展现出分支和回溯的稳定使用，而失败的轨迹要么过度使用、要么不足使用探索性动作。第二，反思只有嵌入在演绎推理中时才有效；被困在分析循环中的反思专注于局部数值细节，却忽略了全局逻辑错误。这些发现表明，当前的长期思维链模型可能更多因推理的表象而非真正的演绎进展获得奖励。我们讨论了改进评估和训练的方向，包括测量跨轨迹稳定性、惩罚“空转”轨迹、鼓励更深的逻辑修正，以及将推理时的计算资源重新分配到演绎和回溯上。总体而言，推理质量不仅取决于反思发生的多少，还取决于反思是否一致地出现在恰当的逻辑尺度上。

</details>


### [179] [Sparsely gated tiny linear experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07414)
*Simon Schug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为sgatlin（稀疏门控线性神经元）的网络结构，将每个专家缩小为单个线性神经元，在极大提升稀疏性的同时去除了非线性激活函数，从而在等计算量条件下提升语言模型的困惑度，并增强前馈电路的可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现存MoE模型中专家仍然庞大且稠密，稀疏性提升有限；同时，非线性激活函数阻碍了模型的可解释性。作者希望进一步增加稀疏性并提高计算效率，同时提升模型的可解释性。

Method: 将每个MoE专家缩小为单个神经元，并从大量可用神经元中选取极小一部分激活；移除专家中的非线性激活函数，得到稀疏门控线性神经元网络（sgatlin），并将其替换Transformer中的所有前馈层。

Result: 在等计算量（isoflop）比较下，sgatlin在不同计算预算的语言模型中均提升了困惑度；sgatlin的前馈电路形成语义结构化的聚类，并在事实回忆任务中具有因果作用，无需训练额外的替代模型即可被解释。

Conclusion: sgatlin为构建计算高效且可解释的Transformer前馈层提供了一条可行的路径。

Original Abstract: Sparsity allows scaling model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost. While mixture of experts (MoE) models are made increasingly sparse, individual experts typically remain large and dense. Here, we demonstrate that further increasing sparsity by shrinking each expert to consist of a single neuron and selecting a tiny fraction of many available neurons can improve compute efficiency and interpretability. Counterintuitively, the key to achieving both is removing the nonlinearity typically applied to the experts, resulting in a network of sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin). In an isoflop comparison, we find that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity in language models across different compute budgets. At the same time, the sparsity and linearity of the resulting feedforward circuits present new opportunities for model interpretability. In a small-scale case study, we demonstrate that feedforward circuits in sgatlin can be interpreted without having to train additional replacement models. We find that they form semantically structured clusters and are causally implicated in factual recall. Our findings paint a possible path towards compute-efficient and interpretable transformer feedforward layers.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏性允许在不成比例增加计算成本的情况下扩展模型参数。尽管混合专家（MoE）模型的稀疏性不断提高，但单个专家通常仍然庞大且稠密。本文证明，通过将每个专家缩小为单个神经元并从大量可用神经元中选择极小一部分，可以进一步提升稀疏性，从而提高计算效率和可解释性。反直觉的是，实现这两个目标的关键在于移除通常应用于专家的非线性激活函数，从而得到一个稀疏门控线性神经元（sgatlin）网络。在等计算量比较下，我们发现用sgatlin替换所有Transformer前馈层可以在不同计算预算下改善语言模型的困惑度。同时，由此产生的前馈电路的稀疏性和线性为模型可解释性提供了新的可能性。在一个小规模案例研究中，我们证明了sgatlin中的前馈电路可以在无需训练额外替代模型的情况下被解释。我们发现它们形成了语义结构化的聚类，并且在事实回忆中具有因果作用。我们的研究结果为构建计算高效且可解释的Transformer前馈层描绘了一条可能的路径。

</details>


### [180] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用高速视频观测等离子体羽流，通过TabPFN、CNN及多种回归方法预测大气等离子喷涂中飞行粒子温度与速度的可行性，结果表明视频特征可实现非侵入式实时过程监测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: APS过程中飞行粒子的温度与速度对涂层质量至关重要，但运行中难以连续监测，因此需要开发非侵入式的数据驱动诊断方法。

Method: 提取三种视频衍生特征表示，采用Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN)、卷积神经网络(CNN)、随机森林、梯度提升、支持向量回归和XGBoost等模型进行预测，并进行分组留一法交叉验证；同时评估基于预训练CNN直接处理原始视频帧的性能。

Result: TabPFN在温度预测上表现最优（R²=0.86）；CNN在速度预测上更强（R²=0.81）；预训练CNN直接处理原始视频帧取得最佳综合性能，温度和速度预测R²分别达0.90和0.82。

Conclusion: 视频衍生的羽流信息为非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监测提供了有前景且可扩展的基础。

Original Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

中文摘要翻译: 大气等离子喷涂(APS)是一种广泛应用的涂层工艺，其中飞行粒子的温度和速度对涂层质量有着重要影响。然而，这些粒子特性在运行过程中难以连续监测，因此推动了非侵入式数据驱动诊断方法的发展。本文研究了利用高速视频观测等离子体羽流来估计APS中飞行粒子特性的预测潜力。我们引入了三种不同的视频衍生特征表示，并使用Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN)、卷积神经网络(CNN)以及包括随机森林、梯度提升、支持向量回归和XGBoost在内的经典回归基线方法进行评估。实验采用分组留一法交叉验证，在来自63次APS喷涂运行的126个标记喷涂前后视频记录上进行。在工程化特征实验中，TabPFN在温度预测上表现最为稳定，使用组合特征表示时R²达到0.86。CNN模型在速度预测上表现更强，R²达到0.81。此外，我们评估了使用预训练CNN直接处理原始视频帧的模型，发现带有回归头的预训练CNN取得了最高性能，温度和速度预测的R²分别为0.90和0.82。结果表明，视频衍生的羽流信息为非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监测提供了有前景且可扩展的基础。

</details>


### [181] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Deflex是一种端到端AI方法，能从多尺度复杂系统中自动提取不同形式的尺度特定公式（包括不变量和分布），在六个代表性系统上比现有最优方法效率提升高达7倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有AI方法在单尺度系统中表现优异，但在多尺度复杂系统中难以识别尺度特定的公式，亟需一种能自动解耦多尺度潜在关系并发现不同形式数学公式的方法。

Method: Deflex由两个子系统组成：Deflexpressor（基于lambda演算的符号回归模型，用于高阶公式发现）和Deflexformer（可分解深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表征）。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统上，Deflex比现有最优方法的效率提升高达7倍，并实现了自动化的多尺度公式发现。

Conclusion: Deflex为跨学科科学发现提供了一种高效、自动化的多尺度公式提取工具。

Original Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas.
