Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

中文摘要翻译: 科学中的一个基本问题是以简洁数学公式的形式识别复杂系统的潜在模式。当前基于人工智能（AI）的方法在单尺度系统中表现出强大的性能，但在识别多尺度复杂系统中的尺度特定公式方面仍然有限。我们提出了Deflex，一种端到端的AI方法，能够从复杂系统中自动提取可能具有不同形式（包括不变量和分布）的多尺度公式。Deflex由两个子系统组成，分别命名为Deflexformer和Deflexpressor。Deflexpressor是一个用于高阶公式的lambda演算符号回归模型。Deflexformer是一个可分解的深度能量模型，用于学习跨尺度的统一表征。Deflexpressor生成合成数据以预训练Deflexformer，然后Deflexformer通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统上，Deflex的效率比现有最优方法高出7倍，同时实现了自动化的多尺度发现。我们的工作可以成为跨学科科学发现的有用工具。

</details>


### [182] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督持续聚类方法FBCC，通过前向-后向知识蒸馏，在不存储旧数据的情况下持续学习新聚类，同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督持续学习方法忽略了聚类特定目标，且知识蒸馏和重放缓冲等策略存在内存和隐私问题，亟需一种无需标签、不存储旧数据且能保持聚类结构的持续学习方法。

Method: FBCC包含一个持续教师网络（带聚类投影头）和轻量级任务特定学生网络，通过前向-后向知识蒸馏双阶段过程，使教师在学习新聚类的同时保留先前发现的聚类结构，无需存储历史数据。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有持续学习基线，并显著减少了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: FBCC作为无监督持续聚类领域的开创性方法，有效解决了无监督场景下的聚类持续学习问题，兼顾了聚类性能与抗遗忘能力。

Original Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

中文摘要翻译: 无监督持续学习旨在使神经网络能够在没有标签或无法访问先前数据的情况下学习连续任务。该设置下的一个主要挑战是灾难性遗忘，即模型在学习新任务时会遗忘先前学过的任务。由于缺乏标签来引导学习和记忆保持，这一挑战在无监督持续学习中被进一步放大。现有的缓解策略（如知识蒸馏和重放缓冲）常引发内存和隐私问题。此外，当前的無监督持续学习方法在很大程度上忽略了聚类特定的目标。为填补这一空白，我们引入了无监督持续聚类这一任务，并提出了面向持续聚类的前向-后向知识蒸馏方法。FBCC采用了一个带有聚类投影头的持续教师网络以及轻量级的任务特定学生网络。通过一个双阶段的前向-后向蒸馏过程，教师在学习新聚类的同时能够保留先前发现的聚类结构，且无需存储历史数据。FBCC代表了无监督持续聚类领域的一种开创性方法，展示了其在连续任务上改进聚类性能的能力。在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，FBCC在聚类准确率上持续优于现有的持续学习基线，同时显著减少了灾难性遗忘。

</details>


### [183] [Graph Neural Network leveraging Higher-order Class Label Connectivity for Heterophilous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07475)
*Takuto Takahashi,Itsuki Nakayama,Takahiro Mitani,Ryosuke Kikuchi,Yuya Sasaki,Makoto Onizuka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为标签上下文分类器（LCC）的新方法，用于捕获有向异配图中的高阶类标签连通性，通过与任意GNN集成提升节点分类性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GNN在同配图上表现优异，但在异配图上性能有限，尤其是无法捕获真实世界异配图中常见的高阶类标签连通性。

Method: 提出标签上下文分类器（LCC），通过四种不同类型的游走生成标签上下文嵌入，估计目标节点的类标签；并支持LCC与任意GNN自适应学习重要性后进行集成。

Result: 实验结果表明，与LCC集成的GNN优于当前最先进方法，标签上下文嵌入能有效提升异配有向图上的节点分类性能。

Conclusion: LCC通过捕获高阶类标签连通性，有效解决了GNN在异配图上性能受限的问题，且具有良好的可集成性。

Original Abstract: Node classification in graph neural networks (GNNs) has been widely applied in various fields of graph analysis. GNNs achieve high-accuracy node classification in homophilous graphs, where nodes with the same class label tend to be connected. However, their performance remains limited in heterophilous graphs, where nodes with different class labels are more likely to be connected. In particular, current GNNs derived from graph convolutional networks cannot capture higher-order class label connectivity, which is frequently observed in real-world heterophilous graphs. To address this issue, we propose a novel classifier, Label Context Classifier (LCC), designed to capture higher-order class label connectivity in directed graphs. LCC estimates the class label of a target node by leveraging label context embeddings that are generated through four distinct types of walks. In addition, our approach allows the integration of LCC and any GNN by adaptively learning their importance. Experimental results demonstrate that GNNs integrated with LCC outperform SOTA methods and the label context embeddings improve the node classification performance in heterophilous directed graphs.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络（GNN）中的节点分类已广泛应用于图分析的各个领域。在同配图中，具有相同类标签的节点倾向于相互连接，GNN能实现高精度的节点分类。然而，在异配图中，不同类标签的节点更可能相互连接，GNN的性能仍然受限。特别地，当前源自图卷积网络的GNN无法捕获高阶类标签连通性，而这在真实世界的异配图中经常出现。为解决该问题，我们提出了一种新颖的分类器——标签上下文分类器（LCC），旨在捕获有向图中的高阶类标签连通性。LCC通过利用四种不同类型的游走生成的标签上下文嵌入来估计目标节点的类标签。此外，我们的方法允许LCC与任意GNN通过自适应学习它们的重要性进行集成。实验结果表明，与LCC集成的GNN优于当前最先进方法，并且标签上下文嵌入提升了异配有向图中的节点分类性能。

</details>


### [184] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件漂移（conditional drifting）的生成式代理模型，用于计算流体动力学（CFD）仿真，解决了传统CFD计算成本高、扩散模型采样慢的问题，实现了高质量、单次生成的实时替代方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: CFD虽能提供高保真流场，但计算成本高，难以快速探索设计空间；现有生成式代理模型（如扩散模型）虽比确定性网络分布建模更好，但迭代采样速度慢，无法满足实时推理需求。

Method: 将生成式漂移框架（generative drifting）适配到流体力学领域；构建条件架构，在学习的VAE潜空间中执行漂移过程；采用标签感知掩码（label-aware masking）使生成样本与其边界条件对齐；此外提出空间条件变体以泛化到未见过的几何结构。

Result: 标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上匹敌迭代扩散模型，但运行速度快两个数量级；空间条件变体展现了对未见几何结构的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 条件漂移方法可作为扩散模型的高效替代方案，在推理速度至关重要的场景中解锁实时CFD代理模型。

Original Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

中文摘要翻译: 虽然计算流体动力学（CFD）可以为优化室内环境提供高保真流场，但其计算成本限制了快速探索。为解决这一问题，生成式代理模型相比确定性网络能提供更好的分布建模，但迭代采样速度较慢。为了实现高质量的单次生成，我们将新颖的生成式漂移框架适配到流体力学领域。我们引入了一种条件架构，在学习的VAE潜空间中执行漂移，并使用标签感知掩码将生成样本与其边界条件对齐。我们的标签条件模型在精度和流一致性上匹敌迭代扩散模型，同时运行速度快两个数量级。此外，我们提出了一种空间条件变体，为泛化到未见几何结构开辟了有前景的路径。最终，条件漂移作为基于扩散方法的高效替代方案，在推理速度至关重要的场景中解锁了实时CFD代理模型。

</details>


### [185] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CascadeNet，一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，无需指定扩散模型即可从动态级联数据中恢复隐藏的影响网络，具有$\sqrt{n}$一致性和渐近正态性，支持统计推断。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法通常假设特定的扩散模型，当模型设定错误时性能显著下降，因此需要一种不依赖特定扩散机制的通用网络恢复方法。

Method: CascadeNet首先构建一个灵活的单步转移函数估计器，然后利用其雅可比矩阵刻画底层影响结构，并通过Riesz表示子进行Neyman正交去偏，得到去偏后的雅可比矩阵，实现$\sqrt{n}$一致性和渐近正态性。

Result: 在模拟实验中，CascadeNet在九种常见数据生成过程中取得了最高的网络恢复精度；在西班牙52个省份的新冠传播实证应用中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实的省际移动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络与真实网络无显著对齐。

Conclusion: CascadeNet能够在无需指定扩散模型的前提下高效恢复隐藏影响网络，并且支持正式的统计推断，在多种场景下优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

中文摘要翻译: 许多重要的结果以动态级联的形式展开，包括产品采纳、疾病传播、金融困境和信息扩散。其中一个核心挑战是恢复这些级联背后隐藏的影响网络。现有方法通常假设特定的扩散模型，当该假设被错误设定时，其性能会显著下降。我们提出CascadeNet，一种基于雅可比矩阵的机器学习框架，用于网络恢复，无需指定扩散机制。关键思想在于，底层影响结构可以通过单步转移函数的雅可比矩阵来刻画。CascadeNet首先构建一个灵活的转移函数估计器，进一步通过Riesz表示子应用Neyman正交去偏，使得去偏后的雅可比矩阵具有$\sqrt{n}$一致性和渐近正态性，从而能够对网络结构进行正式的统计推断。我们在模拟实验和真实世界实证应用中对CascadeNet进行了验证。在模拟中，数据生成过程已知的情况下，CascadeNet在九种常见数据生成过程中达到了最高的网络恢复精度。在西班牙52个省份的新冠传播实证应用中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实的省际移动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络与真实网络无显著对齐。

</details>


### [186] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种持续元学习框架，用于个性化心脏神经代理模型，使其能够在新数据序列化到达时持续整合信息并识别数据来源，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于少样本生成建模和元学习的个性化心脏模拟方法假设训练分布是静态且多样化的，且有已知任务标识符。当新数据序列化到达时，这些方法需要重新利用全部旧数据进行代价高昂的重新训练以避免灾难性遗忘，这在临床场景中不可行。

Method: 提出持续元学习框架，利用基于记忆缓冲区的持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型（Continual Bayesian GMM），随时间推断数据的标识符和关系，从而实现有效的元学习。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上的实验结果表明，该方法在模拟预测、计算可扩展性和对灾难性遗忘的鲁棒性方面均优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的持续元学习框架能够有效解决个性化心脏神经代理模型在序列化数据到达时的持续学习和灾难性遗忘问题，为临床场景下的动态数据整合提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 个性化虚拟心脏模拟面临着模型个性化和计算成本方面的挑战。尽管神经代理模型提供了最先进的解决方案，但它们通常只解决高效个性化或训练可泛化模型中的某一个方面。近期的工作通过少样本生成建模、集合条件代理模型和元学习摊销推理，将这一问题重新定义为使用有限的受试者特定上下文数据来学习个性化代理模型的过程。然而，这些方法假设训练数据分布是静态且多样化的，并且具有已知的任务标识符。当新数据可用时，它们需要使用所有先前数据进行代价高昂的重新训练，以避免灾难性遗忘——即模型在学习新任务时遗忘旧任务的现象。这在临床环境中是一个重大限制，因为未标记数据通常是序列化到达的，而完全重新训练是不可行的。本文提出了一种新的持续元学习框架，以实现个性化神经代理模型，该模型不仅能够持续整合信息，还能识别新到达的数据是来自已知还是未知的动力学来源。通过利用基于记忆缓冲区的持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型，我们的框架能够随时间推断数据的标识符及其关系——这是实现有效元学习所必需的。在合成心脏数据上的实验结果表明，与现有基线方法相比，我们的方法在模拟预测、计算可扩展性以及对灾难性遗忘的鲁棒性方面均表现更优。

</details>


### [187] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Pedro Domingos提出的路径核插值公式基础上，发展了其二阶形式，考虑了曲率加权插值项、随机梯度下降中的采样噪声项以及带动量的SGD中的记忆因子修正，并给出了预测浓度的集中估计，从而精细化了对神经网络预测的路径核解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的路径核插值公式仅为一阶表征，无法捕捉曲率（二阶）信息和随机优化中的噪声影响，因此需要发展二阶形式的插值公式以更精确地解释训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测。

Method: 本文通过理论推导，在确定性梯度下降的基础上引入二阶曲率加权插值项；针对随机梯度下降，补充了由小批量梯度噪声协方差与预测曲率耦合产生的采样诱导项；并将表示扩展到带动量的SGD，引入记忆因子修正权重；最后建立了终端预测的浓度估计。

Result: 推导出了二阶形式的路径核插值公式，包含曲率加权项；对于SGD，得到了耦合梯度噪声协方差与预测曲率的额外项；对于带动量的SGD，插值结构保持不变但权重被记忆因子修正；并给出了预测围绕期望二阶表示的波动尺度集中估计。

Conclusion: 这些结果共同提供了对神经网络预测的路径核解释的精炼，更完整地刻画了训练数据通过一阶和二阶结构影响模型预测的机制。

Original Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 理解训练数据如何塑造神经网络预测是现代学习理论的核心问题。2020年，Pedro Domingos提出了一个适用于确定性梯度下降所学习到的任意模型的插值公式。该公式将模型的预测表达为沿着优化路径对数据相关核的积分，该核在测试数据和训练数据上对齐了模型的梯度。这种一阶表征对于基于批量的随机优化训练的模型仍然有效。本文发展了这些插值公式的二阶形式。我们表明，领先的路径核插值由一个曲率加权的插值项补充。对于随机梯度下降，一个额外的采样诱导分量出现，将预测的曲率与小批量梯度噪声的协方差耦合。我们还将表示扩展到带动量的随机梯度下降，其中插值结构保持不变，但权重由记忆相关因子修正。此外，我们建立了终端预测的浓度估计，识别了围绕期望二阶表示的波动尺度。总之，这些结果提供了对神经网络预测的路径核解释的精炼。

</details>


### [188] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MG-ADSGD算法，通过将八卦轮次与小批量大小耦合，同时实现加速的通信复杂度 \(\sqrt{κ/(1-β)}\) 依赖，在去中心化随机强凸优化中取得了当前最优的通信复杂度上界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的去中心化随机优化方法无法同时实现加速的 \(\sqrt{κ}\) 和 \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) 依赖，而确定性方法可以。这促使作者寻找一种能同时获得两种加速效果的随机方法。

Method: 将Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速八卦平均相结合，并通过将八卦深度与小批量大小耦合，使得额外的通信轮次同时提高共识精度和降低梯度方差。

Result: 通信复杂度为 \(\widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\frac{κ}{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right)\)，据作者所知，这是去中心化随机强凸优化中当前最好的通信复杂度（忽略与ε无关的对数因子）。

Conclusion: MG-ADSGD首次在去中心化随机强凸优化中同时实现了加速的 \(\sqrt{κ}\) 和 \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) 依赖，达到了当前最优的通信复杂度。

Original Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

中文摘要翻译: 去中心化随机优化是大规模网络学习的基本范式，其中智能体仅与其邻居通信，无需中央协调器。对于强凸问题，通信效率主要由条件数 \(κ=L/μ\) 和网络谱间隙 \(1-β\) 决定。尽管确定性去中心化方法可以同时实现加速的 \(\sqrt{κ}\) 和 \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) 依赖，但现有的随机方法均无法同时获得这两方面的改进。本文提出\textbf{多八卦加速去中心化随机梯度下降}（MG-ADSGD），这是一种结合了Nesterov型原始-对偶外推与多轮快速八卦平均的去中心化随机算法。其关键思想是将八卦深度与小批量大小耦合，使得额外的通信轮次同时提高共识精度并降低梯度方差。我们证明MG-ADSGD达到了以下通信复杂度：\[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε + \sqrt{\frac{κ}{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] 其中 \(ε\) 表示目标精度，\(n\) 是节点数，\(σ^2\) 是梯度方差。据我们所知，该上界在去中心化随机强凸优化中取得了当前最佳的通信复杂度（忽略与 \(ε\) 无关的对数因子）。

</details>


### [189] [Sparse Subspace-to-Expert Sharing for Task-Agnostic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07500)
*Fatema Siddika,Md Anwar Hossen,Tanwi Mallick,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SETA通过将参数分解为任务特定专家和共享专家，并引入自适应弹性锚定与路由感知正则化，解决了LLM持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性困境，在多个基准上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有持续学习方法对参数统一处理，未能区分任务特定知识与共享能力，导致新任务学习时灾难性遗忘旧知识。

Method: 提出SETA框架，通过自适应稀疏子空间分解将参数划分为任务特定专家（隔离任务特有模式）和共享专家（捕获通用特征），并采用自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化在权重和路由层面保护共享知识，统一门控网络在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。

Result: 在多个领域特定基准上，SETA在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上相比SOTA持续学习方法取得竞争性或更优的整体性能，尤其在早期任务知识保留和后向迁移方面表现突出。

Conclusion: SETA通过稀疏专家混合与结构化参数分解有效解决了持续学习中的可塑性-稳定性冲突，实现了对新任务的学习能力与旧知识的保留之间的良好平衡。

Original Abstract: Continual learning in Large Language Models (LLMs) is hindered by the plasticity-stability dilemma, where acquiring new capabilities often leads to catastrophic forgetting of previous knowledge. Existing methods typically treat parameters uniformly, failing to distinguish between specific task knowledge and shared capabilities. We introduce Mixture of Sparse Experts for Task Agnostic Continual Learning (SETA), a framework that resolves the plasticity-stability conflict through adaptive sparse subspace decomposition into task-specific expert modules. Unlike standard updates, where tasks compete for the same parameters, SETA separates knowledge into unique experts, designed to isolate task-specific patterns, and shared experts, responsible for capturing common features. This structure is maintained through adaptive elastic anchoring and a routing-aware regularization that jointly protect shared knowledge at both the weight and routing levels and enable a unified gating network to automatically retrieve the correct expert combination during inference. Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

中文摘要翻译: 大规模语言模型（LLM）的持续学习受到可塑性-稳定性困境的阻碍：获取新能力往往导致对先前知识的灾难性遗忘。现有方法通常对参数进行统一处理，未能区分特定任务知识与共享能力。我们提出了用于任务不可知持续学习的稀疏专家混合框架（SETA），该框架通过将参数自适应稀疏子空间分解为任务特定专家模块来解决可塑性-稳定性冲突。与标准更新中任务竞争相同参数不同，SETA将知识分离为独特的专家——设计用于隔离任务特定模式的特定专家和负责捕获通用特征的共享专家。该结构通过自适应弹性锚定和路由感知正则化来维持，后者在权重和路由层面共同保护共享知识，并使统一门控网络能够在推理时自动检索正确的专家组合。跨多个领域特定基准的大量实验表明，SETA相对于最先进的持续学习基线取得了具有竞争力或更优的整体性能，在LLaMA-2 7B和Qwen3-4B上尤其展现出强大的早期任务知识保留能力和改进的后向迁移。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [SEMIKHORN: Globally balanced affinities for mmWave Localization in MU mMIMO systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06640)
*Abhisha Garg,Raghav Shukla,Suraj Srivastava,Aditya K. Jagannatham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出SEMIKHORN半监督信道图表框架，利用t-SNEkhorn（基于熵最优传输的t-SNE变体）进行毫米波定位，在仅需不到15%标注CSI样本的情况下，实现半径100m范围内6.86%的平均定位误差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统t-SNE对每个数据点独立归一化亲和度，导致邻域表示不一致；毫米波定位中需要更鲁棒的信道图表方法以减少对大量标注数据的依赖。

Method: 采用t-SNEkhorn（基于熵最优传输的双随机t-SNE变体）生成全局平衡的相似度矩阵；各分布式基站从CSI构建局部不相似矩阵，融合为全局不相似矩阵后通过流形学习嵌入用户位置；使用贝叶斯优化超参数以最小化平均定位误差。

Result: 在模拟室外环境中，半径100m范围内平均定位误差(MLE)为6.86%，仅需不到15%的标注CSI样本。

Conclusion: SEMIKHORN框架通过t-SNEkhorn实现全局平衡的邻域表示，有效融合多基站CSI信息，在半监督场景下实现高精度毫米波定位。

Original Abstract: This work conceives SEMIKHORN, a semisupervised channel charting (CC) framework for mmWave localization, which leverages t-SNEkhorn, a doubly stochastic variant of t-distributed Stochastic Neighbor Embedding (t-SNE) that utilizes entropic optimal transport to construct pairwise similarities. Unlike standard t-SNE, which normalizes affinities independently for each data point, t-SNEkhorn generates globally balanced similarities ensuring consistent neighborhood representation. We consider wireless networks with distributed base stations (BSs) equipped with multiple antennas, where each BS constructs a local dissimilarity matrix from the channel state information (CSI). These local dissimilarity matrices are then fused to obtain a single global dissimilarity matrix, which is processed through manifold learning to embed users onto a geometric map. The performance is evaluated in a simulated outdoor environment, and Bayesian optimization is employed on the framework hyperparameters to minimize the mean localization error (MLE). Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves an MLE of 6.86% in a circular vicinity of radius 100m, requiring less than 15% of labeled CSI samples.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了SEMIKHORN——一种用于毫米波定位的半监督信道图表(CC)框架。该框架利用t-SNEkhorn——一种基于t分布随机邻域嵌入(t-SNE)的双随机变体，通过熵最优传输构建成对相似度。与标准t-SNE对每个数据点独立归一化亲和度不同，t-SNEkhorn生成全局平衡的相似度矩阵，确保邻域表示的一致性。我们考虑配备多天线的分布式基站(BS)构成的无线网络，每个基站从信道状态信息(CSI)构建局部不相似矩阵。这些局部不相似矩阵被融合为单个全局不相似矩阵，再通过流形学习将用户嵌入到几何地图上。在模拟室外环境中评估性能，并采用贝叶斯优化对框架超参数进行调优以最小化平均定位误差(MLE)。实验结果表明，所提出的框架在半径100m的圆形区域内实现了6.86%的平均定位误差，仅需不到15%的标注CSI样本。

</details>


### [191] [Variational Bayes Estimation for Affine-Precoded Superimposed Pilots in Partially Connected Dual-Wideband Tera-Hertz MU-MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06672)
*Abhisha Garg,Suraj Srivastava,Aditya K. Jagannatham*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于仿射预编码的太赫兹MIMO系统模型（CP-JCE和USP-DCE），并利用叠加导频和变分贝叶斯推断算法实现高精度的结构化稀疏信道估计，最后对两种系统进行了性能比较与权衡分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统基于导频的信道估计方法在太赫兹MIMO系统中会产生巨大的带宽开销，亟需更高效的方案。

Method: 提出两种仿射预编码系统模型（CP-JCE和USP-DCE）；对双宽带效应下的部分连接架构进行精确信道建模；采用叠加导频代替传统导频；构建结构化稀疏信道模型；开发变分贝叶斯推断算法，联合估计信道系数并学习底层稀疏结构。

Result: 在严重模型不确定性下实现了鲁棒且高精度的叠加导频信道估计；并对两种系统进行了性能对比和权衡分析。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效降低导频开销，同时保证信道估计精度，两种系统各有优劣，需根据场景进行权衡。

Original Abstract: This work conceives two affine precoding based system models, common precoding with joint channel estimation (CP-JCE) and user-specific precoding for decoupled channel estimation (USPDCE). Considering a dual-wideband effected partially connected architecture, we rigorously model the terahertz (THz) multiple input multiple output (MIMO) channel for each subarray corresponding to each user by incorporating the absorption, reflection, and freespace losses. Next, to address the significant bandwidth overhead associated with conventional pilot-based channel estimation, we employ superimposed pilots. Building on this, we formulate a structured sparse channel model and develop a variational Bayesian inference algorithm that jointly estimates the channel coefficients and learns the underlying sparsity structure through hyperparameter inference, thereby enabling robust and high-precision superimposed pilotbased channel estimation under severe model uncertainty. Lastly, we compare our results for both systems and provide a trade-off analysis between them.

中文摘要翻译: 本文构思了两种基于仿射预编码的系统模型：联合信道估计的公共预编码（CP-JCE）和用于解耦信道估计的特定用户预编码（USP-DCE）。针对双宽带效应的部分连接架构，我们通过对每个用户对应的子阵列进行吸收、反射和自由空间损耗的建模，严格建立了太赫兹多输入多输出（MIMO）信道模型。其次，为解决传统基于导频的信道估计带来的巨大带宽开销问题，我们采用叠加导频方案。在此基础上，我们构建了结构化稀疏信道模型，并开发了一种变分贝叶斯推断算法，该算法通过超参数推断联合估计信道系数并学习底层稀疏结构，从而在严重的模型不确定性下实现了鲁棒且高精度的叠加导频信道估计。最后，我们比较了两种系统的结果，并进行了权衡分析。

</details>


### [192] [Deep Learning Based Sparse Array Design with Pre-Steering for Adaptive Beamforming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06723)
*Ian Straub,Syed A Hamza*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于卷积神经网络(CNN)的深度学习方法，用于学习稀疏阵列配置，实现近最优波束成形。通过阵列预导向策略和鲁棒误差增强训练，该方法在动态传播环境下无需针对每个源角度重新训练，即可快速重构稀疏阵列，在宽范围源和干扰角度下实现超过90%的测试精度和最大信干噪比(SINR)。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统或基于凸优化的稀疏阵列配置算法在高度动态的传播环境中难以快速重配置，无法满足实时性的需求。本文旨在利用深度学习实现稀疏阵列的快速重构，以应对动态变化的环境。

Method: 提出CNN学习稀疏阵列配置；引入阵列预导向(pre-steering)策略，仅在阵列侧射方向训练网络，测试时对输入进行预导向对齐；针对实际缺陷，采用鲁棒误差增强训练，在训练中引入小的结构化预导向扰动，使网络在角度不确定性下仍保持高分类精度和最大SINR。

Result: 所提方法在宽范围源和干扰角度下实现超过90%的测试准确率，有效最大化信号与干扰加噪声比(SINR)。

Conclusion: 该方法在动态环境中具有实时、鲁棒的稀疏阵列配置潜力，能显著提升波束成形性能。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates the use of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) for learning sparse array configurations that achieve near-optimal beamforming under varying source and interference angles. Unlike conventional or convex optimization based algorithms, the proposed deep learning approach enables rapid reconfiguration of sparse arrays in highly dynamic propagation environments. The paper considers a single desired source and a single interference signal at arbitrary angles, analyzing scenarios with both fixed and varying desired source directions. To avoid retraining for each possible source angle, an array pre-steering strategy is introduced, whereby the network is trained only at broadside, while test inputs are pre-steered to align with the broadside direction. To account for practical imperfections, the effect of pre-steering errors is examined, and a robust error-augmented training is adopted. The approach systematically incorporates small, structured pre-steering perturbations during training, enabling the network to maintain high classification accuracy and maximize the signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) even under angular uncertainty. The results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves over 90% test accuracy across wide ranges of source and interference angles, highlighting its potential for real-time, robust sparse array configuration in dynamic environments.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了使用卷积神经网络（CNN）学习稀疏阵列配置，以实现随源和干扰角度变化下的近最优波束成形。与传统或基于凸优化的算法不同，所提出的深度学习方法能够在高度动态的传播环境中快速重构稀疏阵列。本文考虑单个期望源和单个干扰信号位于任意角度的情况，分析了期望源方向固定和变化两种场景。为避免对每种可能的源角度重新训练，引入了一种阵列预导向策略，即网络仅在侧射方向训练，而测试输入通过预导向与侧射方向对齐。为考虑实际缺陷，研究了预导向误差的影响，并采用了鲁棒误差增强训练。该方法在训练过程中系统地引入小的结构化预导向扰动，使网络即使在角度不确定性下也能保持高分类精度并最大化信号与干扰加噪声比（SINR）。结果表明，所提出的方法在宽范围的源和干扰角度下实现了超过90%的测试准确率，凸显了其在动态环境中实现实时、鲁棒的稀疏阵列配置的潜力。

</details>


### [193] [Angular Sector-Based Sparse Array Design for Adaptive Beamforming Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06732)
*John Kobak,Ethan Atiyeh,Syed A Hamza*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对宽角扇区优化的稀疏阵列设计框架，通过角扇区类别约简策略和轻量级CNN/ResNet50网络实现近最优波束赋形，在保持高SINR性能的同时减少不必要的阵列重构，适用于实时认知感知与自适应干扰抑制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在动态射频环境中，干扰快速变化要求稀疏阵列既具备适应性又保持稳定性，现有方法难以同时满足这两点，因此需要一种能在大角度范围内实现近最优波束赋形、同时降低重构频率的框架。

Method: 计算候选配置的全数据相关矩阵，采用角扇区类别约简策略合并由相同配置主导的相邻扇区，得到56个代表性类别；通过受控的上采样和下采样构建四种数据集变体（高/低样本量、平衡/不平衡），分别训练轻量级CNN和ResNet50网络实现稀疏阵列选择。

Result: ResNet50分类准确率最高达97.3%，大多数类别的SINR偏差低于1%，即使靠近阵列法线的困难干扰角度，SINR偏差也低于5%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够实现鲁棒的稀疏阵列选择，保持优异的SINR性能，减少不必要的阵列重构，为实时认知感知和自适应干扰抑制提供了有效框架。

Original Abstract: Efficient sparse array reconfigurability is essential for cognitive sensing in dynamic radio frequency environments, where rapid interference variations require both adaptability and stability. This work presents a framework for designing sparse arrays optimized over broad angular sectors, enabling near-optimal beamforming that maximizes the signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) across a range of interferer angles. Full data correlation matrices are computed for candidate configurations, and an angular-sector-based class reduction strategy is applied to merge adjacent sectors dominated by the same configuration, resulting in 56 representative classes. Controlled up- and down-sampling produce four dataset variants involving, high and low sample count, balanced and unbalanced datasets, to systematically evaluate the effects of dataset size and class distribution on neural network performance. A lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) and a deeper ResNet 50 architecture are trained and evaluated using these datasets. Results demonstrate high classification accuracy, with ResNet 50 achieving up to 97.3%, while SINR deviations remain below 1% for most classes and below 5% even for challenging interference angles near broadside. The proposed approach enables robust sparse array selection, maintains strong SINR performance, reduces unnecessary reconfigurations, and provides an effective framework for real-time cognitive sensing and adaptive interference mitigation.

中文摘要翻译: 高效的稀疏阵列可重构性对于动态射频环境中的认知感知至关重要，因为快速变化的干扰既要求适应性也要求稳定性。本文提出了一种针对宽角扇区优化的稀疏阵列设计框架，能够实现近最优波束赋形，在一系列干扰角度范围内最大化信干噪比（SINR）。计算候选配置的全数据相关矩阵，并采用基于角扇区的类别约简策略，合并由相同配置主导的相邻扇区，最终得到56个代表性类别。通过受控的上采样和下采样构建四种数据集变体，分别对应高/低样本量以及平衡/不平衡数据集，以系统评估数据集规模和类别分布对神经网络性能的影响。训练并评估了轻量级卷积神经网络（CNN）和更深层的ResNet50架构。结果表明，分类准确率较高，ResNet50最高可达97.3%；大多数类别的SINR偏差低于1%，即使对于靠近阵列法线的困难干扰角度，SINR偏差也低于5%。所提出的方法能够实现鲁棒的稀疏阵列选择，保持优异的SINR性能，减少不必要的重构，为实时认知感知和自适应干扰抑制提供了有效框架。

</details>


### [194] [Copula Function Parameter Regions in Analyzing Wireless Communications Performances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06792)
*Mona Mohsenzadeh,Saeid Pakravan,Ghosheh Abed Hodtani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文引入copula依赖参数区域的概念，以双变量FGM copula建模的两用户无线MAC信道为例，从通信理论和概率视角推导出显式参数区域，发现实际通信和统计需求会显著缩小经典copula允许区间，使部分理论上可行的依赖结构在实际中不可行。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究通常直接用copula依赖参数表达性能指标，而未显式刻画这些参数的可行区域，导致理论上可用的依赖结构可能在实际通信场景中不可实现。本文旨在填补这一空白，引入copula依赖参数区域概念并探讨其在无线通信中的重要性。

Method: 以双变量Farlie-Gumbel-Morgenstern (FGM) copula建模两用户无线多址接入信道（MAC）中的相关瑞利衰落，从中断概率和皮尔逊相关系数（PCC）约束出发，从通信理论和概率视角分别推导出显式的参数可行区域。

Result: 实际通信和统计需求会显著缩小经典的copula允许区间，使得部分理论上可接受的依赖结构在实际中不可行。

Conclusion: 明确了通信约束和统计约束对copula依赖参数可行区域的限制作用，为无线通信中copula建模的实际应用提供了更精确的参数选择指导。

Original Abstract: Copula functions have been widely employed in wireless communication analysis to model dependence structures and evaluate system performance. However, existing studies generally express performance metrics in terms of copula dependence parameters without explicitly characterizing their admissible regions. This letter introduces the concept of copula dependence parameter regions and investigates its significance in wireless communications. Considering a two-user wireless multiple access channel (MAC) with correlated Rayleigh fading modeled by the bivariate Farlie--Gumbel--Morgenstern (FGM) copula, explicit parameter regions are derived from communication-theoretic and probabilistic perspectives using outage probability and Pearson correlation coefficient (PCC) constraints. The results show that practical communication and statistical requirements can significantly shrink the classical copula admissible interval, rendering some theoretically admissible dependence structures infeasible. Numerical examples illustrate the proposed concept and its practical implications.

中文摘要翻译: Copula函数已广泛用于无线通信分析中，以建模依赖结构并评估系统性能。然而，现有研究通常直接用copula依赖参数表达性能指标，而未显式刻画这些参数的可行区域。本文引入copula依赖参数区域的概念，并探讨其在无线通信中的重要性。考虑一个以双变量Farlie-Gumbel-Morgenstern (FGM) copula建模的相关瑞利衰落两用户无线多址接入信道（MAC），从中断概率和皮尔逊相关系数（PCC）约束出发，从通信理论和概率视角分别推导出显式的参数区域。结果表明，实际通信和统计需求会显著缩小经典的copula允许区间，使得部分理论上可接受的依赖结构在实际中不可行。数值示例展示了所提概念及其实际意义。

</details>


### [195] [Variable-Length Finite-Rate CSI Feedback With Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06846)
*Yangxuan Cheng,Fanyang Meng,Jian Zou,Jiacheng Xie,Zhongqiang Zhang,Ye Wang,Yongsheng Liang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文从结构视角研究变长有限速率CSI反馈，提出CsiCoGen生成式反馈结构，支持可迁移码本机制且无需联合训练。UE将信道矩阵映射为有序码本索引序列，BS利用共享去噪先验从任意部分序列递归恢复CSI，实现了灵活的反馈长度控制和可调量化精度。基于扩散模型的解码器在COST2100数据集上，高码率下室内NMSE达-31 dB、室外达-20 dB，优于多种基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有CSI反馈方法通常需要联合训练编码器/码本与重构器，缺乏灵活性，无法动态调整反馈长度和量化精度，且难以跨任务迁移。本文旨在设计一种无需联合训练、支持可变长度反馈和可迁移码本的生成式反馈结构。

Method: 提出CsiCoGen生成式反馈结构：UE端将信道矩阵H0编码为有序码本索引序列；BS端利用共享去噪先验（实例化为扩散模型），从任意接收到的部分索引序列递归恢复CSI。该结构无需联合训练任务特定的反馈编码器或码本，同一在线结构可搭配不同预训练去噪器。

Result: 在COST2100信道数据集上，CsiCoGen在高码率区间达到室内NMSE约-31 dB、室外NMSE约-20 dB，在率-NMSE和率-ρ权衡上优于代表性基线方法，同时具备可扩展的解码复杂度和可调的每步量化精度。

Conclusion: CsiCoGen验证了生成式范式在变长有限速率CSI反馈中的有效性，其无需联合训练的结构设计和可迁移码本机制为实现灵活、高效的CSI反馈提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: This letter studies variable-length finite-rate CSI feedback from a structural perspective and proposes CsiCoGen, a novel generative feedback structure with a transferable codebook mechanism without joint training. The UE maps $H_0$ into an ordered sequence of codebook indices, while the BS recursively recovers CSI from any received partial sequence of feedback indices using a shared denoising prior. This enables flexible control of feedback sequence length and per-step quantization precision through codebook size. CsiCoGen does not require jointly training a task-specific feedback encoder or codebook with the reconstructor, and the same online structure can be paired with different pretrained denoisers. In this work, we instantiate the decoder with a generative diffusion model. Simulation results on COST2100 show favorable rate-NMSE and rate-$ρ$ tradeoffs against representative baselines, with CsiCoGen reaching about -31 dB indoor NMSE and -20 dB outdoor NMSE in the high-rate regime while demonstrating scalable decoding complexity and adjustable per-step quantization precision.

中文摘要翻译: 本文从结构视角研究变长有限速率信道状态信息（CSI）反馈，并提出CsiCoGen——一种新颖的生成式反馈结构，其具备无需联合训练的可迁移码本机制。用户设备将信道矩阵H0映射为有序的码本索引序列，而基站则利用共享的去噪先验，从任意接收到的部分反馈索引序列中递归地恢复CSI。该方法能够通过码本大小灵活控制反馈序列长度和每步量化精度。CsiCoGen无需联合训练任务特定的反馈编码器或码本与重构器，同一在线结构可与不同的预训练去噪器搭配使用。本文将解码器实例化为生成式扩散模型。在COST2100信道数据集上的仿真结果表明，CsiCoGen在率-归一化均方误差（NMSE）和率-ρ权衡上均优于代表性基线方法，在高码率区间室内NMSE达到约-31 dB、室外NMSE达到约-20 dB，同时具备可扩展的解码复杂度和可调的每步量化精度。

</details>


### [196] [Learn to Access and Backhaul the Sky: Multi-Scale Radio Map Guided Multi-UAV Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06954)
*Yifeng Yuan,Shijian Gao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出多尺度无线地图引导（MRMG）框架，通过三级无线电信息解耦宏观移动与微观链路自适应，结合多智能体强化学习实现无人机集群的联合移动、路由与功率控制，显著提升网络吞吐量和边缘用户速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低空经济驱动的无人机集群需要灵活的空地接入与回传，但用户移动性与建筑物遮挡在三维场景中形成快速变化的端到端瓶颈，而传统启发式方法难以应对多维度联合控制问题。

Method: 提出MRMG框架，集成三级无线地图信息：全局地图（区域覆盖）、局部地图（邻域服务状态）、链路地图（高分辨率信道特征），以解耦宏观移动与微观链路自适应；并使用多智能体强化学习（MARL）学习无人机移动、下一跳选择和发射功率控制的协作策略。

Result: MRMG框架不仅提升了网络吞吐量，还显著增强了小区边缘服务，将第5百分位用户速率提升了近一倍。

Conclusion: 多尺度无线电地图引导结合多智能体强化学习，能有效应对无人机集群在三维异构环境中的联合控制挑战，实现长期性能优化。

Original Abstract: Driven by the emerging low-altitude economy, uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) swarms offer flexible integrated air-ground access and backhaul. However, providing seamless connectivity is difficult due to the interdependent dynamics of user mobility and building blockages in these 3D scenarios. These factors create rapidly shifting bottlenecks in end-to-end paths. Furthermore, the multi-dimensional nature of joint control limits the effectiveness of traditional heuristics. To address these challenges, a \textbf{\underline{M}}ulti-Scale \textbf{\underline{R}}adio \textbf{\underline{M}}ap-\textbf{\underline{G}}uided (MRMG) framework is proposed. The MRMG framework handles heterogeneous dynamics by integrating three distinct levels of radio information: global-level maps provide regional coverage insights, local-level maps capture neighborhood-scale service conditions, and link-level maps characterize high-resolution channel features. This design effectively decouples macro-movement from micro-link adaptation. To yield long-term performance improvements, A multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) controller learns cooperative policies for UAV movement, next-hop selection, and transmit-power control. Simulation results show that the MRMG framework not only improves network throughput but also significantly bolsters cell-edge service, nearly doubling the 5th-percentile user rate.

中文摘要翻译: 受新兴低空经济的驱动，无人机集群提供了灵活的空地一体化接入与回传。然而，由于用户移动性与建筑物遮挡在三维场景中的相互依赖动态，提供无缝连接十分困难。这些因素导致端到端路径上出现快速变化的瓶颈。此外，联合控制的多维特性限制了传统启发式方法的有效性。为应对这些挑战，本文提出了一种多尺度无线电地图引导（MRMG）框架。MRMG框架通过整合三个不同级别的无线电信息来处理异构动态：全局地图提供区域覆盖洞察，局部地图捕获邻域尺度服务状况，链路地图表征高分辨率信道特征。该设计有效解耦了宏观移动与微观链路自适应。为实现长期性能提升，一个多智能体强化学习控制器学习无人机移动、下一跳选择和发射功率控制的协作策略。仿真结果表明，MRMG框架不仅提升了网络吞吐量，还显著增强了小区边缘服务，将第5百分位用户速率提升了近一倍。

</details>


### [197] [A Novel Stripe-based RIS Optimization for UAV Communications and Sensing in Low-Altitude Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07026)
*Burak Ahmet Celebi,Sefa Kayraklik,Onur Salan,Ibrahim Hokelek,Ali Emre Pusane,Ali Gorcin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可重构智能表面（RIS）的条带化相位优化框架，用于低空无线网络，在降低计算复杂度的同时兼顾通信可靠性与无人机被动感知跟踪能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高复杂度优化方法难以在动态3D环境下同时满足低空无线网络的可靠通信和无人机跟踪需求，亟需一种低复杂度、高效的RIS配置方案。

Method: 提出一种条带化（stripe-based）RIS相位偏移优化框架，利用RIS相邻单元固有的结构相位梯度特性，大幅缩小RIS配置的搜索空间，实现随无人机移动快速计算和更新RIS配置。

Result: 在收敛速度和计算效率上优于传统基准方法；在存在相位估计误差和低信噪比场景下仍能保持稳健的高信噪比连接；通过真实RIS原型在室外校园环境中的测量实验验证了方法实用性。

Conclusion: 所提条带化RIS优化框架能在低复杂度下实现可靠的通信与无人机被动感知，具有实际部署可行性。

Original Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks (LAWN) envision a reconfigurable 3D network capable of supporting mission-critical aerial operations. This paper presents a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-assisted LAWN to establish a reliable communication with an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) across varying wireless channel conditions and signal blockages. A low complexity stripe-based RIS phase shift optimization framework is proposed to simultaneously enhance communication reliability and provide passive sensing capability for UAV tracking under 3D mobility. Unlike high-complexity optimization approaches, the proposed method leverages the inherent structural phase-gradient of the RIS adjacent elements to significantly reduce the search space for calculating and updating the RIS configuration as the UAV moves. The analysis and simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms conventional benchmarks in convergence speed and computational efficiency, while maintaining robust, high signal-to-noise-ratio (SNR) connectivity even in the presence of phase estimation errors and low SNR regimes. In addition, the measurement experiments using a real RIS prototype in an outdoor campus environment are performed to demonstrate the practical viability of the proposed approach.

中文摘要翻译: 低空无线网络（LAWN）设想了一种可重构的三维网络，能够支持关键任务的空中作业。本文提出了一种可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助的LAWN，用于在不同无线信道条件和信号阻塞下与无人机建立可靠通信。提出了一种低复杂度的条带化RIS相位偏移优化框架，能够在三维机动条件下同时增强通信可靠性并提供无人机跟踪的被动感知能力。与高复杂度优化方法不同，所提方法利用RIS相邻单元固有的结构相位梯度特性，显著缩小了随无人机移动计算和更新RIS配置时的搜索空间。分析与仿真结果表明，所提框架在收敛速度和计算效率上优于传统基准方法，并且在存在相位估计误差和低信噪比场景下仍能保持稳健的高信噪比连接。此外，通过在真实室外校园环境中使用真实RIS原型进行的测量实验，验证了所提方法的实际可行性。

</details>


### [198] [Geometric Time-Domain Identification of Three-Phase Load Equivalents from Terminal Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07048)
*Francisco G. Montoya,Francisco de Leon,Francisco M. Arrabal-Campos,Alfredo Alcayde*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于几何时域方法的三相负载等效参数识别方法，通过分析公共耦合点的电压电流波形轨迹在欧几里得信号空间中的几何特性来恢复负载参数，并实现了采样数据实现、可识别性测试与能量守恒约束。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将已有的单相几何识别方法扩展到三相和四线系统，特别是三线系统（无中性点电压测量，需满足基尔霍夫约束），并解决实际采样数据实现中的可识别性与模型验证问题。

Method: 基于几何时域方法，在欧几里得信号空间中解释测量波形为轨迹，从轨迹几何恢复负载等效参数；采用局部时间窗口、归一化矩阵方程、谐波投影导数与原始坐标、显式几何可识别性测试、无源性约束及能量/基尔霍夫残差来实现采样数据版本。

Result: 数值仿真（含干净数据、测量噪声、窗口长度扫描和传感器延迟）表明该方法能准确识别信息丰富的三相轨迹，并暴露结构退化情况（如纯单频激励下高阶三线模型的不可识别性）。对于给定拓扑，识别出的电路能在分析窗口内闭合实测负载的瞬时端能量平衡。

Conclusion: 该方法不会在测量轨迹信息不足时强行拟合模型，而是报告低秩或病态窗口为低置信度证据；能有效识别三相负载参数并检测不可识别情形。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a geometric time-domain method for identifying three-phase load equivalents from instantaneous voltage and current measurements at the point of common coupling. Measured waveforms are interpreted as trajectories in Euclidean signal spaces, and load-equivalent parameters are recovered from the geometry of those trajectories. The method extends a previously published single-phase geometric identification formulation to three- and four-wire systems and places special emphasis on the three-wire case, where no neutral voltage is measured and the terminal data must satisfy coupled Kirchhoff constraints. The main advance over the earlier analytical formulation is a sampled-data implementation based on local time windows, normalized matrix equations, harmonic-projection derivative and primitive coordinates, explicit geometric identifiability tests, passivity constraints, and energy/Kirchhoff residuals. The method does not force a model when the measured trajectory lacks enough information; instead, it reports low-rank or ill-conditioned windows as low-confidence evidence. Numerical simulations with clean data, measurement noise, window-length sweeps, and sensor delay show that the method accurately identifies informative three-phase trajectories and exposes structurally degenerate cases such as pure single-frequency excitation for higher-order three-wire models. For a given admissible topology the identified circuit closes the instantaneous terminal energy balance of the measured load over the analysis window.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出一种几何时域方法，用于从公共耦合点的瞬时电压和电流测量值中识别三相负载等效参数。将测量波形解释为欧几里得信号空间中的轨迹，并从这些轨迹的几何特性中恢复负载等效参数。该方法将先前发表的单相几何识别公式扩展到三线和四线系统，并特别强调三线情况——此时无中性点电压测量，且终端数据必须满足耦合的基尔霍夫约束。相较于先前的分析公式，主要进展在于实现了基于局部时间窗口的采样数据版本，包括归一化矩阵方程、谐波投影导数与原始坐标、显式几何可识别性测试、无源性约束以及能量/基尔霍夫残差。当测量轨迹缺乏足够信息时，该方法不会强行拟合模型，而是将低秩或病态窗口报告为低置信度证据。采用干净数据、测量噪声、窗口长度扫描和传感器延迟的数值仿真表明，该方法能准确识别信息丰富的三相轨迹，并暴露结构退化情况（如高阶三线模型在纯单频激励下的退化）。对于给定的可容许拓扑，识别出的电路能在分析窗口内闭合实测负载的瞬时端能量平衡。

</details>


### [199] [Optimized Sampling of Angle-Resolved Scatterometry Data Using End-to-End Compressed Learning Model for Nanograss Deficiency Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07050)
*Mehdi Abdollahpour,Carsten Bockelmann,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端压缩学习框架，将可学习的基于纬度的采样层与卷积神经网络相结合，用于氧化锌纳米草的5级空位缺陷检测。该方法利用角分辨散射测量图像，在保持高分类性能的同时大幅减少采样点数（最多减少99.7%），并采用GAN增强数据加速训练收敛。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的角分辨散射测量术在纳米表面缺陷检测中虽为非侵入式在线检测方法，但由于需要密集角度采样导致采集时间过长，限制了实际应用效率。因此，本文旨在减少采样点数的同时保持高分类精度，解决数据采集效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出端到端压缩学习框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 可学习的纬度基采样层，利用ARS图像的物理结构特性，学习信息丰富的纬度区域，减少采样搜索空间；(2) 卷积神经网络用于缺陷分类。采样层与网络在训练过程中联合优化。此外，采用基于GAN的数据增强方法生成合成数据用于模型预训练，以应对有限训练数据问题。

Result: 全ARS图像下五级缺陷分类准确率达94.2%，合格/不合格二分类准确率达98.6%。所提采样模型在使用比全采样少90%的角度采样点时性能持平；即使采样点减少99.7%，分类准确率下降不超过10个百分点。GAN增强数据仅需少量微调轮次即可实现快速收敛。

Conclusion: 本文提出的压缩学习框架有效解决了ARS数据采集效率问题，通过可学习采样与CNN联合优化，在极大减少采样点数的前提下仍能保持高精度缺陷检测性能，为纳米表面在线检测提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Original Abstract: Reliable inspection of nanosurfaces is essential to ensure the quality of nanostructure manufacturing. Angle-resolved scatterometry provides a non-invasive inspection method that can be used in-line but often suffers from long acquisition times due to dense angular sampling. This paper addresses the data acquisition challenge by proposing an end-to-end compressed learning framework for 5-level vacancy deficiency detection in zinc oxide nanograss using ARS images. The proposed framework integrates a learnable latitude-based sampling layer with a convolutional neural network, allowing sampling and classification to be jointly optimized during training. The sampling layer exploits the physical structure of ARS patterns and learns informative latitudinal regions, which reduces the sampling search space and improves convergence. Evaluation results show that the proposed approach achieves high and stable deficiency-level classification performance under different noise conditions. Using full ARS images, the model achieves 94.2% accuracy for five-level deficiency classification and 98.6% accuracy for separating deficient from non-deficient nanosurfaces. The proposed sampling model matches full-image performance while using up to 90% fewer angular sampling points. Even when sampling points are reduced by 99.7%, the classification accuracy decreases by less than 10 percentage points. To further improve training with limited data, we also studied a GAN-based augmentation approach and used GAN-generated data for model pretraining. Augmented data resulted in fast convergence within only a few fine-tuning epochs.

中文摘要翻译: 可靠地检测纳米表面对保证纳米结构制造质量至关重要。角分辨散射测量术（ARS）提供了一种可用于在线检测的非侵入式检测方法，但由于密集角度采样导致采集时间较长。本文针对数据采集挑战，提出了一种端到端压缩学习框架，用于基于ARS图像的氧化锌纳米草5级空位缺陷检测。所提框架将可学习的纬度基采样层与卷积神经网络相结合，使得采样和分类在训练过程中能够联合优化。采样层利用ARS图像的物理结构特性，学习信息丰富的纬度区域，从而减少采样搜索空间并改善收敛性。评估结果表明，所提方法在不同噪声条件下均实现了高且稳定的缺陷等级分类性能。使用全ARS图像时，模型在五级缺陷分类上达到94.2%的准确率，在区分有缺陷与无缺陷纳米表面时达到98.6%的准确率。所提采样模型在使用比全采样少90%的角度采样点时可匹配全图像性能。即使采样点减少99.7%，分类准确率下降也不超过10个百分点。为进一步改善有限数据下的训练，我们还研究了基于GAN的数据增强方法，并使用GAN生成的数据进行模型预训练。增强后的数据仅需少量微调轮次即可实现快速收敛。

</details>


### [200] [Branch-Level Energy Localization in Three-Phase Loads: Resolving Indeterminacy in Time-Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07076)
*Francisco G. Montoya,Francisco de Leon,Francisco M. Arrabal-Campos,Alfredo Alcayde*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了三相负载的支路级能量局域化框架，将可集总等效的瞬时终端功率唯一分解为焦耳耗散、磁储能变化率和电储能变化率，并建立了三个形式化定理，通过六个案例验证了该框架的有效性，作为IEEE Std. 1459等标准方法的补充。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有三相电能理论（如IEEE Std. 1459、瞬时p-q理论、FBD理论等）对能量的分解方式各不相同，缺乏统一的支路级能量局域化视角，导致一些看似矛盾的悖论（如de Leon–Cohen开路相悖论）。本文旨在建立一个严格的支路级能量局域化框架，从基本原理上澄清这些矛盾。

Method: 基于可集总等效电路的支路拓扑，将瞬时终端功率唯一分解为每条支路上的焦耳耗散、磁储能变化率和电储能变化率；提出分支级局域化定理、拓扑不确定性定理和广义能量对偶定理，其中广义能量对偶定理将经典电路对偶（诺顿-戴维南、串并联、L与C、R与G）统一为时域原理的特例，用时变参数替代常数参数等效。

Result: 在六个测试案例上验证了框架：de Leon–Cohen开路相悖论、开关电阻负载、三相三线Δ-Y虚拟不确定性、波动相负载、以及含滞回、线性和开关支路的四线非线性负载。结果表明该框架能统一解释经典悖论，并与现有标准方法互补。

Conclusion: 该支路级能量局域化框架为解决三相负荷能量流问题提供了严格的数学基础，与IEEE Std. 1459、CPC、瞬时p-q理论和FBD理论互为补充——每种方法回答不同的问题，一旦问题被精确表述，表面上的悖论便消失了。

Original Abstract: This paper develops a branch-level energy-localization framework for three-phase loads. The instantaneous terminal power of an admissible lumped equivalent is decomposed uniquely as Joule dissipation plus magnetic and electric stored-energy rates, branch by branch. Three formal results are established: a Branch-Level Localization Theorem (uniqueness given an admissible topology); a Topology-Indeterminacy Theorem (multiple admissible topologies reproduce identical terminal data with distinct localizations); and a Generalized Energetic Duality Theorem that organizes classical electrical dualities (Norton-Thevenin, series--parallel, L vs C, R vs G) as restrictions to Linear Time Invariant (LTI) sinusoidal regimes of a single time-domain principle in which constant-parameter equivalence is replaced by time-varying parameters. The framework is exercised on six test cases including the de Leon--Cohen open-phase paradox, switched-resistive loads, three-wire delta-versus-wye-virtual indeterminacy, fluctuating-phase loads, and a four-wire nonlinear load with hysteretic, linear, and switched branches. The framework is positioned as complementary to IEEE Std. 1459, CPC, instantaneous p-q, and Fryze-Buchholz-Depenbrock: each answers a different question, and the apparent paradoxes vanish once the question is posed precisely.

中文摘要翻译: 本文开发了一种面向三相负载的支路级能量局域化框架。可集总等效电路的瞬时终端功率被唯一分解为每条支路上的焦耳耗散、磁储能变化率和电储能变化率。建立了三个形式化结果：分支级局域化定理（给定可集总拓扑下的唯一性）；拓扑不确定性定理（多个可集总拓扑能复现相同的终端数据但局域化结果不同）；以及广义能量对偶定理，该定理将经典电路对偶（诺顿-戴维南、串并联、L与C、R与G）组织为线性时不变（LTI）正弦稳态下的约束条件，这些约束源于一个统一的时域原理，其中常数参数等效被时变参数所取代。该框架在六个测试案例上进行了验证，包括de Leon–Cohen开路相悖论、开关电阻负载、三相三线Δ-Y虚拟不确定性、波动相负载，以及含滞回、线性和开关支路的四线非线性负载。该框架被定位为IEEE Std. 1459、CPC、瞬时p-q理论和Fryze-Buchholz-Depenbrock（FBD）理论的补充：每种方法回答不同的问题，一旦问题被精确表述，表面上的悖论便随之消失。

</details>


### [201] [Rate-Splitting--Inspired Uplink Near-Field ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07091)
*Anup Mishra,Israel Leyva-Mayorga,Petar Popovski*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于速率分割（RS）的上行近场通感一体化（ISAC）框架，通过将通信消息分割到感知操作中，解决了NOMA启发ISAC框架中感知中心与通信中心端点排序的局限，并分析了残余感知干扰对通信-感知速率区域的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有NOMA启发的ISAC框架存在感知中心与通信中心端点排序的局限性，且上行ISAC中感知与通信之间的功能间干扰给接收机设计带来根本性挑战，需要更优的干扰管理框架来扩展通感性能折衷边界。

Method: 提出速率分割（RS）启发的上行近场ISAC框架，将通信消息分割到感知操作中，推导了考虑目标响应估计不确定性的残余感知干扰下的通信速率（CR）与感知速率（SR）闭式表达式，并采用孔径感知近场信道模型进行大阵列极限分析。

Result: 在感知匹配照明下，所提单帧RS启发边界包含NOMA启发的时分共享区域；RS启发边界能够匹配或严格扩大通感折衷；高SNR分析表明非对齐信道中残余感知干扰改变速率偏移但不改变主导斜率，对齐信道中则限制斜率；大阵列极限下可达速率保持有限。

Conclusion: RS启发框架优于NOMA启发方案，残余感知干扰对通感性能有显著影响，物理一致近场建模导致大阵列行为有界，为近场ISAC系统设计提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) enables sensing and communication (S&C) functionalities to share spectrum, hardware, and signal-processing resources, but the resulting inter-functionality interference creates a fundamental receiver-design challenge, particularly in uplink operation. This paper develops a rate-splitting (RS)-inspired framework for uplink near-field ISAC. The framework generalizes the sensing-centric (S-C) and communication-centric (C-C) endpoint orders of non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA)-inspired ISAC by splitting the communication message across the sensing operation. Closed-form expressions are derived for the communication-rate (CR) and sensing-rate (SR), accounting for residual sensing interference from target-response estimation uncertainty. The achievable CR-SR rate region is characterized under sensing-matched illumination, where the proposed single-frame RS-inspired boundary contains the NOMA-inspired time-sharing region. Unlike the classical Gaussian uplink multiple access channel, where RS recovers the time-sharing dominant face, the split factor in uplink ISAC also reshapes the sensing-stage interference, allowing the RS-inspired boundary to match or strictly enlarge the S&C tradeoff. High-SNR analysis shows that, for non-aligned S&C channels, residual sensing interference changes the rate offsets but not the leading S&C slopes, whereas in the fully-aligned case it becomes slope-limiting. Using an aperture-aware near-field channel model, large-array limits are derived, showing that achievable rates remain finite as the array grows. Numerical results validate the analysis and demonstrate the benefits of the RS-inspired scheme, the impact of residual sensing interference, and the bounded large-array behaviour induced by physically consistent near-field modelling.

中文摘要翻译: 通感一体化（ISAC）使感知与通信（S&C）功能能够共享频谱、硬件和信号处理资源，但由此产生的功能间干扰带来了根本性的接收机设计挑战，尤其是在上行链路操作中。本文开发了一种速率分割（RS）启发的上行近场ISAC框架。该框架通过将通信消息分割到感知操作中，推广了非正交多址接入（NOMA）启发ISAC的感知中心（S-C）和通信中心（C-C）端点排序。推导了考虑目标响应估计不确定性的残余感知干扰下的通信速率（CR）和感知速率（SR）闭式表达式。在感知匹配照明下刻画了可达CR-SR速率区域，所提单帧RS启发边界包含NOMA启发的时分共享区域。与经典高斯上行多址接入信道中RS恢复时分共享主导面不同，上行ISAC中的分割因子还重塑了感知阶段干扰，使得RS启发边界能够匹配或严格扩大通感折衷。高信噪比分析表明，对于非对齐的S&C信道，残余感知干扰改变速率偏移但不改变主导S&C斜率，而在完全对齐情况下，它成为斜率限制因素。使用孔径感知近场信道模型，推导了大阵列极限，表明随着阵列增长可达速率保持有限。数值结果验证了分析结论，展示了RS启发方案的优势、残余感知干扰的影响以及物理一致近场建模导致的有界大阵列行为。

</details>


### [202] [Robust Secure Beamforming for Movable Antenna Enhanced Integrated Sensing and Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07104)
*Yuan Chen,Ning Wei,Ahmad Bazzi,Xiangyu Dong,Ran Yang,You Li,Yue Xiu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了可移动天线(MA)增强的安全通感一体化(ISAC)系统中的鲁棒波束赋形设计，针对不完美窃听信道状态信息(CSI)场景，提出了一种基于块坐标下降(BCD)的算法，联合优化发射波束赋形与天线位置，在保障通信安全的同时显著提升雷达信干噪比(SINR)。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有ISAC系统在安全通信与雷达感知性能的联合优化方面存在不足，尤其是可移动天线引入的非线性信道映射以及窃听信道不确定性带来的优化难题尚未得到有效解决。

Method: 提出了一种基于块坐标下降(BCD)的算法，融合了逐次凸近似(SCA)和分数规划(FP)技术，通过联合优化发射波束赋形和天线位置来最大化雷达SINR，同时确保通信数据安全。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法具有快速收敛特性，在保证通信安全的前提下显著提升了雷达SINR。

Conclusion: 本文提出的BCD-SCA-FP联合优化方法能够有效应对可移动天线系统中的非线性信道映射和信道不确定性挑战，实现了安全通信与雷达感知性能的双重提升。

Original Abstract: In this letter, we investigate robust beamforming design for a movable antenna (MA)-enhanced secure integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) system with imperfect eaves?dropping channel state information (CSI). To improve radar sensing performance, we formulate a radar signal-to-interference?plus-noise ratio (SINR) maximization problem by jointly opti?mizing the transmit beamforming and antenna placement while ensuring communication data security. However, the resulting op?timization problem is inherently intractable due to the nonlinea mapping from antenna positions to channel coefficients, as well as the eavesdropper (Eve) channel uncertainty. To handle these challenges, we propose a block coordinate descent (BCD)-based algorithm incorporating successive convex approximation (SCA) and fractional programming (FP) techniques. Simulation results show that our proposed algorithm exhibits fast convergence and achieves a significant improvement in the radar SINR while guaranteeing communication security.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了可移动天线(MA)增强的安全通感一体化(ISAC)系统在不完美窃听信道状态信息(CSI)条件下的鲁棒波束赋形设计问题。为了提升雷达感知性能，我们通过联合优化发射波束赋形与天线放置，构建了一个雷达信干噪比(SINR)最大化问题，同时确保通信数据的安全性。然而，由于天线位置到信道系数存在非线性映射，且窃听者(Eve)信道具有不确定性，导致该优化问题本质上难以求解。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种基于块坐标下降(BCD)的算法，融合了逐次凸近似(SCA)和分数规划(FP)技术。仿真结果表明，所提算法具有快速收敛特性，在保证通信安全的前提下显著提升了雷达SINR。

</details>


### [203] [RSMA Enabled Hierarchical UAV Networks with Non Linear Energy Harvesting: Outage Probability Analysis and UAV Placement Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07284)
*Faicel Khennoufa,Khelil Abdellatif,Metin Ozturk,Halim Yanikomeroglu,Safwan Alfattani*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向6G网络的分层自组织无人机网络，采用非线性无线能量收集（EH）和速率分割多址接入（RSMA），在硬件损伤（HWI）和非完美信道状态信息（ICSI）条件下提升可靠性与能效。推导了Nakagami-m衰落信道下的中断概率表达式，并通过优化问题验证了所提系统优于现有基准方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多无人机系统在6G网络中面临能量受限、续航短、通信连续性差等挑战。现有方案中，地面能量源存在路径损耗，而无人机间能量收集更具可持续性。此外，RSMA在分层无人机网络中对HWI和ICSI的影响尚未充分研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出分层自组织无人机网络架构，结合非线性EH（从周围无人机收集能量）和RSMA。在Nakagami-m衰落信道下，考虑HWI、ICSI和非线性EH等实际约束，推导地面IoT设备、各簇头及系统的中断概率闭式表达式及高发射功率下的近似表达式，并构建两个优化问题以提升可靠性与性能。

Result: 所提系统在中断概率上优于所有基准方案；推导的中断概率表达式准确刻画了HWI、ICSI和非线性EH对系统性能的影响；高发射功率下的近似表达式具备良好精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的融合非线性EH与RSMA的分层自组织无人机网络能有效提升通信可靠性与能效，在考虑HWI和ICSI的实际场景中具有显著优势，为6G无人机通信提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are expected to enhance connectivity, extend network coverage, and support advanced communication services in sixth-generation (6G) cellular networks, particularly in public and civil applications. Although multi-UAV systems offer greater efficiency and cost-effectiveness than single-UAV deployments, their implementation still faces several fundamental challenges that limit their reliability, sustainability, and scalability. The limited onboard energy restricts mission duration and communication continuity. Therefore, wireless energy harvesting (EH) emerges as a promising solution to overcome this limitation. However, terrestrial energy sources experience path loss, making EH from surrounding UAVs more sustainable. Moreover, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) remains insufficiently explored in hierarchical UAV networks under hardware impairments (HWI) and imperfect channel state information (ICSI). This paper proposes a hierarchical ad hoc UAV network with non-linear EH and RSMA to enhance both energy and cost efficiency, where UAVs harvest energy from surrounding UAVs. For a practical scenario, we consider the effect of HWI and ICSI in our proposed system. To the best of the authors knowledge, this study is the first to investigate such a scenario in the literature. The outage probability expressions for ground Internet of things (IoT) devices, each CMU, and the overall outage probability of the proposed system are derived over Nakagami-$m$ fading channels while considering practical constraints such as HWI, ICSI, and non-linear EH. Additionally, approximate outage probability expressions are derived for high transmit power regimes. Subsequently, we formulate two optimization problems to enhance reliability and performance. Our findings indicate that the proposed system outperforms all benchmarks in terms of outage probability.

中文摘要翻译: 无人驾驶飞行器（UAV）有望在第六代（6G）蜂窝网络中增强连接性、扩展网络覆盖范围并支持先进的通信服务，特别是在公共和民用应用中。尽管多无人机系统比单无人机部署具有更高的效率和成本效益，但其实现仍面临若干根本性挑战，限制了其可靠性、可持续性和可扩展性。机载能量有限制约了任务持续时间和通信连续性。因此，无线能量收集（EH）成为克服这一限制的有前景的解决方案。然而，地面能量源存在路径损耗，使得从周围无人机收集能量更具可持续性。此外，速率分割多址接入（RSMA）在存在硬件损伤（HWI）和非完美信道状态信息（ICSI）的分层无人机网络中尚未得到充分探索。本文提出一种融合非线性EH和RSMA的分层自组织无人机网络，以提升能量效率和成本效率，其中无人机从周围无人机收集能量。针对实际场景，我们在所提系统中考虑了HWI和ICSI的影响。据作者所知，本研究是文献中首次对该场景进行探索。在Nakagami-m衰落信道上，考虑HWI、ICSI和非线性EH等实际约束，推导了地面物联网（IoT）设备、各簇头的中断概率表达式以及所提系统的整体中断概率。此外，还推导了高发射功率 regime 下的近似中断概率表达式。随后，我们构建了两个优化问题以提升可靠性和性能。研究结果表明，所提系统在中断概率方面优于所有基准方案。

</details>


### [204] [Implementation and Calibration of 3GPP-Compliant ISAC Channel Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07328)
*Chien-Han Wu,Ming-Chun Lee,Ta-Sung Lee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文实现了3GPP TR 38.901中指定的ISAC信道模型模拟器，并进行了全面的校准分析，将仿真结果与3GPP报告中的参考结果进行对比，讨论了关键实现细节，并开源了相关代码和数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 由于ISAC信道建模方法复杂且3GPP报告中缺乏完全明确的实现细节，不同实现可能导致不一致或不同步的仿真结果，因此需要提供一个准确、可复现的标准化实现和校准分析。

Method: 基于3GPP TR 38.901规范实现ISAC信道模型模拟器，按照标准化的校准流程进行仿真，并将仿真结果与3GPP中各公司报告的参考结果进行对比分析。

Result: 实现了与3GPP参考结果一致的ISAC信道模拟器，揭示了若干关键的实现细节，并提供了开源代码、数据集和校准结果。

Conclusion: 本文工作为ISAC系统的性能评估提供了标准化的信道模拟工具，通过开源发布促进了研究的可复现性和后续发展。

Original Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has emerged as a key technology for 6G systems. To support the development of ISAC systems, accurate channel modeling and simulation for performance evaluation is essential. Recently, 3GPP introduced a standardized ISAC channel model and its associated calibration procedure for this purpose. However, due to the complexity of the modeling methodology and the lack of fully explicit implementation details in the 3GPP reports, different implementations may lead to inconsistent or unsynchronized simulation results. To address this issue, in this work, we implement the 3GPP ISAC channel model simulator specified in TR 38.901 and conduct a comprehensive calibration analysis. We compare the simulation results with the reference results reported by companies in 3GPP and discuss several key implementation details to provide insights into the implementation and calibration of the simulator. To facilitate reproducibility and further research, the developed simulator, together with the relevant datasets and calibration results, has been released as an open-source project on GitHub.

中文摘要翻译: 集成感知与通信（ISAC）已成为6G系统的关键技术。为支持ISAC系统的开发，用于性能评估的精确信道建模与仿真至关重要。近期，3GPP为此引入了标准化的ISAC信道模型及其相关的校准流程。然而，由于建模方法的复杂性以及3GPP报告中缺乏完全明确的实现细节，不同的实现可能导致不一致或不同步的仿真结果。为解决此问题，本文实现了3GPP TR 38.901中指定的ISAC信道模型模拟器，并进行了全面的校准分析。我们将仿真结果与3GPP中各公司报告的参考结果进行了对比，并讨论了若干关键的实现细节，为模拟器的实现和校准提供了见解。为促进可复现性和后续研究，开发的模拟器及相关数据集和校准结果已作为开源项目发布在GitHub上。

</details>


### [205] [CSI Phase Averaging for High-Sensitivity Wi-Fi Sensing in Low-Multipath Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07347)
*Toshinori Suzuki,Shin-ichiro Ogura,Yu Morishima,Hiroshi Matsuura*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型驱动的低复杂度运动检测方法，用于户外Wi-Fi感知。该方法利用低多径传播环境下CSI相位分量的结构特性来消除设备相位偏移误差，并通过相位平均抑制随机噪声。实验在IEEE 802.11ac设备上针对户外果园中飞翔的乌鸦进行验证，结果表明该方法能检测到距视距路径数米外的鸟类，且风速低于3 m/s时植被运动干扰可忽略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 低多径传播环境通常被认为不利于Wi-Fi感知，因为缺乏足够的散射信号来捕捉运动信息。然而，户外场景（如果园监控）恰恰属于低多径环境，现有方法在该场景下性能受限。因此，本文旨在开发一种适用于低多径环境的Wi-Fi运动检测方法。

Method: 基于模型驱动的方法：利用CSI相位分量的结构特性消除无线设备引入的相位偏移误差；通过相位平均处理获得处理增益，降低量化噪声和热噪声等随机噪声成分。

Result: 在户外果园环境中，使用商用IEEE 802.11ac设备的压缩波束成形帧进行实验，成功检测到距离收发天线视距路径数米外飞行的野生乌鸦；当风速低于3 m/s时，植被运动引起的波动可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅适用于果园监控，也有望推广至其他低多径环境下的户外Wi-Fi感知应用。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a low-complexity motion detection method for outdoor Wi-Fi sensing based on a model-driven approach. The method exploits the structural characteristics of the phase components in channel state information (CSI) for low-multipath propagation environments, which are generally considered disadvantageous for Wi-Fi sensing, to mitigate the phase offset errors originating from wireless devices. In addition, phase averaging provides a processing gain that reduces the random noise components, including quantization and thermal noise. The theoretical basis of the method is described and its effectiveness is experimentally evaluated using Compressed Beamforming frames obtained from commercial IEEE 802.11ac devices. The experiments primarily focus wild crows flying in an outdoor orchard environment. The experimental results demonstrate that the method can detect birds even when they fly several meters away from the direct line-of-sight path between the transmitter and receiver antennas. Furthermore, the results indicated that fluctuations caused by vegetation movement were negligible when the wind speed was less than 3~m/s. The proposed approach is expected to be applicable not only to orchard monitoring but also to other outdoor Wi-Fi sensing applications in low-multipath environments.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于模型驱动的低复杂度运动检测方法，用于户外Wi-Fi感知。该方法利用低多径传播环境下信道状态信息（CSI）中相位分量的结构特性——这类环境通常被认为不利于Wi-Fi感知——来消除源自无线设备的相位偏移误差。此外，相位平均提供了处理增益，可降低包括量化噪声和热噪声在内的随机噪声成分。本文描述了该方法的理论基础，并利用从商用IEEE 802.11ac设备获取的压缩波束成形帧进行了实验验证。实验主要聚焦于户外果园环境中飞翔的野生乌鸦。实验结果表明，该方法能够检测到距离收发天线视距路径数米外飞行的鸟类。此外，结果还表明，当风速低于3 m/s时，植被运动引起的波动可忽略不计。所提方法不仅有望应用于果园监控，还可推广至其他低多径环境下的户外Wi-Fi感知应用。

</details>


### [206] [Beyond Backscatter: InSAR coherence from detected SAR images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07374)
*Francescopaolo Sica,Andrea Pulella,Michael Schmitt*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度学习（Residual U-Net）的SAR图像相干性回归框架，可直接从检测后SAR图像中估计相干性，无需精确配准。模型在配准后的Sentinel-1 SLC数据上训练，在不同数据集和地理位置上均表现良好，且能泛化到未见过的时相基线。该方法可在全球可获取的分析就绪数据（如GRD数据）上运行，适用于大规模应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统相干性估计依赖精确配准的SLC数据，计算成本高且难以大规模应用。现有基于强度的相干性估计方法精度有限。本文旨在开发一种无需精确配准、可直接从检测后SAR图像（如GRD数据）中回归高分辨率相干性的深度学习方法，以便在大规模任务中应用。

Method: 使用Residual U-Net深度学习网络，以精确配准的Sentinel-1 SLC数据生成的相干性图为训练目标，学习SAR后向散射幅度与相干性之间的映射关系。模型在12天间隔的SLC影像对上训练，并直接在检测后SAR图像（如GRD数据）上进行推理。

Result: 该方法实现了高分辨率相干性回归，精度优于现有基于强度的方法。模型在不同地理位置、不同辐射特性、几何和位置的多样化数据集上均表现出良好的泛化能力，且能泛化到训练时从未见过的时相基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度学习框架能够从检测后SAR图像中准确回归相干性，无需精确配准，且泛化能力强。由于可直接应用于全球可获取的分析就绪数据（如Google Earth Engine中的GRD数据），该方法在任务设计、变化监测及多种制图任务中具有大规模应用潜力。

Original Abstract: In this work, we propose a deep learning framework for coherence regression directly from detected SAR images, without the need for accurate coregistration. A Residual U-Net is trained using coherence maps derived from precisely coregistered Sentinel-1 SLC data to learn the relationship between backscatter magnitudes and coherence. The model is trained on 12-day SLC pairs and evaluated across different datasets, including coregistered SLC products and open access analysis-ready data, covering diverse radiometric properties, geometries, and locations. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves high-resolution coherence regression with improved accuracy compared to existing intensity-based approaches. The network generalizes well across diverse geographical locations and even across different temporal baselines that were never seen at training time. Additionally, the ability to operate on globally available analysis-ready data, such as ground range detected data, e.g., distributed through Google Earth Engine, enables its large-scale application in mission design, change monitoring, and diverse mapping tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种深度学习框架，可直接从检测后SAR图像中进行相干性回归，无需精确配准。我们使用Residual U-Net，以精确配准后的Sentinel-1 SLC数据生成的相干性图为训练目标，学习后向散射幅度与相干性之间的映射关系。模型在12天间隔的SLC影像对上训练，并在不同数据集上进行评估，包括配准后的SLC产品和开放获取的分析就绪数据，涵盖多样的辐射特性、几何结构和地理位置。实验结果表明，与现有基于强度的方法相比，所提方法实现了更高分辨率的相干性回归，精度更优。该网络在不同地理位置以及训练过程中从未见过的时相基线上均表现出良好的泛化能力。此外，该方法可运行于全球可获取的分析就绪数据（例如通过Google Earth Engine分发的地距检测数据），从而使其能够在任务设计、变化监测及多样化的制图任务中实现大规模应用。

</details>


### [207] [Amortized Neural Optimization for Pre-Layout Signal Integrity Design Space Exploration using Differentiable Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07463)
*Julian Withöft,Werner John,Emre Ecik,Ralf Brüning,Jürgen Götze*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于预布局信号完整性设计空间探索的摊销神经优化（ANO）方法，利用全微分神经网络代理模型离线学习全局优化策略，将迭代黑箱优化转化为单次前向传播，在牺牲约10%最优性的情况下实现三到四个数量级的加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 预布局高速信号完整性分析中的设计空间探索（DSE）受限于仿真计算成本和迭代优化算法。现有机器学习代理模型虽加速了仿真，但优化仍需迭代黑箱搜索，多角点扫描计算开销极大。

Method: 使用全微分神经网络代理模型提取解析梯度，离线训练全局优化策略（摊销优化），推理时将不同信道上下文直接映射到接近最优的设计参数，只需一次确定性前向传播。

Result: 在DDR5 DFE、9维SerDes Tx/Rx协同均衡、DDR3 DQS差分对布线三个场景中，相比实例特定黑箱算法，牺牲约10%最优性，获得三到四个数量级的加速。32万实例多角点SerDes扫描优化从数天缩短至毫秒级单次批前向传播。

Conclusion: ANO将计算昂贵的信号完整性优化转化为实时交互式预布局设计空间探索，大幅提升了效率。

Original Abstract: Pre-layout design space exploration (DSE) for high-speed signal integrity (SI) analysis is often limited by the computational cost of simulations and iterative optimization algorithms within modern electronic design automation (EDA) workflows. While machine learning surrogate models accelerate the simulation step, optimizing designs still requires utilizing iterative black-box search methods. This iterative nature scales poorly, making multi-corner sweeps computationally expensive. As a solution, this paper proposes amortized neural optimization (ANO) for pre-layout SI design. ANO entirely eliminates iterative black-box inference by utilizing fully differentiable neural network surrogate models. ANO extracts analytical gradients from the surrogate to train a global optimization policy. Instead of solving the optimization problem repeatedly at inference, the optimization process is learned offline and therefore amortized. Once the ANO policy is trained, it maps different channel contexts directly to near-optimal design parameters in a single deterministic forward pass. The efficiency and accuracy of the ANO framework are demonstrated based on three complex SI design scenarios, including DDR5 decision feedback equalization (DFE), 9-dimensional SerDes Tx/Rx co-equalization, and DDR3 DQS differential pair routing to optimize eye diagram metrics under intra-pair skew constraints. By trading roughly 10% in optimality compared to instance-specific black-box algorithms, it realizes speedups of three to four orders of magnitude. For a large-scale 320,000-instance multi-corner SerDes sweep optimization, ANO collapses what would have taken days of computation using iterative search algorithms into a single batched forward pass that completes in milliseconds. This transforms computationally expensive SI optimization into real-time and interactive pre-layout DSE.

中文摘要翻译: 预布局的高速信号完整性（SI）分析设计空间探索（DSE）通常受限于现代电子设计自动化（EDA）工作流中仿真和迭代优化算法的计算成本。虽然机器学习代理模型加速了仿真步骤，但优化设计仍需使用迭代黑箱搜索方法。这种迭代性质扩展性差，使得多角点扫描计算开销巨大。作为解决方案，本文提出了用于预布局SI设计的摊销神经优化（ANO）。ANO通过利用全微分神经网络代理模型，完全消除了迭代黑箱推理。ANO从代理模型中提取解析梯度来训练全局优化策略。推理时不再重复求解优化问题，而是离线学习优化过程，从而实现摊销。一旦ANO策略训练完成，它通过单次确定性前向传播将不同的信道上下文直接映射到接近最优的设计参数。基于三个复杂的SI设计场景验证了ANO框架的效率和准确性，包括DDR5决策反馈均衡（DFE）、9维SerDes Tx/Rx协同均衡，以及在片内偏差约束下优化眼图指标的DDR3 DQS差分对布线。与实例特定的黑箱算法相比，ANO在牺牲约10%最优性的情况下实现了三到四个数量级的加速。对于大规模32万实例多角点SerDes扫描优化，ANO将本需数天计算的迭代搜索算法压缩为毫秒级完成的单次批前向传播。这将计算昂贵的SI优化转变为实时交互式的预布局DSE。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [208] [Which Anatomy Matters Under Limited Labels? A Data-Efficient Anatomy-Aware Benchmark for Cardiac Pathology Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06509)
*Himanshu Singh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 在有限标注和计算资源下，医学影像中表征临床相关解剖结构比增加模型复杂度更能提升性能。通过在ACDC数据集上进行5类心脏病理预测的低数据解剖感知基准实验，发现表征主导复杂性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究在标注稀缺和计算受限的医疗场景中，模型性能的提升究竟主要来自更强大的模型架构，还是来自对临床相关解剖结构的更好表征。

Method: 基于ACDC MRI数据集构建低数据解剖感知基准，从右心室、心肌和左心室提取分割导出的患者描述符，使用线性、核方法和基于树的分类器比较解剖特定表征和多结构表征。

Result: 在有限标签设置下，表征（representation）对性能的主导作用超过模型复杂度（complexity）。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的医疗环境中，识别并表征最具信息量的解剖结构比单纯增加模型复杂度更为重要。

Original Abstract: Numerous medical imaging problems must be solved under limited labels and constrained compute, yet it remains unclear whether performance gains are driven mainly by more expressive models or by better representation of clinically meaningful anatomy. We study this question through a low-data anatomy-aware benchmark for 5-class cardiac pathology prediction on the public ACDC MRI dataset. Using segmentation-derived patient descriptors from the right ventricle, myocardium, and left ventricle, we compare anatomy-specific and multi-structure representations across linear, kernel, and tree-based classifiers. We find that under limited label settings, representation dominates complexity. These results suggest that in resource-constrained healthcare settings, identifying and representing the most informative anatomy may matter more than the increasing complexity of the model alone.

中文摘要翻译: 许多医学影像问题必须在有限标注和受限计算资源下解决，然而目前尚不清楚性能提升主要来自更具表达力的模型，还是来自对临床相关解剖结构的更好表征。我们通过在公开的ACDC MRI数据集上构建一个低数据解剖感知基准，研究这个问题，该基准用于5类心脏病理预测。利用从右心室、心肌和左心室提取的分割导出患者描述符，我们在线性、核函数和基于树的分类器上比较了解剖特定表征和多结构表征。我们发现，在有限标签设置下，表征主导了复杂度。这些结果表明，在资源受限的医疗场景中，识别并表征最具信息量的解剖结构可能比单纯增加模型复杂度更为重要。

</details>


### [209] [Dilated Symmetric Difference for Binary Image Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06512)
*Sharon Urieli*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数学形态学的二值图像比较方法，引入膨胀对称差分算子，可在指定对齐误差范围内有效检测二值图像间的差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的二值图像比较方法在对齐误差存在时鲁棒性不足，需要一种能够容忍一定对齐误差的差异检测算子。

Method: 基于数学形态学框架，定义并引入膨胀对称差分算子（dilated symmetric difference），该算子通过形态学膨胀操作扩展对称差分的检测能力。

Result: 理论分析表明，只要残差对齐误差在指定边界内，膨胀对称差分算子能有效检测出二值图像之间的差异。

Conclusion: 膨胀对称差分为二值图像比较提供了一种鲁棒的形态学方法，在允许的误差范围内具有良好的差异检测性能。

Original Abstract: The comparison of two binary images is formulated in terms of mathematical morphology. A new operator, the dilated symmetric difference, is introduced. It is shown that the dilated symmetric difference effectively detects differences between binary images, provided that the residual alignment error is within specified bounds.

中文摘要翻译: 两个二值图像之间的比较以数学形态学的形式进行表述。引入了一种新的算子——膨胀对称差分。研究表明，只要残差对齐误差在指定范围内，膨胀对称差分能够有效地检测二值图像之间的差异。

</details>


### [210] [Advanced Flood Prediction with Physics-Guided Deep Learning: Combining UNet, FNO, and SAR/Optical Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06524)
*Tewodros Syum Gebre,Jagrati Talreja,Leila Hashemi-Beni*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理引导的深度学习框架，融合多模态遥感和浅水方程约束，用于高精度、物理一致的洪水映射。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 由于地面观测有限、地形条件异质性强，且数据驱动模型难以保证水动力学一致性，准确且可扩展的洪水测绘仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 构建了UNet与傅立叶神经算子(FNO)的混合架构，结合多模态遥感数据，并引入基于物理信息的残差损失以确保质量和动量守恒。

Result: 洪水范围预测IoU为0.82、F1为0.90；水深RMSE为0.21 m，流速RMSE为0.15 m/s；质量不平衡低于2.1%。消融实验证实移除物理正则化会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: 将水动力学原理嵌入深度学习可生成更准确、可靠且物理一致的洪水预测，具备用于业务监测和大规模部署的潜力。

Original Abstract: Accurate and scalable flood mapping remains challenging due to limited ground observations, heterogeneous terrain conditions, and the difficulty of enforcing hydrodynamic consistency within data-driven models. This work introduces a physics-guided deep learning framework that integrates multi-modal remote sensing (Sentinel-1 SAR, Sentinel-2 optical imagery, and DEM-derived terrain features) with constraints from the depth-averaged shallow water equations (SWE). The proposed hybrid architecture combines a UNet to capture fine-scale spatial details with a Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) to model basin-scale hydraulic interactions, while physics-informed residual losses ensure mass and momentum consistency. Evaluated across diverse floodplain settings, the hybrid model achieves an Intersection over Union of 0.82 and an F1 score of 0.90 for flood extent prediction, outperforming UNet-only and FNO-only baselines. Using hydrodynamic simulations as reference data, the model achieves an RMSE of 0.21 m for water depth and 0.15 m/s for flow velocity. Physics consistency is maintained, with low residuals and mass imbalance below 2.1%. Ablation studies confirm that removing physicsbased regularization significantly degrades performance, underscoring the value of physical constraints for stability and generalization. These results demonstrate that embedding hydrodynamic principles into deep learning yields more accurate, reliable, and physically coherent flood predictions, offering strong potential for operational monitoring and large-scale deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 由于地面观测数据有限、地形条件异质性强以及数据驱动模型难以保证水动力学一致性，准确且可扩展的洪水测绘仍然面临挑战。本文提出了一种物理引导的深度学习框架，该框架融合了多模态遥感数据（Sentinel-1 SAR、Sentinel-2光学影像和DEM衍生的地形特征）与基于深度平均浅水方程(SWE)的约束。所提出的混合架构结合了用于捕捉细尺度空间细节的UNet和用于建模流域尺度水力相互作用的傅立叶神经算子(FNO)，同时通过物理信息残差损失确保质量和动量一致性。在不同洪泛区环境下的评估中，该混合模型在洪水范围预测上实现了0.82的交并比和0.90的F1分数，优于仅使用UNet和仅使用FNO的基线模型。以水动力学模拟作为参考数据，该模型在水深上实现了0.21 m的均方根误差，在流速上实现了0.15 m/s的均方根误差。物理一致性得以保持，残差较低且质量不平衡低于2.1%。消融研究证实，移除基于物理的正则化会显著降低性能，突显了物理约束对稳定性和泛化能力的重要性。这些结果表明，将水动力学原理嵌入深度学习能够生成更准确、可靠且物理一致的洪水预测，为业务监测和大规模部署提供了巨大潜力。

</details>


### [211] [Attention Consistent Longitudinal Medical Visual Question Answering Guided by Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06534)
*Jialin Wu,Qianru Zhang,Georges El Fakhri,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 针对纵向医学VQA任务，提出一种注意力引导的编码器-解码器框架，通过轻量级仿射配准模块对齐时序图像、DINO掩码生成器提取显著性区域，并引入掩码重建损失、Gram风格一致性损失和KoLeo均匀性损失辅助训练，在Medical-Diff-VQA基准上取得了优异结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纵向医学VQA需要推理当前时间点图像与参考时间点图像之间的解剖差异，传统直接对比方法受运动伪影等噪声干扰，难以精准捕捉变化信号。因此需要一种能够减少无关运动、增强变化信号并具备可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出注意力引导的编码器-解码器框架：(1) 轻量级仿射配准模块将当前图像与参考图像进行共配准，减少运动干扰；(2) 配准后的图像对经图像编码器编码后，分别通过冻结的DINO掩码生成器和可训练的自适应掩码生成器生成掩码，并应用于原始图像对；(3) 掩码后的图像对再次编码，与文本特征拼接后输入多模态Transformer解码器生成答案；(4) 引入辅助目标函数：掩码重建损失、逐对Gram风格一致性损失和KoLeo均匀性损失，稳定训练并增强表征几何结构。

Result: 在Medical-Diff-VQA基准上，模型在BLEU、ROUGE-L、CIDEr和METEOR指标上表现优异，同时通过共享显著性掩码提供内在可解释性。

Conclusion: 显著性条件生成结合轻量级预对齐为纵向医学VQA提供了一种原则性框架，同时展示了在生物医学中同时优化监督和无监督学习目标的训练范式潜力。

Original Abstract: Longitudinal medical visual question answering (VQA) requires reasoning about anatomical differences between an image of a current time point and an image of a referred time point. We propose an attention-guided encoder-decoder for this task with chest X-rays. Instead of conventional direct contrast, we propose to include a lightweight affine registration module to reduce nuisance motion by co-registering the current image to the reference image with a small registration regularizer. The registered image pair is fed into the image encoder, followed by a frozen DINO-based mask generator and a trainable adaptive mask generator to produce masks applied to the original image pairs. The masked image pairs are again fed into the image encoder and concatenated with text features as the input to a multimodal transformer-based decoder to generate final answers. To facilitate learning stabilization and clarify the change signal, inspired by DINO-v3, we include additional auxiliary objectives, including a mask rebuilding loss, a pairwise Gram-style consistency loss, and a KoLeo uniformity loss, which enhances the geometry of the representation. On the Medical-Diff-VQA benchmark, the model delivers strong BLEU, ROUGE-L, CIDEr, and METEOR scores while offering intrinsic interpretability through the shared saliency mask. These results support saliency-conditioned generation with mild pre-alignment as a principled framework for longitudinal reasoning in medical VQA. Our training strategy also illustrates the potential of a paradigm in utilizing image foundation models in biomedicine: optimizing both supervised and unsupervised learning objectives simultaneously.

中文摘要翻译: 纵向医学视觉问答（VQA）需要对当前时间点图像与参考时间点图像之间的解剖差异进行推理。针对这一任务，我们提出了一种基于胸部X光片的注意力引导编码器-解码器框架。不同于传统的直接对比方法，我们引入了一个轻量级仿射配准模块，通过一个小的配准正则化项将当前图像与参考图像进行共配准，以减少无关运动干扰。配准后的图像对输入图像编码器，随后依次经过一个冻结的DINO掩码生成器和一个可训练的自适应掩码生成器，生成掩码并应用于原始图像对。掩码后的图像对再次输入图像编码器，与文本特征拼接后作为多模态Transformer解码器的输入，生成最终答案。为促进训练稳定并明确变化信号，受DINO-v3启发，我们引入了额外的辅助目标函数，包括掩码重建损失、逐对Gram风格一致性损失和KoLeo均匀性损失，这些损失增强了表征的几何结构。在Medical-Diff-VQA基准上，该模型在BLEU、ROUGE-L、CIDEr和METEOR指标上取得了优异分数，同时通过共享显著性掩码提供了内在可解释性。这些结果支持将显著性条件生成与轻量级预对齐作为纵向医学VQA推理的一种原则性框架。我们的训练策略也展示了在生物医学中同时优化监督和无监督学习目标的范式潜力。

</details>


### [212] [ErA: Error-Aware Deep Unrolling Network for Single Image Defocus Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06540)
*Tu Vo,Chan Y. Park*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: ErA是一种端到端的误差感知深度展开网络，用于单图像散焦去模糊。它联合学习紧凑核基和逐像素权重，通过增广拉格朗日展开中的误差感知项交替更新校正核估计误差，在多个数据集上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单图像散焦去模糊方法在处理核估计误差方面存在不足，导致去模糊效果受限。作者希望引入误差感知机制，通过可学习的展开网络更精确地校正核估计误差，从而提升去模糊质量。

Method: 提出ErA框架，联合学习紧凑核基和逐像素权重；在增广拉格朗日展开中引入误差感知项，通过交替更新机制校正核估计误差；结合ResUNet作为去噪器进行迭代优化。

Result: 在DPDD、RealDOF和RTF数据集上PSNR/SSIM均达到最先进水平；在无真实标签的CUHK数据集上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: ErA通过误差感知的深度展开网络有效解决了单图像散焦去模糊中的核估计误差问题，在多个基准数据集上取得了最优性能，并具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Original Abstract: We introduce ErA (Error-Aware Deep Unrolling Network), an end-to-end frame work for single-image defocus deblurring. ErA jointly learns a compact kerne basis and per-pixel weights, while an error-aware term in Augmented Lagrangian unrolling corrects kernel estimation errors via alternating updates and ResUNet denoisers. It achieves state-of-the-art PSNR/SSIM on DPDD, RealDOF, and RTF, and shows strong generalization on CUHK without ground truth.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了ErA（误差感知深度展开网络），这是一种用于单图像散焦去模糊的端到端框架。ErA联合学习紧凑的核基和逐像素权重，同时增广拉格朗日展开中的误差感知项通过交替更新和ResUNet去噪器校正核估计误差。该方法在DPDD、RealDOF和RTF数据集上实现了最先进的PSNR/SSIM指标，并在无真实标签的CUHK数据集上展现出强泛化能力。

</details>


### [213] [Compute-Optimal Network Design for Echocardiography Myocardial Segmentation and Perfusion Quantification using Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06725)
*Clara Rodrigo González,Matthieu Toulemonde,Lasha Gvinianidze,Cameron A. B. Smith,Oscar Bates,Roxy Senior,Fu Siong Ng,Meng-Xing Tang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经缩放定律应用于少量医学影像数据（超声心肌灌注分割），通过外推子集性能预测全数据集下的最优网络规模，并在CEUS数据集上实现了与资深心脏病专家相当的临床灌注量化性能，同时将参数量降低240倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 超声心肌灌注量化因依赖耗时的手动标注而难以临床推广，自动化分割又受限于域内训练数据匮乏。现有大语言模型的数据驱动优化策略难以直接适配小规模医学影像数据集，因此需要一种能指导小数据场景下计算最优模型设计的方法。

Method: 借鉴神经缩放定律（neural scaling laws），在CAMUS超声心动图数据集和25名患者的CEUS数据集上，通过外推数据子集的性能来预测全数据集下的最优网络规模，选择两个网络进行验证。

Result: 1) 缩放定律外推能准确预测全数据集下的测试损失；2) 选出的两个网络在CAMUS上取得SOTA性能，参数量减少240倍；3) 缩放定律的梯度从CAMUS迁移到CEUS，但预测损失存在偏差；4) 自动分割掩膜在心肌灌注量化上与资深心脏病专家表现相当。

Conclusion: 神经缩放定律可作为小规模医学影像数据集下数据驱动、计算最优模型设计的实用工具。

Original Abstract: Myocardial perfusion quantification using contrast-enhanced ultrasound offers a bedside non-ionizing alternative to nuclear imaging modalities. However, its clinical adoption is hindered by time-consuming manual labelling. Automated segmentation has proved challenging due to a paucity of in-domain training data. Adapting strategies currently used to optimise large language models for large datasets, we apply neural scaling laws to predict network performance for myocardial segmentation. We extrapolate performance on subsets of the data to determine optimal network size on the CAMUS echocardiography dataset and a 25-patient contrast-enhanced ultrasound (CEUS) dataset. Finally, we validate the clinical utility of our models by comparing the final myocardial perfusion parameters with those obtained by a senior cardiologist. Extrapolation based on the scaling law is predictive of test loss at the full dataset size, allowing us to select two networks that obtained state-of-the-art performance on CAMUS with a 240-fold reduction in parameter count. We observe the gradient of the scaling law transfers from CAMUS to the CEUS dataset with a bias in the predicted losses. The automatically segmented masks perform equivalently to a senior cardiologist in myocardial perfusion quantification.
